<path>D:\zuixin\docs\讲解卡.md</path>

<content>
1: # SmartDesk AI 讲解卡
2: 
3: > 用途：面试 / 答辩 / 自己复习时"照着讲"。每个文件一句话，被追问时用"为什么"那段。
4: > 代码规模：`backend/` 约 4200 行 / 36 个 py，`scripts/` 约 1500 行，`frontend/src` 约 1500 行 / 13 个文件。
5: 
6: ---
7: 
8: ## 0. 一句话讲这个项目
9: 
10: 企业内部 IT 服务台的 Agent：员工用自然语言提问或提需求，Agent 要么给**带引用的答案**，要么生成**待确认的工单**，要么停在**人工审批**等有权限的人点头。
11: **动作按危险程度分三级**（只读直通 / 可撤销本人确认 / 不可逆需审批），**审批可以跨进程恢复**。
12: 
13: ---
14: 
15: ## 1. 一张图：9 个节点怎么走
16: 
17: ```
18: START → classify_request → gather_evidence
19:                              ├─(知识型)→ answer_with_citations → compose_reply → END
20:                              └─(动作型)→ plan_action
21:                                           ├─ 缺参数 → ask_missing_info（interrupt，答完回 plan_action）
22:                                           ├─ 可撤销 → confirm_action（interrupt）
23:                                           ├─ 不可逆 → approve_action（interrupt）
24:                                           └─ 只读   → execute_action
25:                                           → execute_action → compose_reply → END
26: ```
27: 
28: - 闲聊：`classify_request` 直接回 `compose_reply`，不检索、不规划。
29: - 分支**全部**写在 `graph.py` 的 5 个 `route_*` 函数里；节点只返回 dict。
30: 
31: ---
32: 
33: ## 2. 每个文件讲什么（按层）
34: 
35: ### 2.1 图与状态（最该先读）
36: 
37: | 文件 | 讲什么 | 一句话 |
38: |---|---|---|
39: | `backend/app/agents/state.py` | `TicketState`（17 个字段）+ frozen `UserContext` | State 是"一张随流程走的记录单"；Context 是"这次调用是谁"（只读、不进 checkpoint） |
40: | `backend/app/agents/graph.py` | 装配 9 个节点 + 5 个路由函数 + `compile_graph` | 图只负责"连边"；决策在 `route_after_classify/gather/plan/confirm/human` |
41: | `backend/app/agents/checkpoint.py` | `PostgresSaver` / `SqliteSaver` 二选一 | 每走完一个节点写一次档，进程杀掉换个进程还能接着跑 |
42: | `backend/app/agents/settings.py` | `.env` → 配置对象 | 模型、Milvus、PG、租户默认值都在这里 |
43: 
44: ### 2.2 节点（9 个，加上 1 张表文件）
45: 
46: | 文件 | 讲什么 | 一句话 |
47: |---|---|---|
48: | `nodes/classify.py` | 结构化输出 `Classification` | 一次模型调用只判三件代码推不出来的事：问知识 / 要办事 / 闲聊 + 动作类型 + 指代是哪一张；**风险与 server 不在这里**（风险按表算、server 由 op 推导） |
49: | `nodes/gather_evidence.py` | 取两袋证据 | 制度走 Milvus 检索，事实走只读 MCP 工具；两袋都塞进 State 再决定怎么说 |
50: | `nodes/answer.py` | 带引用作答 | 只准用检索到的制度回答，每条标 `[1][2]`；检索不到就认怂并给三个下一步 |
51: | `nodes/action_spec.py` | 三张表 | `RISK_BY_OP` / `compute_risk` / `GATE_BY_RISK` / `FIELD_LABELS`——风险与闸门全是数据，不是 if |
52: | `nodes/plan_action.py` | 动作规划 | 抽参数 + 标来源（from_user / from_policy / agent_inferred）+ 算风险 + 选闸门 + 渲染确认卡片 |
53: | `nodes/human_gate.py` | 三道闸门合一 | 澄清 / 确认 / 审批三种 interrupt；审批"发起≠批准"、恢复时二次鉴权、留痕写 `approvals` |
54: | `nodes/execute.py` | 唯一能写库的节点 | 走 MCP 调工具；不可逆动作没有 `approval.approver`（没人批过）直接拒绝 |
55: | `nodes/respond.py` | 拼人话 | 把结构化字段拼成员工能看懂的一段话（模板，不调模型） |
56: | `nodes/clarify.py` | 缺参数反问 | 少什么问什么；有多个候选就让用户挑 |
57: 
58: ### 2.3 RAG（知识库）
59: 
60: | 文件 | 讲什么 | 一句话 |
61: |---|---|---|
62: | `rag/vector_store.py` | Milvus 读写 | 稠密 1024 + 内置 BM25 双路召回，服务端 `RRFRanker(k=60)` 融合取 Top3 |
63: | `rag/embedding.py` | 文本转向量 | 用 LangChain 现成 `OpenAIEmbeddings`（DashScope 兼容端点，单批 ≤10 条） |
64: | `rag/pdf_parser.py` | PDF → Markdown | 调 MinerU 官方 SDK（flash 免 token），输出带层级的 Markdown |
65: | `rag/splitter.py` | 结构感知切分 | 先按 `#/##/###` 切，超长块再递归切；每块前面拼【文档 · 章 · 节】面包屑 |
66: | `knowledge.py` | 门面 | `search` / `ingest` / `publish_article` / `health`——节点只跟这个文件打交道 |
67: 
68: ### 2.4 工具（MCP）
69: 
70: | 文件 | 讲什么 | 一句话 |
71: |---|---|---|
72: | `mcp/servers/ticket_server.py` | 工单 server（5 工具 + 审批留痕 2 个） | 8 个 `@ticket_mcp.tool()`：建/改/关/查/列表 + 存审批/查审批 |
73: | `mcp/servers/kb_server.py` | 知识 server（2 工具） | `search_kb` 给外部客户端复用；`publish_kb_article` 图内可达 |
74: | `mcp/servers/guard.py` | 工具层鉴权 | 3 个角色（employee / approver / kb_owner），跨租户、越权一律 `forbidden` |
75: | `tools/mcp_client.py` | MCP 客户端 | `MultiServerMCPClient` 起 stdio 子进程；沙箱禁子进程时自动降级进程内调用 |
76: | `tools/ticket_tools.py` / `kb_tools.py` | 工具封装 | 节点不直接拼参数，走这里统一传 `actor` |
77: 
78: ### 2.5 HTTP / 流式
79: 
80: | 文件 | 讲什么 | 一句话 |
81: |---|---|---|
82: | `api/main.py` | FastAPI 入口 | `uvicorn backend.app.api.main:app --port 8010`；`/api/v1` 前缀 + `/health` |
83: | `api/routes.py` | 6 个接口 | `/chat/stream`、`/runs/{id}/resume`、`/runs/{id}`、`/graph/mermaid`、`/tickets`、`/approvals` |
84: | `api/streaming.py` | 把图事件变 SSE | 6 种事件：`meta / node / token / interrupt / done / error`；token 只转发 answer 节点 |
85: | `api/deps.py` | 身份依赖 | 三个请求头 `X-User-Id / X-Tenant-Id / X-Roles` → `UserContext`；换 JWT 只改这里 |
86: | `api/schemas.py` | 请求/响应模型 | `ChatRequest` / `ResumeRequest`（把三种闸门的回答统一成一种 payload） |
87: 
88: ### 2.6 脚本与前端
89: 
90: | 文件 | 讲什么 |
91: |---|---|
92: | `scripts/run_graph.py` | CLI 入口：`run / resume / state / graph / tools / chat / approvals` |
93: | `scripts/ingest_docs.py` | 入库：`--md docs/kb/md --to-milvus --force` |
94: | `scripts/eval_rag.py` | 评测：51 条 golden set，出 Recall@1/3、MRR、引用准确率、延迟 |
95: | `scripts/trace_run.py` | 逐节点跟读，教学用 |
96: | `frontend/src/views/ChatView.vue` | 会话页：SSE 渲染 + 闸门卡片 + 右侧状态面板 |
97: | `frontend/src/views/ApprovalView.vue` | 审批中心：`approvals` 表分页/筛选 |
98: | `frontend/src/views/TicketsView.vue` | 工单看板 |
99: | `frontend/src/api/sse.ts` | 解析 `event:/data:` 帧；`chatStream` / `resumeStream` |
100: 
101: ---
102: 
103: ## 3. 八个"为什么"（面试主战场）
104: 
105: 1. **为什么用 LangGraph 手写，不用 `create_agent`？**
106:    因为我要的是"**停下来等人**"：审批要跨进程、要能按新参数重算风险、要留痕。高层封装把工具调用交给模型自由决定，审批与审计就不可控了。
107: 
108: 2. **为什么 9 个节点这样切？**
109:    沿一条请求的真实阶段切：理解 → 取证 → 分叉（回答/规划）→ 三个闸门 → 执行 → 拼话。每个节点只干一件事，出问题能定位到节点。
110: 
111: 3. **为什么"制度和事实"要分两袋？**
112:    制度回答"规定是什么"（要引用），事实回答"这张单现在什么状态"（不要引用、不要编号）。混在一起会让模型把工具返回当制度引用，也会答非所问。
113: 
114: 4. **为什么风险必须由表决定，不能让模型判？**
115:    模型可以被话术骗。风险只能是 `RISK_BY_OP` + `compute_risk`（参数升级：关单 + 通知 = 不可逆），这样"危险等级"可测、可审计、可讲。
116: 
117: 5. **为什么"发起 ≠ 批准"？**
118:    员工该能提需求，但不能自己批自己。所以谁能发起是权限，能不能点通过看**恢复时**拿同一 `thread_id` 的那个人；有审批权的人自己批自己要标 `self_approved`。
119: 
120: 6. **为什么确认卡改完参数还要重算风险？**
121:    原本"关单"是可撤销，但勾了"通知提单人"就变成对外可见、不可撤回——不重算就会漏掉一道审批。所以 `confirm_action` 通过后重新 `compute_risk`，该升级就升级。
122: 
123: 7. **为什么只有 `execute_action` 能写库？**
124:    写操作集中在一个节点，才能保证"进到这里之前该批的都批了"，再补一道 `approval.approver` 兜底。别的节点只读。
125: 
126: 8. **为什么检索用 RRF 而不是加权分数？**
127:    稠密分数（余弦）和 BM25 分数不在一个量纲，归一化很脆；RRF 只用排名，稳、不用调参，取 `k=60` 让前几名权重接近、避免单路垄断。
128: 
129: ---
130: 
131: ## 4. 一条请求怎么走（演示脚本）
132: 
133: ```bash
134: # 1) 知识型：带引用回答
135: .venv\Scripts\python.exe -m scripts.run_graph run --thread t1 -m "年假有多少天？"
136: 
137: # 2) 动作型：抽出动作参数（action.args），停在确认门
138: .venv\Scripts\python.exe -m scripts.run_graph run --thread t2 -m "帮我建个工单：VPN 连不上，影响 2 个人"
139: .venv\Scripts\python.exe -m scripts.run_graph resume --thread t2 --approve
140: 
141: # 3) 审批门：员工发起、主管跨进程批准
142: .venv\Scripts\python.exe -m scripts.run_graph run --thread t3 --role employee -m "把 TK-... 关掉，顺便通知提单人"
143: .venv\Scripts\python.exe -m scripts.run_graph resume --thread t3 --role approver --user u-9001 --approve
144: 
145: # 4) 看审批表
146: .venv\Scripts\python.exe -m scripts.run_graph approvals --role approver
147: ```
148: 
149: Web：`uvicorn backend.app.api.main:app --port 8010` + `cd frontend && npm run dev`（浏览器开 `http://localhost:5173`）。
150: 
151: ---
152: 
153: ## 5. 三个"坑"（面试加分项）
154: 
155: 1. **恢复时节点会重跑**：`interrupt` 恢复不是"从下一行继续"，而是重放该节点。所以写审批留痕要幂等（`save_approval` 先查 pending）。
156: 2. **MCP 返回形状会变**：新版 adapters 返回 `[{"type":"text","text":...}]` 而不是 `ToolMessage`，所以要 `_text_of()` 兼容三种形状，否则建单报"无法解析的工具返回"。
157: 3. **Word 表格边框不可靠**：做简历/文档时 `tblBorders=none` 在 LibreOffice 里仍会画线——与项目无关，但说明"别相信渲染一致性"。
158: 
159: ---
160: 
161: ## 6. 边界（不知道就说不了解）
162: 
163: - 没做**多 Agent**：单链路业务，拆多 Agent 只多同步成本。
164: - 没做**精排**：两条检索未命中要靠 cross-encoder（gte-rerank-v2）才能补。
165: - **审批权限**目前是请求头身份 + 工具层校验，没有登录/JWT。
166: - 知识库是**演示语料**（7 份虚构制度），入库链路是真的。

(End of file - total 166 lines)
</content>

<content>
1: # 检索与引用评测报告
2: 
3: - 用例：`docs/eval/golden_set.jsonl`，共 **51** 条（标注到「文档 + 小节」，例如 `01-员工手册.md · 3.1 年假`）
4: - 检索：Milvus 服务端混合检索（dense + 内置 BM25 + RRFRanker），top_k=3
5: - 环境：向量库 backend=milvus · collection=smartdesk_kb · chunks=97（Milvus 统计值显示 98，是 1 条已删旧行的滞后统计，按 tenant 查询实为 97）
6: 
7: ## 1. 检索指标（51 条）
8: 
9: | 指标 | 数值 | 含义 |
10: |---|---|---|
11: | Recall@1 | **82.35%** | 期望小节排第一的比例 |
12: | Recall@3 | **96.08%** | 期望小节进前 3 的比例 |
13: | MRR@3 | **0.8922** | 期望小节排名倒数的平均值（越接近 1 越好） |
14: | 首条关键词命中率 | 94.12% | 首条检索结果里出现期望关键词 |
15: | 检索延迟 | 平均 253.6 ms / P95 361.2 ms | 单次 hybrid_search |
16: 
17: ## 2. 端到端回答（前 20 条，真模型）
18: 
19: | 指标 | 数值 | 含义 |
20: |---|---|---|
21: | 引用准确率 | **100.00%** | 引用的资料里含期望小节 |
22: | 引用标记率 | 100.00% | 回答正文带 `[1]` 这类标注 |
23: | 关键词落进回答 | 90.00% | 回答正文出现期望关键词 |
24: | 端到端延迟 | 平均 3.14 s / P95 3.82 s | 分类 + 检索 + 生成 |
25: | 失败条数 | 0 | 抛异常的用例 |
26: 
27: ## 3. 端到端逐条
28:

<path>D:\zuixin\backend\app\agents\graph.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """主图：9 个节点，四条路。
2: 
3: 一句话读法（每个节点名都是动词短语）：
4:     classify_request → gather_evidence → answer_with_citations → compose_reply      （知识型）
5:                                       ↘ plan_action → confirm_action / approve_action / ask_missing_info
6:                                                       → execute_action → compose_reply
7: 
8: 两处容易讲错的地方（面试常问）：
9: - confirm_action 不是"确认完就执行"：员工可能在卡片上改出**不可逆**参数（关单加"通知提单人"），
10:   所以确认后还要按新参数重算风险，需要时补一道 approve_action（route_after_confirm）。
11: - approve_action 里"发起"和"批准"是两件事：员工发起不可逆动作只会进 pending 队列，
12:   能不能点通过看**恢复那一刻的身份**（主管拿同一个 thread_id resume 就能批）。
13: 
14: 路由统一写在本文件：每个条件边配一个**纯函数 router**，节点只负责"算 + 返回字段"。
15: 这对应黑马《第2节 常见 WorkFlow》的 Routing：看 State 决定去哪，而不是节点内部 goto。
16: 唯一会让图"停下来"的是三个闸门里的 `interrupt()`（ask_missing_info / confirm_action / approve_action）。
17: """
18: 
19: from __future__ import annotations
20: 
21: from typing import Literal
22: 
23: from langgraph.graph import END, START, StateGraph
24: 
25: from .checkpoint import get_checkpointer
26: from .nodes.answer import answer_with_citations
27: from .nodes.classify import classify_request
28: from .nodes.clarify import ask_missing_info
29: from .nodes.execute import execute_action
30: from .nodes.gather_evidence import gather_evidence
31: from .nodes.human_gate import approve_action, confirm_action
32: from .nodes.plan_action import plan_action
33: from .nodes.respond import compose_reply
34: from .state import TicketState, UserContext
35: 
36: NodeName = Literal[
37:     "classify_request",
38:     "gather_evidence",
39:     "answer_with_citations",
40:     "plan_action",
41:     "ask_missing_info",
42:     "confirm_action",
43:     "approve_action",
44:     "execute_action",
45:     "compose_reply",
46: ]
47: 
48: # gate → 节点名：plan_action 只写 gate（clarify/confirm/approve/execute），不决定去哪
49: GATE_TO_NODE: dict[str, str] = {
50:     "clarify": "ask_missing_info",
51:     "confirm": "confirm_action",
52:     "approve": "approve_action",
53:     "execute": "execute_action",
54: }
55: 
56: 
57: # 路由函数一律"直接返回节点名"：不返回 key，也不把 outcome 值当路由值用。
58: # outcome 只配当判断条件（有值 = 没办成 = 去回复），它本身不是路由。
59: def route_after_classify(state: TicketState) -> NodeName:
60:     """闲聊不查资料；其余都要先取证据。"""
61:     return "compose_reply" if state.get("intent") == "chat" else "gather_evidence"
62: 
63: 
64: def route_after_gather(state: TicketState) -> NodeName:
65:     """要动手（动作型）才进 plan_action；问知识直接答。"""
66:     return "plan_action" if state.get("intent") == "action" else "answer_with_citations"
67: 
68: 
69: def route_after_plan(state: TicketState) -> NodeName:
70:     """没办成（denied / rejected / failed）→ 回复；否则按 gate 进对应的门。"""
71:     if state.get("outcome"):
72:         return "compose_reply"
73:     gate = state.get("gate") or "execute"
74:     return GATE_TO_NODE.get(gate, "execute_action")  # type: ignore[return-value]
75: 
76: 
77: def route_after_confirm(state: TicketState) -> NodeName:
78:     """被拒/没资格 → 回复；改出不可逆参数 → 补一道审批；否则直接执行。"""
79:     if state.get("outcome"):
80:         return "compose_reply"
81:     return "approve_action" if state.get("gate") == "approve" else "execute_action"
82: 
83: 
84: def route_after_human(state: TicketState) -> NodeName:
85:     """没人批 / 执行前就失败 → 回复；批过了 → 执行。"""
86:     return "compose_reply" if state.get("outcome") else "execute_action"
87: 
88: 
89: 
90: def compile_graph(*, checkpointer=None):
91:     """装配并编译主图（`checkpointer=None` 时用全局默认：PG 优先、失败退 SQLite）。"""
92:     builder = StateGraph(TicketState, context_schema=UserContext)
93: 
94:     builder.add_node("classify_request", classify_request)
95:     builder.add_node("gather_evidence", gather_evidence)
96:     builder.add_node("answer_with_citations", answer_with_citations)
97:     builder.add_node("plan_action", plan_action)
98:     builder.add_node("ask_missing_info", ask_missing_info)
99:     builder.add_node("confirm_action", confirm_action)
100:     builder.add_node("approve_action", approve_action)
101:     builder.add_node("execute_action", execute_action)
102:     builder.add_node("compose_reply", compose_reply)
103: 
104:     builder.add_edge(START, "classify_request")
105:     builder.add_conditional_edges("classify_request", route_after_classify)
106:     builder.add_conditional_edges("gather_evidence", route_after_gather)
107:     builder.add_edge("answer_with_citations", "compose_reply")
108:     builder.add_conditional_edges("plan_action", route_after_plan)
109:     builder.add_edge("ask_missing_info", "plan_action")  # 补完参数回去重算计划
110:     builder.add_conditional_edges("confirm_action", route_after_confirm)
111:     builder.add_conditional_edges("approve_action", route_after_human)
112:     builder.add_edge("execute_action", "compose_reply")
113:     builder.add_edge("compose_reply", END)
114: 
115:     saver = checkpointer if checkpointer is not None else get_checkpointer()
116:     return builder.compile(checkpointer=saver)
117: 
118: 
119: _GRAPH = None
120: 
121: 
122: def get_graph():
123:     """进程内单例（编译一次，之后复用；checkpointer 自己带连接）。"""
124:     global _GRAPH
125:     if _GRAPH is None:
126:         _GRAPH = compile_graph()
127:     return _GRAPH

(End of file - total 127 lines)
</content>

<path>D:\zuixin\backend\app\agents\state.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """主图状态（State）与调用上下文（Context）。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义：
4: - 《第1节 基本概念和入门》：State 用 TypedDict；AgentState 已内置 messages + add_messages reducer
5: - 《第1节 Runtime》：Context（每次调用只读：谁 / 哪个租户 / 什么角色）与 State（图内可变）分离
6: - 《第3节 Think In LangGraph》：State 只保留下游决策需要、且推不出来的字段
7: 
8: 本项目把"一次请求"拆成三件事，State 也按这三件事分组：
9: 1. 理解：intent / action / risk
10: 2. 证据：policy_evidence（制度、手册召回）+ facts（对象事实，如工单详情）
11: 3. 动作：action（参数唯一真相） -> gate（走哪个闸门） -> approval（谁批的） -> result
12: """
13: 
14: from __future__ import annotations
15: 
16: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
17: from typing import Any, Literal
18: 
19: from langchain.agents import AgentState
20: 
21: # 三种意图：知识问答 / 动作请求 / 闲聊
22: Intent = Literal["knowledge", "action", "chat"]
23: # 三个闸门 + 直通
24: Gate = Literal["clarify", "confirm", "approve", "execute"]
25: # 风险等级：只读 / 可撤销写 / 不可逆或全员可见
26: Risk = Literal["read", "write_reversible", "write_irreversible"]
27: 
28: 
29: @dataclass(frozen=True)
30: class UserContext:
31:     """调用上下文：一次 invoke 内只读，不写进 checkpoint。"""
32: 
33:     user_id: str
34:     tenant_id: str
35:     # employee=普通员工；approver=主管；kb_owner=知识库 owner
36:     roles: tuple[str, ...] = field(default_factory=lambda: ("employee",))
37:     display_name: str = "同事"
38: 
39:     @property
40:     def can_publish_kb(self) -> bool:
41:         return bool({"kb_owner"} & set(self.roles))
42: 
43: 
44: class TicketState(AgentState):
45:     """主图状态。AgentState 已包含 messages / jump_to / structured_response。"""
46: 
47:     # ---------- 身份与输入 ----------
48:     tenant_id: str
49:     user_id: str
50:     query: str
51: 
52:     # ---------- 第 1 步：理解 ----------
53:     intent: str
54:     # 动作（唯一真相）：{"server","op","label","args","missing","sources"}
55:     # args 是"定稿参数"：确认卡上改完的值直接写回这里，执行节点读它
56:     action: dict[str, Any] | None
57:     risk: str
58:     # 分类的补充信息：{"topic":..., "summary":..., "urgency":...}
59:     classification: dict[str, Any] | None
60: 
61:     # ---------- 第 2 步：证据（两个字段各写各的 key，没有并行分支，不需要 reducer） ----------
62:     policy_evidence: list[dict[str, Any]]
63:     facts: list[dict[str, Any]]
64: 
65:     # ---------- 第 3 步：动作 ----------
66:     gate: str
67:     approval: dict[str, Any] | None
68:     result: dict[str, Any] | None
69: 
70:     # ---------- 输出 ----------
71:     via: str | None
72:     reply: str | None
73:     # 只有"没办成"才写：rejected（人拒绝或没资格）/ failed（执行失败）；正常跑完是 None
74:     outcome: str | None
75: 
76: 
77: def actor_of(runtime: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
78:     """把 Runtime Context 变成给 MCP server 的身份票据（server 侧鉴权的唯一可信来源）。
79: 
80:     为什么不能从 State / 模型输出取：那是"模型说的"，可能填错也可能被提示词攻击改写。
81:     """
82:     context = getattr(runtime, "context", None)
83:     if context is None:
84:         raise RuntimeError("缺少 UserContext：调用 invoke 时必须传 context=UserContext(...)")
85:     return {
86:         "user_id": context.user_id,
87:         "tenant_id": context.tenant_id,
88:         "roles": list(context.roles),
89:     }
90: 
91: 
92: def initial_state(
93:     *,
94:     tenant_id: str,
95:     user_id: str = "u-1001",
96:     query: str,
97:     messages: list[Any] | None = None,
98: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
99:     """构造一次 invoke 的初始输入（只传增量字段，其余由 LangGraph 补默认值）。"""
100:     return {
101:         "tenant_id": tenant_id,
102:         "user_id": user_id,
103:         "query": query,
104:         "intent": "",
105:         "action": None,
106:         "risk": "read",
107:         "classification": None,
108:         "policy_evidence": [],
109:         "facts": [],
110:         "gate": "execute",
111:         "approval": None,
112:         "result": None,
113:         "via": None,
114:         "reply": None,
115:         "outcome": None,
116:         "messages": messages or [],
117:     }

1: """动作的"规格表"：风险等级、风险到闸门的映射、字段中文名。
2: 
3: 这个文件只放表和"读表的函数"，不放流程。想改 Agent 的行为（比如"关单+通知要审批"），
4: 先来这里改表，通常不用碰节点逻辑——这也是面试里"你的策略写在哪"的答案。
5: """
6: 
7: from __future__ import annotations
8: 
9: from typing import Any
10: 
11: # 每个工具"默认"多危险（read 直通 / write_reversible 确认 / write_irreversible 审批）
12: RISK_BY_OP: dict[str, str] = {
13:     "query_ticket": "read",
14:     "list_tickets": "read",
15:     "search_kb": "read",
16:     "create_ticket": "write_reversible",
17:     "update_ticket": "write_reversible",
18:     "close_ticket": "write_reversible",
19:     "publish_kb_article": "write_irreversible",
20: }
21: 
22: 
23: def compute_risk(op: str, args: dict[str, Any], base: str) -> str:
24:     """风险不只由"调哪个工具"决定，还要看"带什么参数"（本项目拓展）。
25: 
26:     - close_ticket 默认可撤销（能重开）；一旦 notify_reporter=True 就会对外发消息，
27:       撤不回来 → 升级为 write_irreversible，必须走审批。
28:     - publish_kb_article 发布出去全员可见 → 审批。
29:     """
30:     if op == "close_ticket" and bool(args.get("notify_reporter")):
31:         return "write_irreversible"
32:     if op == "publish_kb_article":
33:         return "write_irreversible"
34:     return base
35: 
36: 
37: # 风险 → 第几道闸门（read 直通执行，不需要人）
38: GATE_BY_RISK: dict[str, str] = {
39:     "read": "execute",
40:     "write_reversible": "confirm",
41:     "write_irreversible": "approve",
42: }
43: 
44: # 卡片上给人看的字段中文名（模型填的是英文 key）
45: FIELD_LABELS: dict[str, str] = {
46:     "title": "标题",
47:     "description": "描述",
48:     "urgency": "优先级",
49:     "category": "分类",
50:     "reporter": "提单人",
51:     "ticket_no": "工单号",
52:     "patch": "要改的字段",
53:     "resolution": "解决说明",
54:     "notify_reporter": "通知提单人",
55:     "content": "正文",
56:     "tags": "标签",
57:     "change_note": "变更说明",
58:     "status": "状态",
59:     "limit": "条数",
60: }
61: 
62: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
63: # 视图：卡片上的字段 / 标题都从 action 现算，不再另存一份"草稿"
64: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
65: EDITABLE_BLOCK = {"reporter", "tenant_id"}  # 身份字段不许在卡片上改
66: 
67: 
68: def label_of(action: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
69:     """动作中文名，例如「关工单」。"""
70:     return str((action or {}).get("label") or "操作")
71: 
72: 
73: def summary_of(action: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
74:     """确认卡/审批卡的一句话摘要，避免出现「建工单：建工单：…」。"""
75:     args = (action or {}).get("args") or {}
76:     label = label_of(action)
77:     head = str(args.get("title") or args.get("ticket_no") or "").strip()
78:     for prefix in (f"{label}：", f"{label}:", label):
79:         if head.startswith(prefix):
80:             head = head[len(prefix):].strip()
81:             break
82:     return f"{label}：{head}" if head else label
83: 
84: 
85: def fields_of(action: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
86:     """把 args 变成前端能直接渲染的卡片字段（值 + 中文标签 + 来源 + 能否编辑）。"""
87:     args = (action or {}).get("args") or {}
88:     sources = (action or {}).get("sources") or {}
89:     return [
90:         {
91:             "name": name,
92:             "label": FIELD_LABELS.get(name, name),
93:             "value": value,
94:             "source": sources.get(name, "agent_inferred"),
95:             "editable": name not in EDITABLE_BLOCK,
96:         }
97:         for name, value in args.items()
98:     ]

(End of file - total 98 lines)

<content>
1: """三个闸门（clarify / confirm / approve）共用的解析、留痕，以及两个"停人"的闸门。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义：
4: - 《第1节 基本概念和入门》Interrupts：停下来等人，靠 checkpoint 记住停在哪
5: - 《第2节 Middleware》HumanInTheLoop：业务级中断手写 interrupt（要把参数、来源、引用给出来）
6: - 《第1节 Runtime》：权限来自 Context（UserContext.roles），不是模型自己判断
7: 
8: 两个闸门的分工（面试最容易被追问的一点）：
9: - `confirm_action`（确认门）：**可撤销**动作，由提单人本人看一眼参数 → 可改 → 确认
10: - `approve_action`（审批门）：**不可逆 / 全员可见**动作，由有权限的人点通过 → 撤不回
11: 
12: 三条必须记住的语义：
13: 1. **发起 ≠ 批准**：任何员工都能发起不可逆动作（只进 pending 队列），能不能点通过只看
14:    恢复那一刻的身份——没权限就继续挂起，绝不替别人点。
15: 2. **确认后要重算风险**：员工可能在确认卡上改出不可逆参数（关单时勾"顺便通知提单人"），
16:    那就不能直接执行，要补一道审批（风险表仍是唯一来源：RISK_BY_OP）。
17: 3. **恢复会重放节点**：interrupt 恢复时本节点从头再跑一遍，所以留痕必须幂等
18:    （`record_approval` → server 侧 `save_approval` 对同一 pending 直接复用）。
19: 
20: resume 的载荷允许三种写法，这里统一归一化成 {"type", "patch", "message", "answers"}：
21: 1. {"type": "decision", "decision": {"type": "approve"}}
22: 2. {"decision": "approve"}
23: 3. "approve"
24: """
25: 
26: from __future__ import annotations
27: 
28: import logging
29: from typing import Any
30: 
31: from langgraph.runtime import Runtime
32: from langgraph.types import interrupt
33: 
34: from ..state import TicketState, UserContext, actor_of
35: from ..tools import ticket_tools
36: from .action_spec import fields_of, label_of, summary_of
37: from .plan_action import GATE_BY_RISK, RISK_BY_OP, compute_risk
38: 
39: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
40: 
41: VALID_DECISIONS = ("approve", "edit", "reject")
42: 
43: # 闸门结果 → 审批表状态（approved / edited / rejected）
44: DECISION_STATUS = {"approve": "approved", "edit": "edited", "reject": "rejected"}
45: 
46: # 谁能批：审批权来自 Context.roles（不是模型说了算）
47: APPROVER_ROLES: dict[str, tuple[tuple[str, ...], str]] = {
48:     "publish_kb_article": (("kb_owner",), "知识库 owner "),
49:     "close_ticket": (("approver",), "主管"),
50: }
51: DEFAULT_APPROVER = (("approver", "kb_owner"), "有权限的同事 ")
52: 
53: 
54: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
55: # 一、resume 载荷解析与参数合并（两个闸门共用）
56: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
57: def normalize_human_input(answer: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
58:     """把 interrupt 恢复值整理成 {"type":..., "patch":..., "message":..., "answers":...}。"""
59:     if answer is None:
60:         return {}
61:     if isinstance(answer, str):
62:         return {"type": answer}
63:     if not isinstance(answer, dict):
64:         return {}
65: 
66:     if "answers" in answer and isinstance(answer["answers"], dict):
67:         return {"type": "answers", "answers": answer["answers"]}
68: 
69:     if "decision" in answer:
70:         decision = answer["decision"]
71:         if isinstance(decision, str):
72:             return {"type": decision}
73:         if isinstance(decision, dict):
74:             return {
75:                 "type": decision.get("type") or decision.get("action") or "approve",
76:                 "patch": decision.get("patch") or {},
77:                 "message": decision.get("message") or "",
78:             }
79: 
80:     if answer.get("type") in VALID_DECISIONS:
81:         return {
82:             "type": answer.get("type"),
83:             "patch": answer.get("patch") or {},
84:             "message": answer.get("message") or "",
85:         }
86: 
87:     return {}
88: 
89: 
90: def apply_patch(args: dict[str, Any], patch: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
91:     """把员工/审批人改过的字段合并进参数（一层字典合并即可）。"""
92:     merged = dict(args or {})
93:     for key, value in (patch or {}).items():
94:         merged[key] = value
95:     return merged
96: 
97: 
98: def patch_action(action: dict[str, Any], patch: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
99:     """人在卡片上改的参数，直接写回 action.args（全项目只有这一份参数，不另存草稿）。"""
100:     if not patch:
101:         return dict(action or {})
102:     args = apply_patch(action.get("args") or {}, patch)
103:     sources = dict(action.get("sources") or {})
104:     for key in patch:
105:         sources[key] = "from_user"  # 人改过的值，来源就是人
106:     return {**action, "args": args, "sources": sources}
107: 
108: 
109: def _citations(state: TicketState) -> list[str]:
110:     """卡片上展示的制度依据（引用串已在证据层拼好）。"""
111:     return [str(item.get("citation") or "") for item in (state.get("policy_evidence") or []) if item]
112: 
113: 
114: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
115: # 二、审批留痕（三处闸门共用的"业务事实"落库）
116: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
117: def approval_target(action: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
118:     """这次审批针对什么：优先工单号，其次标题，最后用摘要兜底。"""
119:     args = (action or {}).get("args") or {}
120:     return str(args.get("ticket_no") or args.get("title") or summary_of(action))
121: 
122: 
123: def record_approval(
124:     actor: dict[str, Any],
125:     action: dict[str, Any],
126:     status: str,
127:     *,
128:     risk: str,
129:     note: str = "",
130:     approver: str = "",
131: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
132:     """把这次闸门的留痕写到工单 server（pending / approved / edited / rejected）。
133: 
134:     返回审批行本身（含 requester / id）；写失败返回空 dict。
135:     为什么要返回值：节点被 interrupt 恢复时会**重放**，此时 runtime.context 已经是恢复人，
136:     发起人只能从服务端那行 pending 里读回来（否则会把审批人当成发起人）。
137:     """
138:     try:
139:         data = ticket_tools.save_approval(
140:             actor,
141:             op=str((action or {}).get("op") or "unknown"),
142:             target=approval_target(action),
143:             risk=risk,
144:             status=status,
145:             summary=summary_of(action),
146:             note=note,
147:             approver=approver,
148:         )
149:     except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - 留痕失败不阻断业务
150:         logger.warning("审批留痕写入失败（不影响主流程）", exc_info=True)
151:         return {}
152:     approval = (data or {}).get("approval") or {}
153:     return approval if isinstance(approval, dict) else {}
154: 
155: 
156: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
157: # 三、确认门：可撤销动作，提单人本人点一下
158: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
159: def confirm_action(state: TicketState, runtime: Runtime[UserContext]) -> dict[str, Any]:
160:     """可撤销的动作，让提单人本人看一眼参数再点确认。"""
161:     action = state.get("action") or {}
162:     risk = str(state.get("risk") or "read")
163:     context = runtime.context
164:     actor = actor_of(runtime)
165: 
166:     pending = record_approval(actor, action, "pending", risk=risk)  # 幂等：重放不会重复插
167:     requested_by = str(pending.get("requester") or context.user_id)
168: 
169:     payload = {
170:         "type": "confirm",
171:         "title": summary_of(action),
172:         "fields": fields_of(action),
173:         "citations": _citations(state),
174:         "risk": risk,
175:         "hint": "确认后才会真正写入系统；发现填错了可以直接改字段。",
176:     }
177:     answer = interrupt(payload)
178: 
179:     # 这一步只等提单人本人；别人 resume 进来直接回一句结束，不替他确认、也不挂起
180:     if runtime.context.user_id != requested_by:
181:         return {
182:             "outcome": "denied",
183:             "reply": (
184:                 f"这一步只等提单人本人（{requested_by}）确认，当前身份不能替他确认。"
185:                 "需要继续的话，让本人拿同一条 thread resume。"
186:             ),
187:         }
188: 
189:     parsed = normalize_human_input(answer)
190:     kind = parsed.get("type") or "approve"
191:     record_approval(
192:         actor,
193:         action,
194:         DECISION_STATUS.get(kind, "rejected"),
195:         risk=risk,
196:         note=parsed.get("message") or "",
197:         approver=runtime.context.user_id,
198:     )
199: 
200:     if kind == "reject":
201:         message = parsed.get("message") or "员工取消了这次操作"
202:         return {
203:             "outcome": "rejected",
204:             "reply": f"{label_of(action)}没有执行：{message}。需要的话换个说法再来一次。",
205:         }
206: 
207:     patch = parsed.get("patch") or {}
208:     action_patched = patch_action(action, patch)
209:     op = str(action_patched.get("op") or "")
210:     # 关键：按"改完之后的参数"重算风险，勾出不可逆参数就升级到审批门
211:     new_risk = compute_risk(op, action_patched.get("args") or {}, RISK_BY_OP.get(op, "write_reversible"))
212:     gate = GATE_BY_RISK.get(new_risk, "confirm")
213:     return {"action": action_patched, "risk": new_risk, "gate": gate}
214: 
215: 
216: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
217: # 四、审批门：不可逆 / 全员可见动作，有权限的人点头
218: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
219: def resolve_approver(op: str, roles: tuple[str, ...]) -> tuple[bool, list[str], str]:
220:     allowed_roles, label = APPROVER_ROLES.get(op, DEFAULT_APPROVER)
221:     ok = any(role in allowed_roles for role in roles)
222:     return ok, list(allowed_roles), label
223: 
224: 
225: def approve_action(state: TicketState, runtime: Runtime[UserContext]) -> dict[str, Any]:
226:     """审批闸门：发起人一律进 pending 队列；恢复时按恢复人的身份决定能不能点。"""
227:     context = runtime.context
228:     action = state.get("action") or {}
229:     actor = actor_of(runtime)
230:     op = str(action.get("op") or "")
231:     risk = str(state.get("risk") or "read")
232: 
233:     pending = record_approval(actor, action, "pending", risk=risk)
234:     requested_by = str(pending.get("requester") or context.user_id)
235: 
236:     can_decide, allowed_roles, approver_label = resolve_approver(op, tuple(context.roles))
237:     payload = {
238:         "type": "approve",
239:         "mode": "decide" if can_decide else "request",
240:         "requested_by": requested_by,
241:         "title": summary_of(action),
242:         "fields": fields_of(action),
243:         "citations": _citations(state),
244:         "risk": risk,
245:         "required_roles": allowed_roles,
246:         "hint": (
247:             "发布后全员可见，且不能撤回；请确认内容与引用都站得住。"
248:             if op == "publish_kb_article"
249:             else "关闭并通知提单人后撤不回；请确认解决说明写得清楚。"
250:         )
251:         if can_decide
252:         else f"已提交给{approver_label}审批。把 thread 交给有权限的同事，用同一条 thread resume 即可继续。",
253:     }
254:     answer = interrupt(payload)
255: 
256:     # 恢复时可能是另一个人（主管）。没有审批权的人点了 → 直接回一句结束，不替他做决定
257:     decider = runtime.context
258:     decider_can, _, decider_label = resolve_approver(op, tuple(decider.roles))
259:     if not decider_can:
260:         return {
261:             "outcome": "denied",
262:             "reply": (
263:                 f"这件事要{decider_label}审批，当前身份（" + "、".join(decider.roles) + "）不能点通过。"
264:                 f"发起人：{requested_by}。请让有权限的同事拿同一条 thread resume。"
265:             ),
266:         }
267: 
268:     parsed = normalize_human_input(answer)
269:     kind = parsed.get("type") or "approve"
270:     self_approved = requested_by == decider.user_id
271:     record_approval(
272:         actor,
273:         action,
274:         DECISION_STATUS.get(kind, "rejected"),
275:         risk=risk,
276:         note=("自审" if self_approved else "") + (parsed.get("message") or ""),
277:         approver=decider.user_id,
278:     )
279:     approval = {
280:         "approver": decider.user_id,
281:         "roles": list(decider.roles),
282:         "required_roles": allowed_roles,
283:         "requested_by": requested_by,
284:         "self_approved": self_approved,
285:     }
286: 
287:     if kind == "reject":
288:         message = parsed.get("message") or "审批未通过"
289:         return {
290:             "approval": approval,
291:             "outcome": "rejected",
292:             "reply": f"{summary_of(action)}没有通过审批：{message}。需要的话改完再提一次。",
293:         }
294: 
295:     action_patched = patch_action(action, parsed.get("patch") or {})
296:     return {"action": action_patched, "approval": approval}

(End of file - total 296 lines)

<path>D:\zuixin\backend\app\agents\nodes\execute.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """execute 节点：全图唯一允许"真正改数据"的地方。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义：
4: - 《第3节 Think In LangGraph》：把"会留下痕迹的动作"集中到一处，方便加审计、重试、幂等
5: - 《第3节 MCP》：工具不在本进程里，通过 MCP 协议调用（FastMCP server + MultiServerMCPClient）
6: - 《第2节 Middleware》HumanInTheLoop：真正的兜底在工具层，业务中断在前面已经做完
7: 
8: 这里做四件事：
9: 1. 装身份：从 Runtime Context 取 actor（工单 / 知识库 server 都按它再判一次权限）
10: 2. 调工具：按 action.server / action.op 分派到对应 MCP server，只传该工具允许的参数
11: 3. 记结果：result / ticket_no / via 写回 State（后续 compose_reply 用它组织话术）
12: 4. 兜底：工具挂了不让整条链路崩，返回结构化错误
13: 
14: 注意：**去重（幂等）已经在 ticket_server 里做完了**，这里只认它回的 deduplicated 标记。
15: """
16: 
17: from __future__ import annotations
18: 
19: import logging
20: from typing import Any
21: 
22: from langgraph.runtime import Runtime
23: 
24: from ..state import TicketState, UserContext, actor_of
25: 
26: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
27: 
28: 
29: def _call_tool(server: str, op: str, args: dict[str, Any], actor: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
30:     """按 server + op 分派到对应的工具封装；参数白名单在这里收口。"""
31:     if server == "kb":
32:         from ..tools import kb_tools
33: 
34:         if op == "publish_kb_article":
35:             return kb_tools.publish_kb_article(
36:                 actor,
37:                 title=args.get("title") or "",
38:                 content=args.get("content") or "",
39:                 tags=args.get("tags") or [],
40:                 change_note=args.get("change_note") or "",
41:                 status=args.get("status") or "published",
42:             )
43:         if op == "search_kb":
44:             return kb_tools.search_kb(actor, args.get("query") or "", top_k=int(args.get("top_k") or 3))
45:         return {"ok": False, "error": {"code": "unsupported", "reason": f"kb server 不支持 {op}"}}
46: 
47:     from ..tools import ticket_tools
48: 
49:     if op == "create_ticket":
50:         return ticket_tools.create_ticket(
51:             actor,
52:             title=args.get("title") or "",
53:             description=args.get("description") or "",
54:             urgency=args.get("urgency") or "medium",
55:             category=args.get("category") or "其他",
56:         )
57:     if op == "update_ticket":
58:         return ticket_tools.update_ticket(actor, args.get("ticket_no") or "", args.get("patch") or {})
59:     if op == "close_ticket":
60:         return ticket_tools.close_ticket(
61:             actor,
62:             args.get("ticket_no") or "",
63:             resolution=args.get("resolution") or "",
64:             notify_reporter=bool(args.get("notify_reporter")),
65:         )
66:     if op == "query_ticket":
67:         return ticket_tools.query_ticket(actor, args.get("ticket_no") or "")
68:     if op == "list_tickets":
69:         return ticket_tools.list_tickets(
70:             actor,
71:             status=args.get("status") or "",
72:             limit=int(args.get("limit") or 10),
73:         )
74:     return {"ok": False, "error": {"code": "unsupported", "reason": f"ticket server 不支持 {op}"}}
75: 
76: 
77: def execute_action(state: TicketState, runtime: Runtime[UserContext]) -> dict[str, Any]:
78:     action = state.get("action") or {}
79:     op = action.get("op") or ""
80:     server = action.get("server") or ("kb" if op == "publish_kb_article" else "ticket")
81:     args = dict(action.get("args") or {})
82:     actor = actor_of(runtime)
83: 
84:     # 最后一道闸门复核：不可逆动作必须带着"已批准"的凭证，否则一律不执行。
85:     # （路由已经保证不会走到这里；这一层防的是以后有人改图/改路由把闸门绕过去）
86:     approved = bool((state.get("approval") or {}).get("approver"))
87:     if state.get("risk") == "write_irreversible" and not approved:
88:         return {
89:             "result": {"ok": False, "error": {"code": "blocked", "reason": "不可逆动作缺少审批"}, "op": op},
90:             "outcome": "failed",
91:             "reply": None,
92:         }
93: 
94:     try:
95:         data = _call_tool(server, op, args, actor)
96:     except Exception as exc:
97:         logger.exception("execute 调用 %s.%s 失败", server, op)
98:         return {
99:             "result": {"ok": False, "error": {"code": "tool_error", "reason": str(exc)}, "op": op},
100:             "outcome": "failed",
101:             "reply": None,
102:         }
103: 
104:     ok = bool(data.get("ok"))
105:     deduplicated = bool(data.get("deduplicated"))
106:     result = {**data, "op": op, "server": server}
107: 
108:     if ok:
109:         via = "mcp_dedup" if deduplicated else "mcp_ok"
110:     elif (data.get("error") or {}).get("code") == "forbidden" if isinstance(data.get("error"), dict) else False:
111:         via = "mcp_denied"
112:     else:
113:         via = "mcp_failed"
114: 
115:     return {
116:         "result": result,
117:         "via": via,
118:         "outcome": None if ok else "failed",
119:         "reply": None,
120:     }

1: """Milvus 数据层：检索/写入全部走 pymilvus 官方封装，不自己算分。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义《第2节 Milvus向量库》：
4: - create_schema / add_field / DataType（dim=1024 的 FLOAT_VECTOR）
5: - 内置 BM25：Function(function_type=FunctionType.BM25)，由 Milvus 服务端算稀疏向量
6: - 索引：dense = AUTOINDEX + COSINE；sparse = SPARSE_INVERTED_INDEX + BM25
7: - 混合检索：client.hybrid_search(reqs=[...], ranker=RRFRanker(k=60))
8: - 标量过滤：tenant_id == "..."，多租户隔离靠 filter 而不是靠多个 collection
9: 
10: 和 LangChain 封装（langchain_milvus.Milvus）的关系：
11: 讲义里两种写法都讲了。这里用 pymilvus 原生 API，是因为要显式展示
12: "两路召回 + RRF" 的过程（面试讲得清），LangChain 那层只是把同一套 API 包了一层。
13: """
14: 
15: from __future__ import annotations
16: 
17: import hashlib
18: import logging
19: import time
20: from typing import Any
21: 
22: from pymilvus import (
23:     AnnSearchRequest,
24:     DataType,
25:     Function,
26:     FunctionType,
27:     MilvusClient,
28:     RRFRanker,
29: )
30: 
31: from ..settings import settings
32: from .embedding import get_embedder
33: from .splitter import Chunk, make_citation
34: 
35: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
36: 
37: DENSE_FIELD = "dense"
38: SPARSE_FIELD = "sparse"
39: TEXT_FIELD = "content"
40: BM25_FUNCTION = "content_bm25"
41: # collection 结构版本标记：缺这个字段说明是老库（旧 schema），自动重建
42: SCHEMA_MARKER = "source_file"
43: # 检索结果回传的标量字段（引用角标 + 溯源用）
44: OUTPUT_FIELDS = [
45:     "doc_id",
46:     "title",
47:     "h1",
48:     "h2",
49:     "h3",
50:     "source_file",
51:     "chunk_index",
52:     "content",
53:     "doc_status",

56: _client: MilvusClient | None = None
57: 
58: 
59: def get_client() -> MilvusClient:
60:     global _client
61:     if _client is None:
62:         _client = MilvusClient(uri=settings.milvus_uri, timeout=settings.milvus_timeout)
63:     return _client
64: 
65: 
66: def reset_client() -> None:
67:     global _client
68:     if _client is not None:
69:         try:
70:             _client.close()
71:         except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
72:             pass
73:     _client = None
74: 
75: 
76: def _filter_expr(tenant_id: str | None, only_published: bool = True) -> str:
77:     """过滤表达式：多租户隔离 + 只检索已发布的文章（草稿不该出现在员工答案里）。"""
78:     parts: list[str] = []
79:     if tenant_id:
80:         parts.append(f'tenant_id == "{tenant_id}"')
81:     if only_published:
82:         parts.append('doc_status == "published"')
83:     return " and ".join(parts)
84: 
85: 
86: def ensure_collection(drop: bool = False, client: MilvusClient | None = None) -> MilvusClient:
87:     """建 collection：字段 + 内置 BM25 函数 + 三个索引。已存在则直接返回。"""
88:     client = client or get_client()
89:     name = settings.milvus_collection
90: 
91:     if client.has_collection(name):
92:         if not drop:
93:             fields = [f.get("name") for f in (client.describe_collection(name).get("fields") or [])]
94:             if SCHEMA_MARKER in fields:
95:                 return client
96:             logger.warning("collection %s 是旧 schema（缺 %s），自动重建", name, SCHEMA_MARKER)
97:         client.drop_collection(name)
98:         logger.info("已删除旧 collection %s", name)
99: 
100:     schema = client.create_schema(auto_id=False, enable_dynamic_field=False)
101:     schema.add_field("pk", DataType.INT64, is_primary=True)
102:     schema.add_field("doc_id", DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=64)
103:     # 多租户隔离：每行带 tenant_id，检索时用 filter 表达式过滤
104:     schema.add_field("tenant_id", DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=64)
105:     schema.add_field("title", DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=512)
106:     # 章节三层：h1=第 X 章、h2=X.Y 节、h3=更深一级；引用串就用这三层拼
107:     schema.add_field("h1", DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=128)
108:     schema.add_field("h2", DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=128)
109:     schema.add_field("h3", DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=128)
110:     # 溯源字段：这个块来自哪个文件、是第几块（重跑入库靠它们幂等覆盖）
111:     schema.add_field(SCHEMA_MARKER, DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=256)
112:     schema.add_field("chunk_index", DataType.INT64)
113:     # 开启中文分词器，BM25 才知道怎么切 "增值税发票"
114:     schema.add_field(
115:         TEXT_FIELD,
116:         DataType.VARCHAR,
117:         max_length=8192,
118:         enable_analyzer=True,
119:         analyzer_params={"type": "chinese"},
120:     )
121:     # 只有 published 允许被检索（审批通过才会写成 published）
122:     schema.add_field("doc_status", DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=16)
123:     schema.add_field(DENSE_FIELD, DataType.FLOAT_VECTOR, dim=settings.embedding_dim)
124:     schema.add_field(SPARSE_FIELD, DataType.SPARSE_FLOAT_VECTOR)
125:     # 内置 BM25：Milvus 服务端把 content 算成稀疏向量写进 sparse 字段
126:     schema.add_function(
127:         Function(
128:             name=BM25_FUNCTION,
129:             input_field_names=[TEXT_FIELD],
130:             output_field_names=[SPARSE_FIELD],
131:             function_type=FunctionType.BM25,
132:         )
133:     )
134: 
135:     index_params = client.prepare_index_params()
136:     index_params.add_index(
137:         field_name=DENSE_FIELD, index_type="AUTOINDEX", metric_type="COSINE"
138:     )
139:     index_params.add_index(
140:         field_name=SPARSE_FIELD, index_type="SPARSE_INVERTED_INDEX", metric_type="BM25"
141:     )
142:     # 标量索引用 INVERTED：tenant_id / source_file 过滤 + doc_id 精确查
143:     index_params.add_index(field_name="tenant_id", index_type="INVERTED")
144:     index_params.add_index(field_name="doc_id", index_type="INVERTED")
145:     index_params.add_index(field_name=SCHEMA_MARKER, index_type="INVERTED")
146:     index_params.add_index(field_name="doc_status", index_type="INVERTED")
147: 
148:     client.create_collection(
149:         collection_name=name, schema=schema, index_params=index_params
150:     )
151:     logger.info("已创建 collection %s（dim=%s）", name, settings.embedding_dim)
152:     return client
153: 
154: 
155: def _chunk_pk(tenant_id: str, doc_id: str, chunk_index: int) -> int:
156:     """主键：租户 + 文档 + 块序号 的稳定哈希。
157: 
158:     auto_id=False 时主键要自己给；用稳定哈希保证「重复入库 = 覆盖同一行」，
159:     不会每次 ingest 都往库里堆一份重复 chunk。
160:     """
161:     digest = hashlib.sha1(f"{tenant_id}:{doc_id}:{chunk_index}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
162:     return int(digest[:15], 16)
163: 
164: 
165: def upsert_chunks(
166:     chunks: list[Chunk],
167:     *,
168:     tenant_id: str | None = None,
169:     doc_status: str = "published",
170:     force: bool = False,
171: ) -> int:
172:     """把切分好的 chunk 写进 Milvus（幂等 upsert）。
173: 
174:     - dense：用 `chunk.text`（正文已带「文档 · 章节」面包屑）算 embedding；
175:     - sparse：不传，由 collection 上的内置 BM25 函数在服务端生成；
176:     - `force=True` 会 drop 重建 collection（改 schema / 换 embedding 模型时用）。
177:     """
178:     tenant_id = tenant_id or settings.default_tenant
179:     if not chunks:
180:         return 0
181:     client = ensure_collection(drop=force)
182:     embedder = get_embedder()
183:     vectors = embedder.embed_documents([chunk.text for chunk in chunks])
184:     rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
185:     for chunk, vector in zip(chunks, vectors):
186:         meta = chunk.metadata
187:         doc_id = str(meta.get("doc_id", ""))
188:         chunk_index = int(meta.get("chunk_index", 0))
189:         rows.append(
190:             {
191:                 "pk": _chunk_pk(tenant_id, doc_id, chunk_index),
192:                 "doc_id": doc_id,
193:                 "tenant_id": tenant_id,
194:                 "title": str(meta.get("title", ""))[:500],
195:                 "h1": str(meta.get("h1", ""))[:120],
196:                 "h2": str(meta.get("h2", ""))[:120],
197:                 "h3": str(meta.get("h3", ""))[:120],
198:                 SCHEMA_MARKER: str(meta.get("source_file", ""))[:240],
199:                 "chunk_index": chunk_index,
200:                 # 存「带面包屑的正文」：BM25 也能吃到章节词，回答引用更准
201:                 "content": chunk.text[:8000],
202:                 "doc_status": doc_status,
203:                 DENSE_FIELD: vector,
204:             }
205:         )
206:     client.upsert(collection_name=settings.milvus_collection, data=rows)
207:     client.flush(settings.milvus_collection)
208:     # 刚写入的数据要先被索引成可检索 segment，稍等一下再检索（否则第一问会“查不到”）
209:     time.sleep(1.2)
210:     return len(rows)
211: 
212: 
213: def collection_stats() -> dict[str, Any]:
214:     client = get_client()
215:     name = settings.milvus_collection
216:     if not client.has_collection(name):
217:         return {"collection": name, "exists": False, "rows": 0}
218:     stats = client.get_collection_stats(name)
219:     return {"collection": name, "exists": True, "rows": int(stats.get("row_count", 0))}
220: 
221: 
222: def _hit_row(hit: dict[str, Any], channel: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
223:     """把 Milvus 的一条命中摊平成业务字段（含现成的引用串）。"""
224:     entity = hit.get("entity") or {}
225:     title = str(entity.get("title", ""))
226:     h1 = str(entity.get("h1", ""))
227:     h2 = str(entity.get("h2", ""))
228:     h3 = str(entity.get("h3", ""))
229:     return {
230:         "doc_id": str(entity.get("doc_id", "")),
231:         "title": title,
232:         "h1": h1,
233:         "h2": h2,
234:         "h3": h3,
235:         "citation": make_citation(title, h1, h2, h3) or str(entity.get(SCHEMA_MARKER, "")),
236:         "source_file": str(entity.get(SCHEMA_MARKER, "")),
237:         "chunk_index": int(entity.get("chunk_index") or 0),
238:         "content": str(entity.get("content", "")),
239:         "score": round(float(hit.get("distance") or 0.0), 4),
240:         "doc_status": str(entity.get("doc_status", "published")),
241:         "channel": channel,
242:     }
243: 
244: 
245: def hybrid_search(
246:     query: str, k: int = 3, tenant_id: str | None = None, only_published: bool = True
247: ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
248:     """混合检索（一条 RPC 完成 两路召回 + RRF 融合）。
249: 
250:     整条链路都在 Milvus 服务端：
251:     - 两路召回：AnnSearchRequest(dense, metric=COSINE) + AnnSearchRequest(sparse, metric=BM25)；
252:     - 融合算法：RRFRanker(k=60)，公式 1/(k+rank) 由 Milvus 实现，客户端一行都没写；
253:     - 结果直接是排好序的 top-k，我们只做字段搬运。
254:     """
255:     client = ensure_collection()
256:     expr = _filter_expr(tenant_id, only_published)
257:     vector = get_embedder().embed_query(query)
258:     reqs = [
259:         AnnSearchRequest(
260:             data=[vector],
261:             anns_field=DENSE_FIELD,
262:             param={"metric_type": "COSINE"},
263:             limit=max(k * 2, 10),
264:             expr=expr,
265:         ),
266:         AnnSearchRequest(
267:             data=[query],
268:             anns_field=SPARSE_FIELD,
269:             param={"metric_type": "BM25"},
270:             limit=max(k * 2, 10),
271:             expr=expr,
272:         ),
273:     ]
274:     results = client.hybrid_search(
275:         collection_name=settings.milvus_collection,
276:         reqs=reqs,
277:         ranker=RRFRanker(k=60),
278:         limit=k,
279:         output_fields=OUTPUT_FIELDS,
280:     )
281:     return [_hit_row(hit, "hybrid") for hit in (results[0] if results else [])]
282: 
283: 
284: def publish_article(
285:     *,
286:     title: str,
287:     content: str,
288:     tags: list[str] | None = None,
289:     change_note: str = "",
290:     tenant_id: str | None = None,
291:     doc_status: str = "published",
292: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
293:     """把一篇知识文章写进 Milvus（供 kb-server 的 publish_kb_article 调用）。
294: 
295:     人工发布不走文档切分，所以只有一块；pk 依旧用稳定的哈希，
296:     同一篇文章重复发布不会插两行（这里用毫秒时间戳做后缀，保证「每次发布算一篇新文章」）。
297:     """
298:     tenant_id = tenant_id or settings.default_tenant
299:     client = ensure_collection()
300:     doc_id = f"kb-{int(time.time() * 1000)}"
301:     tags = tags or []
302:     text = f"【{make_citation(title, tags[0] if tags else '', change_note)}】\n{content}"
303:     row = {
304:         "pk": _chunk_pk(tenant_id, doc_id, 0),
305:         "doc_id": doc_id,
306:         "tenant_id": tenant_id,
307:         "title": title[:200],
308:         "h1": (tags[0] if tags else "知识沉淀")[:60],
309:         "h2": (change_note or (tags[-1] if tags else ""))[:60],
310:         "h3": "",
311:         SCHEMA_MARKER: f"{doc_id}.md",
312:         "chunk_index": 0,
313:         "content": text[:8000],
314:         "doc_status": doc_status,
315:         DENSE_FIELD: get_embedder().embed_query(text),
316:     }
317:     client.upsert(collection_name=settings.milvus_collection, data=[row])
318:     client.flush(settings.milvus_collection)
319:     return {"doc_id": doc_id, "title": title, "doc_status": doc_status, "tenant_id": tenant_id}
320: 
321: 
322: def ping() -> bool:
323:     """健康检查：连得上并且 collection 存在才算 ready。"""
324:     try:
325:         client = get_client()
326:         return bool(client.has_collection(settings.milvus_collection))
327:     except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
328:         logger.warning("Milvus 不可用: %s", exc)
329:         return False

<content>
1: """把 `graph.stream(...)` 翻译成 SSE 文本（这一层只做翻译，不写业务）。
2: 
3: 事件协议（前端只认这 6 种）：
4: 
5:     event: meta       {thread_id, user_id, tenant_id, roles}
6:     event: node       {node, ms, intent?, risk?, gate?, outcome?, ...}  每走完一个节点推进度
7:     event: token      {text}                                           answer 节点的模型输出
8:     event: interrupt  {thread_id, next, payloads, elapsed_ms}           图停下来了，等人
9:     event: done       {outcome, reply, citations, ticket_no, via, ...}  一条链路走完（outcome 为空=正常完成）
10:     event: error      {message}
11: 
12: 为什么用 `stream_mode=["updates", "messages"]`：
13: - `updates`：每走完一个节点给一条，前端能显示"正在检索 / 正在规划"，也用来量节点耗时；
14: - `messages`：模型在节点里产生的 token 也会流出来（LangGraph 自带回调，不用改节点代码），
15:   我们只转发 `answer_with_citations` 的 token（分类节点输出的是 JSON，流给用户看没意义）。
16: """
17: 
18: from __future__ import annotations
19: 
20: import json
21: import time
22: from typing import Any, Iterator
23: 
24: # 只把"作答"节点的 token 推给前端
25: TOKEN_NODES = {"answer_with_citations"}
26: 
27: # 每走完一个节点，往前端推这几个"人看得懂"的字段（其余细节留在 State / 详情接口）
28: NODE_FIELDS = ("intent", "risk", "gate", "outcome", "via")
29: 
30: 
31: def sse(event: str, data: Any) -> str:
32:     """拼一个 SSE 事件（data 走 JSON，避免裸换行破坏协议）。"""
33:     payload = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
34:     return f"event: {event}\ndata: {payload}\n\n"
35: 
36: 
37: def _text_of(message: Any) -> str:
38:     """从 LangChain 的 message chunk 里取文本（兼容 str 和 content block 两种）。"""
39:     content = getattr(message, "content", "")
40:     if isinstance(content, str):
41:         return content
42:     if isinstance(content, list):
43:         parts = []
44:         for block in content:
45:             if isinstance(block, str):
46:                 parts.append(block)
47:             elif isinstance(block, dict) and block.get("type") in {"text", "text_delta"}:
48:                 parts.append(str(block.get("text") or ""))
49:         return "".join(parts)
50:     return ""
51: 
52: 
53: def _usage_of(message: Any) -> tuple[int, int]:
54:     """取模型返回的 token 用量（DeepSeek 在流结束的那条 chunk 上带 usage_metadata）。"""
55:     usage = getattr(message, "usage_metadata", None)
56:     if not isinstance(usage, dict):
57:         return 0, 0
58:     return int(usage.get("input_tokens") or 0), int(usage.get("output_tokens") or 0)
59: 
60: 
61: def _node_event(node: str, delta: Any, ms: int = 0) -> dict[str, Any]:
62:     """节点事件只带"值得展示"的字段，别把整个 state 推给前端。"""
63:     data: dict[str, Any] = {"node": node}
64:     if ms:
65:         data["ms"] = ms
66:     if isinstance(delta, dict):
67:         for key in NODE_FIELDS:
68:             value = delta.get(key)
69:             if value not in (None, "", [], {}):
70:                 data[key] = value
71:         if isinstance(delta.get("policy_evidence"), list):
72:             data["evidence_count"] = len(delta["policy_evidence"])
73:         if isinstance(delta.get("facts"), list):
74:             data["facts_count"] = len(delta["facts"])
75:     return data
76: 
77: 
78: def _citations(state: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
79:     """把检索到的资料整理成前端渲染引用卡片用的字符串。"""
80:     items = []
81:     for hit in state.get("policy_evidence") or []:
82:         citation = hit.get("citation") or hit.get("title") or ""
83:         if citation:
84:             items.append(str(citation))
85:     return items
86: 
87: 
88: def _ticket_no(state: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
89:     """单号从工具返回值现取（State 里不再存第二份）。"""
90:     result = state.get("result") or {}
91:     ticket = result.get("ticket") if isinstance(result.get("ticket"), dict) else {}
92:     return ticket.get("ticket_no") or result.get("ticket_no") or None
93: 
94: 
95: def _done_event(state: dict[str, Any], elapsed_ms: int, tokens: dict[str, int]) -> dict[str, Any]:
96:     return {
97:         "thread_id": state.get("thread_id"),
98:         "outcome": state.get("outcome"),
99:         "intent": state.get("intent"),
100:         "gate": state.get("gate"),
101:         "risk": state.get("risk"),
102:         "reply": state.get("reply"),
103:         "citations": _citations(state),
104:         "ticket_no": _ticket_no(state),
105:         "via": state.get("via"),
106:         "approval": state.get("approval"),
107:         "elapsed_ms": elapsed_ms,
108:         "tokens": tokens,
109:     }
110: 
111: 
112: def iter_sse(
113:     graph: Any,
114:     payload: Any,
115:     config: dict[str, Any],
116:     *,
117:     context: Any,
118:     thread_id: str,
119:     identity: dict[str, Any],
120: ) -> Iterator[str]:
121:     """跑一次图，把过程翻译成 SSE 文本；结束后再读一次 checkpoint 判断"停没停"。"""
122:     started = time.perf_counter()
123:     last = started
124:     tokens_in = tokens_out = 0
125:     yield sse("meta", {"thread_id": thread_id, **identity})
126: 
127:     try:
128:         for mode, chunk in graph.stream(
129:             payload, config, context=context, stream_mode=["updates", "messages"]
130:         ):
131:             if mode == "messages":
132:                 message, meta = chunk
133:                 used_in, used_out = _usage_of(message)
134:                 tokens_in += used_in
135:                 tokens_out += used_out
136:                 if (meta or {}).get("langgraph_node") in TOKEN_NODES:
137:                     text = _text_of(message)
138:                     if text:
139:                         yield sse("token", {"text": text})
140:                 continue
141:             now = time.perf_counter()
142:             for node, delta in (chunk or {}).items():
143:                 if node == "__interrupt__":
144:                     continue
145:                 yield sse("node", _node_event(node, delta, int((now - last) * 1000)))
146:                 last = now
147:     except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - 上层只负责把错误告诉前端
148:         yield sse("error", {"message": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"})
149:         return
150: 
151:     elapsed_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000)
152:     tokens = {"in": tokens_in, "out": tokens_out}
153:     snapshot = graph.get_state(config)
154:     interrupts = tuple(getattr(snapshot, "interrupts", ()) or ())
155:     if interrupts:
156:         yield sse(
157:             "interrupt",
158:             {
159:                 "thread_id": thread_id,
160:                 "next": list(snapshot.next or ()),
161:                 "payloads": [getattr(item, "value", None) for item in interrupts],
162:                 "elapsed_ms": elapsed_ms,
163:                 "tokens": tokens,
164:             },
165:         )
166:         return
167:     yield sse("done", _done_event(dict(snapshot.values or {}), elapsed_ms, tokens))

(End of file - total 167 lines)
</content>

<path>D:\zuixin\backend\app\mcp\servers\guard.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """工具层鉴权（server 侧，Agent 不可信）。
2: 
3: 为什么权限必须放在 MCP server 里（面试要能讲）：
4: - Agent 手里的 tenant_id / reporter 是模型"填"的参数，模型可能填错，也可能被提示词攻击改写；
5: - 工具是真正改数据的地方，谁调用、以什么身份调用，只能由**服务端**按身份再判一次；
6: - 所以每个工具都要求传 `actor`（身份票据），server 用它覆盖 tenant/reporter，并按规则判定能不能做。
7: 
8: 身份票据结构（由 Agent 从 Runtime Context 生成，绝不从模型输出里取）：
9:     {"user_id": "u-1002", "tenant_id": "tenant-demo", "roles": ["employee"]}
10: 
11: 角色与权限：
12: - employee   只能读/改/关**自己提的单**；列单只能列自己的；不能发布知识
13: - approver   能读/改/关本租户所有单（审批人视角）
14: - kb_owner   能发布知识文章（审批通过后）
15: - 跨租户一律拒绝
16: """
17: 
18: from __future__ import annotations
19: 
20: import json
21: from typing import Any
22: 
23: VALID_ROLES = ("employee", "approver", "kb_owner")
24: PRIVILEGED_ROLES = {"approver"}
25: KB_PUBLISHER_ROLES = {"kb_owner"}
26: 
27: 
28: def normalize_actor(actor: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
29:     """把调用方传来的 actor 规整成身份票据；不合法返回 None（就当未认证）。"""
30:     if isinstance(actor, str) and actor.strip():
31:         try:
32:             actor = json.loads(actor)
33:         except json.JSONDecodeError:
34:             return None
35:     if not isinstance(actor, dict):
36:         return None
37:     user_id = str(actor.get("user_id") or "").strip()
38:     tenant_id = str(actor.get("tenant_id") or "").strip()
39:     if not user_id or not tenant_id:
40:         return None
41:     roles = tuple(str(role) for role in (actor.get("roles") or ()) if str(role) in VALID_ROLES)
42:     return {"user_id": user_id, "tenant_id": tenant_id, "roles": roles or ("employee",)}
43: 
44: 
45: def deny(code: str, reason: str, **extra: Any) -> str:
46:     """统一的拒绝响应：机器可读 error.code + 给人看的中文 reason。"""
47:     payload: dict[str, Any] = {"ok": False, "error": {"code": code, "reason": reason}}
48:     payload.update(extra)
49:     return json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)
50: 
51: 
52: def require_actor(actor: Any) -> tuple[dict[str, Any] | None, str | None]:
53:     """返回 (票据, 拒绝响应)。票据为 None 时调用方应直接把拒绝响应返回给 Agent。"""
54:     normalized = normalize_actor(actor)
55:     if normalized is None:
56:         return None, deny("unauthorized", "缺少调用方身份（actor），拒绝执行", need="user_id / tenant_id")
57:     return normalized, None
58: 
59: 
60: def is_privileged(actor: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
61:     return bool(PRIVILEGED_ROLES & set(actor["roles"]))
62: 
63: 
64: def can_publish_kb(actor: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
65:     return bool(KB_PUBLISHER_ROLES & set(actor["roles"]))
66: 
67: def same_tenant(actor: dict[str, Any], record: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
68:     return str(record.get("tenant_id") or "") == actor["tenant_id"]
69: 
70: 
71: def can_read_ticket(actor: dict[str, Any], record: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
72:     """同租户 + （特权角色 / 本人提单）。"""
73:     if not same_tenant(actor, record):
74:         return False
75:     return is_privileged(actor) or str(record.get("reporter") or "") == actor["user_id"]
76: 
77: 
78: def can_write_ticket(actor: dict[str, Any], record: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
79:     """改/关与读同一条规则：自己的单能处理，审批人能处理本租户任意单。"""
80:     return can_read_ticket(actor, record)
81: 
82: 
83: def notify_reports_self(actor: dict[str, Any], record: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
84:     """通知对象就是操作者自己 → 这个通知没有意义（不用为它升级成不可逆）。"""
85:     return str(record.get("reporter") or "") == actor["user_id"]
86: 
87: 
88: def ticket_flags(actor: dict[str, Any], record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
89:     """给读接口附上"这张单你能做什么"的判定结果。
90: 
91:     规则留在 server（Agent 不可信），节点只读结果、不重复实现一遍权限。
92:     """
93:     return {
94:         "can_write": can_write_ticket(actor, record),
95:         "notify_reports_self": notify_reports_self(actor, record),
96:     }

(End of file - total 96 lines)

<content>
1: """classify 节点：先判断"员工在问知识、还是在要动作、还是在闲聊"。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义：
4: - 《第1节 LangChain核心组件》：用 with_structured_output 让模型输出结构化分类
5: - 《第1节 基本概念和入门》：节点就是一个普通函数，入参 State，出参"要更新的字段"
6: - 《第3节 Think In LangGraph》：不做条件边，决策写在节点里，用 Command(goto=...) 指定下一跳
7: 
8: 模型调用失败必须报错（没有规则兜底）：见 llm.py 的"缺 key 直接抛异常"。
9: """
10: 
11: from __future__ import annotations
12: 
13: from typing import Any, Literal
14: 
15: from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
16: from langchain_core.output_parsers import PydanticOutputParser
17: 
18: from ..llm import get_chat_model
19: from ..prompts.service_desk import CLASSIFY_SYSTEM_PROMPT
20: from ..state import TicketState
21: 
22: 
23: class Classification(BaseModel):
24:     """模型侧的结构化输出：意图、动作、主题，以及"这句话提到了哪张单"。
25: 
26:     只让模型判断"代码推不出来"的东西：
27:     - 风险等级不在这里：它是业务规则，由 plan_action 查 RISK_BY_OP + compute_risk 按参数算；
28:     - MCP server 不在这里：op 就能决定（publish_kb_article → kb，其余 → ticket）。
29:     """
30: 
31:     intent: Literal["knowledge", "action", "chat"] = Field(description="员工在问知识 / 要动作 / 闲聊")
32:     op: str = Field(default="none", description="动作类型：create_ticket / close_ticket / update_ticket / list_tickets / query_ticket / publish_kb_article / none")
33:     topic: str = Field(default="其他", description="业务主题")
34:     summary: str = Field(default="", description="一句话概括员工诉求（<=40 字）")
35:     urgency: Literal["low", "medium", "high"] = Field(default="medium")
36:     # 下面两个字段专门给"取证据"用：它和 intent/op 是两条独立的轴
37:     # （知识型问题也会提到单号："TK-… 按规定能不能延期"）。
38:     reference: Literal["none", "number", "my_last", "my_recent"] = Field(
39:         default="none",
40:         description="提到了哪张单：none=没提；number=有明确单号；my_last=我的最近一张；my_recent=我的好几张",
41:     )
42:     reference_hint: str = Field(default="", description="原话里指向这张单的片段，照抄，不要编造单号")
43: 
44: 
45: def extract_query(state: TicketState) -> str:
46:     """取本轮用户输入：优先 state["query"]，否则取最后一条 HumanMessage。"""
47:     query = (state.get("query") or "").strip()
48:     if query:
49:         return query
50:     for message in reversed(state.get("messages") or []):
51:         if getattr(message, "type", "") == "human":
52:             return str(message.content).strip()
53:     return ""
54: 
55: 
56: def _classify_with_llm(query: str) -> Classification:
57:     """调模型做结构化分类。
58: 
59:     DeepSeek 目前只支持 response_format=json_object（不支持 json_schema），
60:     所以用 method="json_mode"，并把字段说明拼进 system prompt。
61:     """
62:     model = get_chat_model()
63:     parser = PydanticOutputParser(pydantic_object=Classification)
64:     chain = model.with_structured_output(Classification, method="json_mode")
65:     result = chain.invoke(
66:         [
67:             ("system", CLASSIFY_SYSTEM_PROMPT + "\n\n" + parser.get_format_instructions()),
68:             ("human", query),
69:         ]
70:     )
71:     if not isinstance(result, Classification):
72:         result = Classification.model_validate(result)
73:     return result
74: 
75: 
76: def classify_request(state: TicketState) -> dict[str, Any]:
77:     """看懂这句话：在提问 / 在命令 / 在闲聊，给出 intent 与动作类型。"""
78:     query = extract_query(state)
79:     result = _classify_with_llm(query)
80: 
81:     action: dict[str, Any] | None = None
82:     if result.intent == "action" and result.op not in {"", "none"}:
83:         action = {"op": result.op, "args": {}, "missing": []}
84: 
85:     update: dict[str, Any] = {
86:         "query": query,
87:         "intent": result.intent,
88:         "action": action,
89:         # 风险由 plan_action 按 op + 参数重算，这里先按最低档占位
90:         "risk": "read",
91:         "classification": {
92:             "topic": result.topic,
93:             "summary": result.summary or query[:40],
94:             "urgency": result.urgency,
95:             # 取证据用：模型只给"是哪一类指代"，不给单号值（单号由正则校验，防编造）
96:             "reference": result.reference,
97:             "reference_hint": result.reference_hint,
98:         },
99:     }
100: 
101:     if result.intent == "chat":
102:         update["reply"] = None
103:     return update

<content>
1: """取证据（gather_evidence）：主图第 3 个节点，一次调用里做两件事。
2: 
3: 为什么要这个节点（面试常问）：
4: - 回答"怎么办"需要公司制度（policy_evidence），回答"到哪一步了"需要业务事实（facts）；
5: - 两者都是"动手/开口之前必须先看清的东西"，所以放同一个节点，先取制度再取事实。
6: 
7: "提到了哪张单"是独立于 intent/op 的一条轴：问制度时也可能提到单号
8: （"TK-… 按规定能不能延期"）。这条轴**由模型判断**（`classification.reference` 枚举：
9: none / number / my_last / my_recent），代码只做两件事：单号必须正则校验通过才采信、
10: 指代类型翻译成查几张。**这里不读 `op`**——op 是 `plan_action`（本节点之后）才有的字段。
11: 
12: 对应黑马讲义：RAG Agent（第1节）里的"先检索再生成"，以及 Think In LangGraph（第3节）的
13: "节点只做一件事：取数"。
14: """
15: 
16: from __future__ import annotations
17: 
18: import logging
19: from typing import Any
20: 
21: from .. import knowledge
22: from langgraph.runtime import Runtime
23: 
24: from ..state import TicketState, UserContext, actor_of
25: from ..tools import ticket_tools
26: from .plan_action import find_ticket_no
27: 
28: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
29: 
30: TOP_K = 3
31: 
32: # 动作型检索用的"主题词"：把口语化请求翻成知识库里可能出现的说法
33: ACTION_TOPICS: dict[str, str] = {
34:     "create_ticket": "工单 建单 提交流程 响应时间 优先级",
35:     "update_ticket": "工单 修改 变更 处理时限",
36:     "close_ticket": "工单 关闭 结案 通知 回访",
37:     "publish_kb_article": "知识库 发布 文章 审核规范",
38:     "query_ticket": "工单 查询 单号 状态",
39:     "list_tickets": "工单 列表 我的工单 未结单",
40: }
41: 
42: # "提到了哪张单"由模型判断（classification.reference），这里只做两件事：
43: # 单号必须能正则校验通过才采信（防模型编造），指代类型翻译成"查几张"。
44: LAST_ONE = 1
45: RECENT_MANY = 5
46: 
47: 
48: def _policy_query(state: TicketState) -> str:
49:     """知识型用员工原话；动作型用「主题词 + 动作」，避免把'帮我建个工单：财务系统登录不上'
50:     拿去检索出考勤/限流这类不相关制度。"""
51:     if state.get("intent") != "action":
52:         return state.get("query", "")
53:     action = state.get("action") or {}
54:     topic = ACTION_TOPICS.get(action.get("op", ""), "")
55:     return f"{topic} {state.get('query', '')}".strip()
56: 
57: 
58: def retrieve_policy(state: TicketState) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
59:     """查公司制度：Milvus 混合检索 → policy_evidence（带引用）。"""
60:     try:
61:         hits = knowledge.search(_policy_query(state), k=TOP_K, tenant_id=state.get("tenant_id"))
62:     except knowledge.MilvusUnavailable as exc:
63:         logger.warning("知识库不可用，跳过制度检索：%s", exc)
64:         return []
65:     evidence: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
66:     for index, hit in enumerate(hits, start=1):
67:         evidence.append(
68:             {
69:                 "idx": index,
70:                 "doc_id": hit["doc_id"],
71:                 "title": hit["title"],
72:                 # chapter = 第 X 章，section = X.Y 节（引用串里两层都带）
73:                 "chapter": hit.get("h2", ""),
74:                 "section": hit.get("h3") or hit.get("h2") or "",
75:                 "source_file": hit.get("source_file", ""),
76:                 "score": hit["score"],
77:                 "snippet": (hit.get("content") or "")[:180],
78:                 "citation": f"[{index}] {hit.get('citation') or hit['title']}",
79:             }
80:         )
81:     return evidence
82: 
83: 
84: def _where_ticket(state: TicketState) -> tuple[str, str]:
85:     """这句话提到了哪张单：返回 (指代类型, 单号)。
86: 
87:     指代类型听模型的（`classification.reference`），单号只认正则校验通过的值——
88:     这样"上次那个卡了半天的报修"也能查，而模型编出来的单号会被丢掉。
89:     """
90:     classification = state.get("classification") or {}
91:     kind = str(classification.get("reference") or "none")
92:     hint = str(classification.get("reference_hint") or "")
93:     ticket_no = find_ticket_no(hint) or find_ticket_no(state.get("query", ""))
94:     if ticket_no:
95:         return "number", ticket_no
96:     return (kind if kind in {"my_last", "my_recent"} else "none"), ""
97: 
98: 
99: def read_ticket_facts(state: TicketState, actor: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
100:     """查业务事实：只读工具（不写库、不改状态）。权限由 MCP server 按 actor 判定。
101: 
102:     三路：有单号 → 查这一张；"我的上一张" → 最近 1 张；"我的那些" → 最近 5 张。
103:     """
104:     args = (state.get("action") or {}).get("args") or {}
105: 
106:     kind, ticket_no = _where_ticket(state)
107:     ticket_no = args.get("ticket_no") or ticket_no
108:     if ticket_no:
109:         result = ticket_tools.query_ticket(actor, ticket_no) or {}
110:         ticket = result.get("ticket")
111:         if ticket:
112:             return [ticket]
113:         # 查不到/没权限也要留一条事实：下游据此"直接拒绝"，而不是继续问参数或进审批队列
114:         error = result.get("error") if isinstance(result.get("error"), dict) else {}
115:         if error:
116:             return [
117:                 {
118:                     "ticket_no": ticket_no,
119:                     "can_write": False,
120:                     "denied_reason": error.get("reason") or error.get("code") or "",
121:                 }
122:             ]
123:         return []
124: 
125:     # 指代消解：不问单号也能查，把"可解析的指代"和"真的缺参数"分开，
126:     # plan_action 就不会拿"我的上一张"去反问了。
127:     limit = {"my_last": LAST_ONE, "my_recent": RECENT_MANY}.get(kind, 0)
128:     if limit:
129:         result = ticket_tools.list_tickets(actor, limit=limit)
130:         return result.get("tickets") or []
131:     return []
132: 
133: 
134: def gather_evidence(state: TicketState, runtime: Runtime[UserContext]) -> dict[str, Any]:
135:     """节点入口：制度 + 事实一起取，返回结构化字段（不写自然语言）。"""
136:     actor = actor_of(runtime)
137:     policy_evidence = retrieve_policy(state)
138:     facts = read_ticket_facts(state, actor)
139:     return {"policy_evidence": policy_evidence, "facts": facts}

1: """plan_action 节点：把"员工想要什么"拆成"工具参数 + 风险等级 + 走哪个闸门"。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义：
4: - 《第1节 Runtime》：Context 只读（谁/租户/角色），State 可变（草稿、闸门、决策）
5: - 《第1节 LangChain核心组件》with_structured_output：模型负责补参数，但必须给出参数来源
6: - 《第2节 常见WorkFlow》：Routing——节点只算数据，走哪条路由由 graph.py 的条件边决定
7:

30: QUOTED_PATTERN = re.compile(r"[「“\"]([^」”\"]{2,40})[」”\"]")
31:

33: def extract_quoted_text(text: str) -> str:
34:     """员工用「」/引号写的标题优先当标题。"""
35:     match = QUOTED_PATTERN.search(text)
36:     return match.group(1).strip() if match else ""

40:     match = TICKET_NO_PATTERN.search(text)
41:     return match.group(0).upper() if match else None

43: 
44: NOTIFY_CLAUSE_PATTERN = re.compile(
45:     r"[，,、]?\s*(顺便|并且|然后|同时)?\s*(通知|告知|知会)(一下)?\s*(提单人|提报人|提交人|对方|他|她)?\s*$"
46: )
47: 
48: 
49: def _extract_resolution(query: str) -> str:
50:     text = NOTIFY_CLAUSE_PATTERN.sub("", query)
51:     text = re.sub(r"(把|将)?(工单)?\s*TK-\d{8}-\d{4}\s*(关闭|关掉|结了|结掉)", "", text)
52:     text = re.sub(r"^(帮我|麻烦|请|帮忙)", "", text)
53:     text = re.sub(r"(把|将)?[^，。,.!！]{0,8}(工单)?[^，。,.!！]{0,6}(关掉|关闭|结了|结掉)", "", text)
54:     text = text.strip("，。,.!！ ")
55:     # 员工只说"把那些单关掉"时，剩下的碎片（"把我工"）不能当解决说明写进工单
56:     return text if len(text) >= 4 else "员工反馈问题已解决，确认关闭"

59: def _extract_patch(query: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
60:     patch: dict[str, Any] = {}
61:     if "紧急" in query or "高优" in query or "最高" in query:
62:         patch["urgency"] = "high"
63:     elif "低优" in query or "不急" in query:
64:         patch["urgency"] = "low"
65:     elif "中优" in query:
66:         patch["urgency"] = "medium"
67:     if "关闭" in query or "关掉" in query:
68:         patch["status"] = "closed"
69:     elif "处理中" in query or "接了" in query:
70:         patch["status"] = "in_progress"
71:     elif "重新打开" in query or "重开" in query:
72:         patch["status"] = "open"
73:     return patch

76: def _kb_content(state: TicketState) -> str:
77:     """发布知识文章的正文：只认员工自己给的引号内容，不拿检索片段硬凑。
78: 
79:     为什么：检索到的政策片段可能是别的主题，拼进正文等于编造内容。
80:     员工没给正文时留空，让 clarify 节点去问一句"正文写什么"。
81:     """

83:     return quoted if len(quoted) >= 15 else ""

128:         ticket_no = find_ticket_no(query) or ""
129:         notify = any(word in query for word in ("通知", "告诉他", "告诉提单人", "发个消息"))
130:         args = {
131:             "ticket_no": ticket_no,
132:             "resolution": _extract_resolution(query),
133:             "notify_reporter": notify,
134:         }
135:         sources = {
136:             "ticket_no": "from_user" if ticket_no else "agent_inferred",
137:             "resolution": "from_user",
138:             "notify_reporter": "from_user",
139:         }
140:         missing = [] if ticket_no else ["ticket_no"]
141:         return args, missing, sources, "关工单"
142: 
143:     if op == "publish_kb_article":
144:         title = extract_quoted_text(query) or (summary if summary and summary != query else (topic + "常见问题"))
145:         args = {
146:             "title": title,
147:             "content": _kb_content(state),
148:             "tags": [topic, "员工提问沉淀"],
149:             "change_note": f"由员工提问整理：{query[:60]}",
150:             "status": "published",
151:         }
152:         sources = {
153:             "title": "agent_inferred",
154:             "content": "from_policy",
155:             "tags": "agent_inferred",
156:             "change_note": "from_context",
157:             "status": "from_user",
158:         }
159:         missing = [] if args["content"].strip() else ["content"]
160:         return args, missing, sources, "发布知识文章"
161: 
162:     if op == "query_ticket":
163:         ticket_no = find_ticket_no(query) or ""
164:         missing = [] if ticket_no else ["ticket_no"]

166:

170:             [],
171:             {"tenant_id": "from_context", "limit": "from_context"},
172:             "列工单",
173:         )
174: 
175:     return {}, ["op"], {}, "未知动作"
176: 
177: 
178: 
179: def _compose_summary(label: str, args: dict) -> str:
180:     """生成确认卡/审批卡的一句话摘要，避免出现"建工单：建工单：…"这种重复前缀。"""
181:     head = str(args.get("title") or args.get("ticket_no") or "").strip()
182:     for prefix in (f"{label}：", f"{label}:", label):
183:         if head.startswith(prefix):
184:             head = head[len(prefix):].strip()
185:             break
186:     return f"{label}：{head}" if head else label
187:

189: 
190: _SUMMARY_PREFIX_PATTERN = re.compile(
191:     r"^\s*(?:员工要求|员工申请|帮我|请|麻烦)?\s*"
192:     r"(?:建|开|提|报|登记|发|关|改|查)?个?(?:工单|单|知识库|文章)?\s*[:：]?\s*"
193: )

196: def _clean_summary(text: str) -> str:
197:     """LLM 常把"建工单："这种动作词带进标题，这里去掉，避免确认卡出现嵌套前缀。"""
198:     cleaned = _SUMMARY_PREFIX_PATTERN.sub("", text or "").strip()
199:     return cleaned or (text or "").strip()
200: 
201: 
202: def not_allowed(state: TicketState, roles: tuple[str, ...]) -> str | None:
203:     """对象级资格：这个人根本不该做这件事时，直接回一句并结束（不进闸门、不进待办）。
204: 
205:     规则本身在 server 的 guard；这里只读 `query_ticket` 带回来的 `can_write` / `denied_reason`。
206:     """
207:     action = state.get("action") or {}
208:     op = str(action.get("op") or "")
209:     if op == "publish_kb_article":
210:         if "kb_owner" not in roles:
211:             return "发布知识库需要 kb_owner 身份，当前身份（" + "、".join(roles) + "）不能发布。"
212:         return None
213:     if op in {"update_ticket", "close_ticket"}:
214:         ticket_no = str((action.get("args") or {}).get("ticket_no") or "")
215:         record = _fact_for(state.get("facts") or [], ticket_no)
216:         if record and record.get("can_write") is False:
217:             return str(record.get("denied_reason") or f"工单 {ticket_no} 你没有权限操作，只有提单人本人或主管能处理。")
218:     return None
219: 
220: 
221: def _fact_for(facts: list[dict[str, Any]], ticket_no: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
222:     """从事实里挑出这张单（证据层已经把权限提示 can_write / notify_reports_self 附上了）。"""
223:     for record in facts or []:
224:         if str(record.get("ticket_no") or "") == ticket_no:
225:             return record
226:     return None
227: 
228: 
229: def plan_action(state: TicketState, runtime: Runtime[UserContext]) -> dict[str, Any]:
230:     """组装动作参数（不写库），按风险选闸门。"""
231:     action = state.get("action") or {}
232:     op = action.get("op") or ""
233:     # server 由 op 推导：发布知识走 kb，其余走 ticket（不交给模型判断，少一个可能填错的字段）
234:     server = "kb" if op == "publish_kb_article" else "ticket"
235:     base_risk = RISK_BY_OP.get(op, "write_reversible")
236: 
237:     args, missing, arg_sources, label = _build_action(state)
238: 
239:     # clarify 里员工补过的字段要生效：用 action.args 覆盖默认推断值，再重算缺口
240:     for key, value in (action.get("args") or {}).items():
241:         if value not in (None, "", [], {}):
242:             args[key] = value
243:             arg_sources[key] = "from_user"
244:     missing = [name for name in missing if args.get(name) in (None, "", [], {})]
245: 
246:     # 证据层已经按"我的上一张 / 那些单"把单号解析出来了，就别再反问用户要单号：
247:     # 唯一命中一张才敢自动填，多张仍然要问（不能替员工猜是哪张）。
248:     facts = state.get("facts") or []
249:     if "ticket_no" in missing and len(facts) == 1 and facts[0].get("ticket_no"):
250:         args["ticket_no"] = facts[0]["ticket_no"]
251:         arg_sources["ticket_no"] = "from_evidence"
252:         missing = [name for name in missing if name != "ticket_no"]
253: 
254:     # "通知提单人"而提单人就是操作者自己 → 没有通知对象，等价于不通知（风险也就不该升级）
255:     if args.get("notify_reporter"):
256:         record = _fact_for(facts, str(args.get("ticket_no") or ""))
257:         if record and record.get("notify_reports_self"):
258:             args["notify_reporter"] = False
259:             arg_sources["notify_reporter"] = "from_context"
260: 
261:     risk = compute_risk(op, args, base_risk)
262:     gate = "clarify" if missing else GATE_BY_RISK.get(risk, "confirm")
263: 
264:     # 动作只有一个真身：args 就是定稿参数（确认卡上改完会直接写回这里）
265:     action_out = {
266:         **action,
267:         "server": server,
268:         "op": op,
269:         "label": label,
270:         "args": args,
271:         "missing": missing,
272:         "sources": arg_sources,
273:     }
274: 
275:     # 对象级资格不通过：直接结束，不弹卡、不排队（"越权"不是"待审批"）
276:     denied = not_allowed({**state, "action": action_out}, tuple(runtime.context.roles))
277:     if denied:
278:         return {"action": action_out, "risk": risk, "gate": gate, "outcome": "denied", "reply": denied}
279: 
280:     update: dict[str, Any] = {"action": action_out, "risk": risk, "gate": gate}
281:     if missing:
282:         update["reply"] = f"还差 {', '.join(missing)}，我先问你一句。"
283:     return update
