<content>
1: """answer 节点：只负责"带出处的回答"，不做任何动作。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义：
4: - 《第1节 RAG Agent》2-Step RAG：先检索、再把资料塞进提示词让模型作答
5: - 《第1节 LangChain核心组件》：答案要能拆出"正文 + 引用"
6: - 《第3节 Think In LangGraph》：一个节点只做一个决定，所以"回答"和"执行动作"分开
7: 
8: 铁律（用户明确要求）：知识型问题没有检索到依据时，不许编，只能认怂并给下一步。
9: """
10: 
11: from __future__ import annotations
12: 
13: from typing import Any
14: 
15: from ..llm import get_chat_model
16: from ..prompts.service_desk import ANSWER_SYSTEM_PROMPT
17: from ..state import TicketState
18: 
19: 
20: def _format_evidence(evidence: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
21:     lines = []
22:     for item in evidence:
23:         # 出处尽量给到「第 X 章 · X.Y 节」，模型才好标 [1][2]
24:         where = " · ".join(
25:             part for part in (item.get("chapter", ""), item.get("section", "")) if part
26:         )
27:         heading = f"（{where}）" if where else ""
28:         lines.append(f"[{item['idx']}] {item['title']}{heading}：{item.get('snippet', '')}")
29:     return "\n".join(lines)
30: 
31: 
32: def _format_facts(facts: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
33:     """把只读查到的工单现状排成清单。事实不标引用编号（它不是制度）。"""
34:     lines = []
35:     for item in facts:
36:         bits = [
37:             f"单号 {item.get('ticket_no', '')}",
38:             f"状态 {item.get('status', '')}",
39:             f"优先级 {item.get('urgency', '')}",
40:         ]
41:         if item.get("title"):
42:             bits.append(f"标题 {item['title']}")
43:         bits.append(f"提单人 {item.get('reporter', '')}")
44:         if item.get("created_at"):
45:             bits.append(f"创建于 {item['created_at']}")
46:         if item.get("resolution"):
47:             bits.append(f"处理结论 {item['resolution']}")
48:         lines.append("- " + "；".join(bits))
49:     return "\n".join(lines)
50: 
51: 
52: def _no_evidence_reply(query: str) -> str:
53:     """没检索到依据：这是产品规则（不许编），不是模型降级。"""
54:     return (
55:         f"知识库里没有检索到和「{query}」直接相关的规定，我不能凭空给结论。\n\n"
56:         "下一步有三个选择：\n"
57:         "1. 换个说法再问一次（比如带上系统名、报错码）；\n"
58:         "2. 我帮你建一张工单，让对应的同事跟进；\n"
59:         "3. 你告诉我大概属于哪个方向（账号权限 / 财务报销 / 考勤假期 / 系统故障），我再查一遍。"
60:     )
61: 
62: 
63: def _llm_answer(state: TicketState) -> str:
64:     """把"制度"和"这几张单的现状"一起交给模型，要求结论标 [编号]、事实不标。"""
65:     evidence = state.get("policy_evidence") or []
66:     facts = state.get("facts") or []
67:     policy_block = _format_evidence(evidence) or "（没有检索到相关制度）"
68:     facts_block = _format_facts(facts) or "（这次没有查到具体工单）"
69:     user_content = (
70:         f"员工的问题：{state.get('query')}\n\n"
71:         f"知识库资料（制度，回答时标引用编号）：\n{policy_block}\n\n"
72:         f"业务事实（只读查到的工单现状，不是制度，不要标引用）：\n{facts_block}\n\n"
73:         "请按系统提示要求作答。"
74:     )
75:     message = get_chat_model().invoke(
76:         [
77:             {"role": "system", "content": ANSWER_SYSTEM_PROMPT},
78:             {"role": "user", "content": user_content},
79:         ]
80:     )
81:     return str(message.content).strip()
82: 
83: 
84: def answer_with_citations(state: TicketState) -> dict[str, Any]:
85:     """生成带引用的回答（知识型链路）。制度与事实都没有时才认怂，不调模型。"""
86:     evidence = state.get("policy_evidence") or []
87:     facts = state.get("facts") or []
88:     if not evidence and not facts:
89:         return {"reply": _no_evidence_reply(state.get("query") or ""), "via": "kb_miss"}
90: 
91:     # 只有事实、没有制度时也算答出来了，但 via 标出来，便于排查"制度为什么没命中"
92:     via = "kb_hit" if evidence else "kb_facts_only"
93:     return {"reply": _llm_answer(state), "via": via}

<content>
1: """知识库切分：把 MinerU 输出的 Markdown 切成「带章节面包屑」的 chunk。
2: 
3: 设计取舍（对应黑马《第 1 节 RAG Agent》2.2.4 / 2.2.5）：
4: 1. 先用 Markdown 结构切（标题层级），因为 MinerU 输出的结构是可信的；
5: 2. 结构切出来的块如果仍然超过 `CHUNK_SIZE`，再对超长块做递归字符切分兜底；
6: 3. 每个 chunk 都把「文档名 · 第 X 章 · X.Y 节」拼回正文开头，
7:    这样 embedding 与 BM25 都能吃到章节信息，回答里的引用也能落到具体小节。
8: 
9: 本模块只负责「切」和「补结构」，不联网、不碰向量库。
10: """
11: from __future__ import annotations
12: 
13: import hashlib
14: import html
15: import re
16: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
17: from pathlib import Path
18: from typing import Any
19: 
20: from langchain_text_splitters import (MarkdownHeaderTextSplitter,
21:                                       RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter)
22: 
23: # —— 切分参数（要调切分就改这里） ——
24: CHUNK_SIZE = 400
25: CHUNK_OVERLAP = 60
26: MIN_CHUNK_CHARS = 30  # 统计用：小于它的 chunk 会单独计数，便于发现问题
27: MIN_PROSE_CHARS = 10  # 去掉标题行后正文短于它 -> 视为「只有标题」，丢弃
28: HEADERS_TO_SPLIT_ON = [("#", "h1"), ("##", "h2"), ("###", "h3")]
29: # 文档头部的免责声明/版权声明：每篇都有、内容几乎一样，进向量库会互相抢召回
30: SKIP_MARKERS = ("技术演示用的虚构示例", "本文档仅供内部使用，请勿外传", "版权所有")
31: RECURSIVE_SEPARATORS = ["\n\n", "\n", "。", "；", "！", "？", "，", " ", ""]
32: 
33: # —— 清洗 MinerU 输出用的正则 ——
34: _HTML_TABLE_RE = re.compile(r"<table\b.*?</table>", re.S | re.I)
35: _TAG_RE = re.compile(r"<[^>]+>")
36: _IMAGE_RE = re.compile(r"!\[[^\]]*\]\([^)]*\)")
37: _HTML_COMMENT_RE = re.compile(r"<!--.*?-->", re.S)
38: _MULTI_BLANK_RE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")
39: _BOLD_LINE_RE = re.compile(r"^\*\*(?P<text>[^*]{2,80})\*\*$")
40: _HEADING_LINE_RE = re.compile(r"^(?P<hashes>#{1,6})\s+(?P<text>.+?)\s*$")
41: _CHAPTER_RE = re.compile(r"^第\s*[一二三四五六七八九十百零\d]{1,4}\s*[章节篇部]")
42: _NUMBERED_RE = re.compile(r"^\d+(?:\.\d+){1,3}[\s、.．]")
43: _DEMO_NOTICE_RE = re.compile(r"^.*技术演示用的虚构示例.*$", re.M)
44: 
45: 
46: def _html_table_to_markdown(block: str) -> str:
47:     """把 MinerU 输出的 <table> 转成 Markdown 表格（否则整张表被当成一坨文字）。"""
48:     rows: list[list[str]] = []
49:     for row_html in re.findall(r"<tr\b.*?</tr>", block, re.S | re.I):
50:         cells = re.findall(r"<t[hd]\b.*?</t[hd]>", row_html, re.S | re.I)
51:         cells = [html.unescape(_TAG_RE.sub(" ", c)).strip() for c in cells]
52:         cells = [re.sub(r"\s+", " ", c) or "-" for c in cells]
53:         if cells:
54:             rows.append(cells)
55:     if not rows:
56:         return ""
57:     width = max(len(r) for r in rows)
58:     rows = [r + ["-"] * (width - len(r)) for r in rows]
59:     lines = ["| " + " | ".join(rows[0]) + " |",
60:              "| " + " | ".join(["---"] * width) + " |"]
61:     lines += ["| " + " | ".join(r) + " |" for r in rows[1:]]
62:     return "\n".join(lines)
63: 
64: 
65: def _promote_bold_headings(markdown: str) -> str:
66:     """MinerU 认不出 Word 自定义标题样式时，会把标题输出成整行 `**加粗**`。
67: 
68:     按「第 N 章 / 4.1 / 其余」做有限度猜测，恢复成标题级别，
69:     否则结构感知切分会把整篇文档当成一个大块。
70:     """
71:     if any(line.lstrip().startswith("#") for line in markdown.split("\n")):
72:         return markdown  # 本身就有标题层级，不动
73: 
74:     out: list[str] = []
75:     seen_h2 = False
76:     for line in markdown.split("\n"):
77:         match = _BOLD_LINE_RE.match(line.strip())
78:         if not match:
79:             out.append(line)
80:             continue
81:         text = match.group("text").strip()
82:         if _CHAPTER_RE.match(text):
83:             out.append(f"## {text}")
84:             seen_h2 = True
85:         elif _NUMBERED_RE.match(text):
86:             out.append(f"### {text}")
87:         elif not seen_h2 and len(text) <= 40:
88:             out.append(f"# {text}")  # 文档大标题
89:         else:
90:             out.append(line)
91:     return "\n".join(out)
92: 
93: 
94: def _restore_heading_levels(markdown: str) -> str:
95:     """MinerU 常把「第 1 章」和「1.1 节」都输出成 `##`，层级就丢了。
96: 
97:     这里按文本形态把层级还原：`第 X 章` -> `##`，`X.Y` / `X.Y.Z` -> `###`，
98:     首个短标题 -> `#`（文档名）。这样切出来的面包屑才带章节。
99:     """
100:     lines = markdown.split("\n")
101:     first_heading_seen = False
102:     out: list[str] = []
103:     for line in lines:
104:         match = _HEADING_LINE_RE.match(line.strip())
105:         if not match:
106:             out.append(line)
107:             continue
108:         text = match.group("text").strip()
109:         if _CHAPTER_RE.match(text):
110:             level = 2
111:         elif _NUMBERED_RE.match(text):
112:             level = 3
113:         elif not first_heading_seen and len(text) <= 40:
114:             level = 1
115:         else:
116:             level = len(match.group("hashes"))
117:         first_heading_seen = True
118:         out.append("#" * level + " " + text)
119:     return "\n".join(out)
120: 
121: 
122: def _strip_demo_notice(markdown: str) -> str:
123:     """去掉「本文档为技术演示用的虚构示例…」声明行，避免它变成一条 chunk。"""
124:     return _DEMO_NOTICE_RE.sub("", markdown)
125: 
126: 
127: def normalize_markdown(markdown: str) -> str:
128:     """MinerU 输出 -> 标准 Markdown：HTML 表格转 Markdown、退化的加粗标题升级成 #。"""
129:     text = (markdown or "").replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n")
130:     text = _HTML_TABLE_RE.sub(lambda m: _html_table_to_markdown(m.group(0)), text)
131:     text = _TAG_RE.sub(" ", text)
132:     text = html.unescape(text)
133:     text = _promote_bold_headings(text)
134:     return text
135: 
136: 
137: def clean_markdown(markdown: str) -> str:
138:     """规范化 + 去声明行 + 还原标题层级 + 去图片/注释 + 压空行。"""
139:     text = normalize_markdown(markdown)
140:     text = _strip_demo_notice(text)
141:     text = _restore_heading_levels(text)
142:     text = _IMAGE_RE.sub("", text)
143:     text = _HTML_COMMENT_RE.sub("", text)
144:     lines = [line.rstrip() for line in text.split("\n")]
145:     return _MULTI_BLANK_RE.sub("\n\n", "\n".join(lines)).strip()
146: 
147: 
148: @dataclass(frozen=True)
149: class Chunk:
150:     """一个可入库的知识片段。"""
151: 
152:     text: str  # 真正写进 Milvus 的文本（含面包屑）
153:     content: str  # 纯正文（不含面包屑，给前端展示/引用用）
154:     metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
155: 
156:     @property
157:     def doc_id(self) -> str:
158:         return str(self.metadata.get("doc_id", ""))
159: 
160:     @property
161:     def chunk_index(self) -> int:
162:         return int(self.metadata.get("chunk_index", 0))
163: 
164:     @property
165:     def citation(self) -> str:
166:         """引用串，例如：星澜科技 员工手册 · 第 4 章 假期管理 · 4.2 年假"""
167:         return make_citation(
168:             str(self.metadata.get("title", "")),
169:             str(self.metadata.get("h1", "")),
170:             str(self.metadata.get("h2", "")),
171:             str(self.metadata.get("h3", "")),
172:         ) or str(self.metadata.get("source_file", ""))
173: 
174: 
175: def make_citation(title: str = "", h1: str = "", h2: str = "", h3: str = "") -> str:
176:     """拼引用串：文档名 · 第 X 章 · X.Y 节（层级去重，空串跳过）。"""
177:     return " · ".join(_dedupe_parts((title, h1, h2, h3)))
178: 
179: 
180: def make_doc_id(tenant_id: str, source_file: str) -> str:
181:     """文档 id：同一租户 + 同一文件名 -> 稳定 id（重复入库不会造重复文档）。"""
182:     digest = hashlib.sha1(f"{tenant_id}:{source_file}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
183:     return f"kb-{digest[:8]}"
184: 
185: 
186: def _prose_length(text: str) -> int:
187:     """去掉 Markdown 标题行后的正文字数（用来判断「是不是只有标题」）。"""
188:     kept = [line for line in text.split("\n") if not line.lstrip().startswith("#")]
189:     return len("".join(kept).strip())
190: 
191: 
192: def _dedupe_parts(pieces: tuple[str, ...]) -> list[str]:
193:     """按顺序去重：完全相同或互相包含（「员工手册」vs「员工手册（模板）」）只留信息量大的那个。"""
194:     parts: list[str] = []
195:     for raw in pieces:
196:         piece = (raw or "").strip()
197:         if not piece:
198:             continue
199:         merged = False
200:         for i, exist in enumerate(parts):
201:             if piece in exist:
202:                 merged = True
203:                 break
204:             if exist in piece:
205:                 parts[i] = piece
206:                 merged = True
207:                 break
208:         if not merged:
209:             parts.append(piece)
210:     return parts
211: 
212: 
213: _SECTION_NO_RE = re.compile(r"^(\d+(\.\d+)*\s|第\s*[一二三四五六七八九十百零\d]{1,4}\s*[章节篇部])")
214: 
215: 
216: def _resolve_title(title: str, text: str, sections: list, stem: str) -> str:
217:     """文档标题：显式入参 > 第一个 # 标题 > 第一个"不像二级标题"的 h2/h1 > 文件名。
218: 
219:     MinerU 解析出来的 PDF 常把文档大标题也标成 `##`，所以不能只看 `# `。
220:     """
221:     doc_title = (title or "").strip()
222:     if not doc_title:
223:         for line in text.split("\n"):
224:             if line.startswith("# "):
225:                 doc_title = line[2:].strip()
226:                 break
227:     if not doc_title:
228:         for section in sections:
229:             meta = section.metadata or {}
230:             for key in ("h1", "h2"):
231:                 candidate = str(meta.get(key, "")).strip()
232:                 if candidate and not _SECTION_NO_RE.match(candidate):
233:                     doc_title = candidate
234:                     break
235:             if doc_title:
236:                 break
237:     if not doc_title:
238:         doc_title = stem
239:     return re.sub(r"^\d{2}[-_\s]*", "", doc_title) or stem  # 去掉 01- 这种序号前缀
240: 
241: 
242: def _breadcrumb(title: str, h1: str, h2: str, h3: str) -> str:
243:     return " · ".join(_dedupe_parts((title, h1, h2, h3)))
244: 
245: 
246: def split_markdown(
247:     markdown: str,
248:     *,
249:     source_file: str = "",
250:     title: str = "",
251:     tenant_id: str = "tenant-demo",
252:     doc_id: str = "",
253:     chunk_size: int = CHUNK_SIZE,
254:     chunk_overlap: int = CHUNK_OVERLAP,
255: ) -> list[Chunk]:
256:     """结构感知切分：Markdown 标题优先，超长再递归字符兜底。"""
257:     text = clean_markdown(markdown)
258:     if not text.strip():
259:         return []
260: 
261:     stem = Path(source_file).stem if source_file else "未命名文档"
262:     header_splitter = MarkdownHeaderTextSplitter(
263:         headers_to_split_on=HEADERS_TO_SPLIT_ON, strip_headers=False
264:     )
265:     recursive = RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter(
266:         chunk_size=chunk_size,
267:         chunk_overlap=chunk_overlap,
268:         separators=RECURSIVE_SEPARATORS,
269:     )
270: 
271:     sections = header_splitter.split_text(text)
272:     doc_title = _resolve_title(title, text, sections, stem)
273:     resolved_doc_id = doc_id or make_doc_id(tenant_id, source_file or stem)
274: 
275:     chunks: list[Chunk] = []
276:     for section in sections:
277:         body = section.page_content.strip()
278:         if not body or _prose_length(body) < MIN_PROSE_CHARS:
279:             continue
280:         if any(marker in body for marker in SKIP_MARKERS):
281:             continue
282:         meta = section.metadata or {}
283:         h1 = str(meta.get("h1", "")).strip()
284:         h2 = str(meta.get("h2", "")).strip()
285:         h3 = str(meta.get("h3", "")).strip()
286:         crumb = _breadcrumb(doc_title, h1, h2, h3)
287:         parts = recursive.split_text(body) if len(body) > chunk_size else [body]
288:         for part in parts:
289:             part = part.strip()
290:             if _prose_length(part) < MIN_PROSE_CHARS:
291:                 continue
292:             chunks.append(
293:                 Chunk(
294:                     text=f"【{crumb}】\n{part}" if crumb else part,
295:                     content=part,
296:                     metadata={
297:                         "doc_id": resolved_doc_id,
298:                         "title": doc_title,
299:                         "h1": h1,
300:                         "h2": h2,
301:                         "h3": h3,
302:                         "chunk_index": len(chunks),
303:                         "source_file": source_file or stem,
304:                         "tenant_id": tenant_id,
305:                     },
306:                 )
307:             )
308:     return chunks
309: 
310: 
311: def chunk_stats(chunks: list[Chunk]) -> dict[str, Any]:
312:     """切分结果体检：块数、字数分布、覆盖了多少个小节。"""
313:     if not chunks:
314:         return {"count": 0, "avg_chars": 0, "max_chars": 0, "min_chars": 0, "sections": 0}
315:     sizes = [len(c.content) for c in chunks]
316:     sections = {(c.metadata.get("h1", ""), c.metadata.get("h2", ""), c.metadata.get("h3", ""))
317:                 for c in chunks}
318:     return {
319:         "count": len(chunks),
320:         "avg_chars": round(sum(sizes) / len(sizes), 1),
321:         "max_chars": max(sizes),
322:         "min_chars": min(sizes),
323:         "sections": len(sections),
324:     }

(End of file - total 324 lines)

1: """ticket-server：工单域的 MCP 服务（5 个工具：2 读 + 3 写）。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义《第3节 MCP》：
4: - Server 用 FastMCP 写，@ticket_mcp.tool() 暴露工具，ticket_mcp.run(transport="stdio") 独立进程
5: - 工具返回**纯文本 JSON**，客户端解析后写回 State
6: - stdio 模式下 server 里禁止 print，否则会污染协议流
7: 
8: 鉴权（本项目拓展，放在 server 里而不是 Agent 里）：
9: - 每个工具都要求 `actor`（身份票据），tenant_id / reporter 一律用它覆盖，不信模型填的值
10: - employee 只能读/改/关**自己提的单**；approver 能处理本租户所有单；跨租户一律拒
11: - 建单去重也在服务端做：同租户同提单人同标题的未关闭单，直接返回原单（deduplicated）
12: 
13: 工具清单（10 个工具里的 8 个）：
14: - query_ticket(actor, ticket_no)                          读
15: - list_tickets(actor, status, limit)                      读
16: - create_ticket(actor, title, description, ...)           写，可撤销 → 确认门
17: - update_ticket(actor, ticket_no, patch)                  写，可撤销 → 确认门
18: - close_ticket(actor, ticket_no, resolution, notify_reporter) 写，notify 时升级审批
19: - save_approval(actor, op, target, status, ...)          写，审批留痕（进闸门 pending / 人点了补最终态）
20: - list_approvals(actor, status, limit)                    读，审批中心的数据源
21: 
22: 为什么审批放服务端：
23: - 审批状态机是"业务事实"，不能只活在 checkpoint blob 里（换掉 Agent 就查不到了）；
24: - 审批中心要按 tenant/status 分页查，只能在库上查。
25: """
26:

28:

34: from typing import Any

45: 
46: ticket_mcp = FastMCP(
47:     "smartdesk-ticket",
48:     instructions="企业服务台工单服务：查询、列单、建单、改单、关单。写操作参数由调用方确认后再提交；权限由服务端按 actor 判定。",
49: )

51: DDL = """
52: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tickets (
53:     id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
54:     ticket_no   TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
55:     tenant_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
56:     title       TEXT NOT NULL,
57:     description TEXT DEFAULT '',
58:     urgency     TEXT DEFAULT 'medium',
59:     category    TEXT DEFAULT '其他',
60:     reporter    TEXT DEFAULT 'agent',
61:     status      TEXT DEFAULT 'open',
62:     resolution  TEXT DEFAULT '',
63:     source      TEXT DEFAULT 'mcp',
64:     notified    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
65:     created_at  TEXT NOT NULL,

88: VALID_STATUS = {"open", "in_progress", "closed"}
89: OPEN_STATUSES = ("open", "in_progress")
90: APPROVAL_STATUSES = {"pending", "approved", "edited", "rejected"}
91: 
92: def _db_path() -> Path:
93:     import os
94: 
95:     return Path(os.environ.get("TICKET_DB_PATH") or DEFAULT_DB)
96: 
97: 
98: def _connect() -> sqlite3.Connection:
99:     path = _db_path()
100:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
101:     conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
102:     conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
103:     return conn
104: 
105: 
106: def _init_db() -> None:
107:     with _connect() as conn:
108:         conn.executescript(DDL)
109:         conn.commit()
110: 
111: 
112: def _now() -> str:
113:     return datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
114: 
115: 
116: def _next_ticket_no(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> str:
117:     day = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
118:     prefix = f"TK-{day}-"
119:     row = conn.execute(
120:         "SELECT ticket_no FROM tickets WHERE ticket_no LIKE ? ORDER BY ticket_no DESC LIMIT 1",
121:         (prefix + "%",),
122:     ).fetchone()
123:     seq = 1
124:     if row:
125:         try:
126:             seq = int(str(row["ticket_no"]).split("-")[-1]) + 1
127:         except ValueError:
128:             seq = 1
129:     return f"{prefix}{seq:04d}"
130: 
131: 
132: def _row_to_dict(row: sqlite3.Row) -> dict[str, Any]:
133:     data = dict(row)
134:     data["notified"] = bool(data.get("notified"))
135:     return data
136:

138: def _fetch(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_no: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
139:     row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM tickets WHERE ticket_no = ?", (ticket_no or "",)).fetchone()
140:     return _row_to_dict(row) if row else None
141:

144:     return guard.deny(

147:         actor={"user_id": actor["user_id"], "roles": list(actor["roles"])},
148:     )

151: @ticket_mcp.tool()
152: def create_ticket(
153:     actor: dict[str, Any],
154:     title: str,
155:     description: str = "",
156:     urgency: str = "medium",
157:     category: str = "其他",
158: ) -> str:
159:     """建一张工单（可撤销动作，调用前应经过提单人确认）。租户与提单人取自 actor。"""
160:     actor_data, denied = guard.require_actor(actor)
161:     if denied:
162:         return denied
163:     title = (title or "").strip()
164:     if not title:
165:         return guard.deny("invalid_argument", "title 不能为空")
166:     if urgency not in {"low", "medium", "high"}:
167:         urgency = "medium"
168: 
169:     _init_db()
170:     with _connect() as conn:
171:         # 服务端去重：同租户 + 同提单人 + 同标题的未关闭单，不再重复开
172:         existing = conn.execute(
173:             """SELECT * FROM tickets
174:                WHERE tenant_id = ? AND reporter = ? AND title = ?
175:                  AND status IN (?, ?)
176:                ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1""",
177:             (actor_data["tenant_id"], actor_data["user_id"], title, *OPEN_STATUSES),
178:         ).fetchone()
179:         if existing:
180:             return json.dumps(
181:                 {"ok": True, "deduplicated": True, "ticket": _row_to_dict(existing)},
182:                 ensure_ascii=False,
183:             )
184: 
185:         for _ in range(5):
186:             ticket_no = _next_ticket_no(conn)
187:             try:
188:                 conn.execute(
189:                     """INSERT INTO tickets
190:                        (ticket_no, tenant_id, title, description, urgency, category,
191:                         reporter, status, source, created_at, updated_at)
192:                        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
193:                     (ticket_no, actor_data["tenant_id"], title, description, urgency, category,
194:                      actor_data["user_id"], "open", "mcp", _now(), _now()),
195:                 )
196:                 break
197:             except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
198:                 continue
199:         else:
200:             return guard.deny("conflict", "生成工单号失败，请重试")
201:         row = _fetch(conn, ticket_no)
202: 
203:     return json.dumps({"ok": True, "ticket": row}, ensure_ascii=False)
204:

206: @ticket_mcp.tool()
207: def query_ticket(actor: dict[str, Any], ticket_no: str) -> str:
208:     """按工单号查一张工单（只读）。只能查自己的单；approver 可查本租户任意单。"""
209:     actor_data, denied = guard.require_actor(actor)
210:     if denied:
211:         return denied
212:     _init_db()
213:     with _connect() as conn:

216:         return guard.deny("not_found", f"工单 {ticket_no} 不存在")
217:     if not guard.can_read_ticket(actor_data, ticket):
218:         return _deny_forbidden(actor_data, ticket_no)
219:     ticket.update(guard.ticket_flags(actor_data, ticket))
220:     return json.dumps({"ok": True, "ticket": ticket}, ensure_ascii=False)

223: @ticket_mcp.tool()
224: def list_tickets(actor: dict[str, Any], status: str = "", limit: int = 5) -> str:
225:     """列工单（只读）。employee 只列自己报的；approver 列本租户全部。"""
226:     actor_data, denied = guard.require_actor(actor)

228:         return denied

244:         ticket = _row_to_dict(row)
245:         ticket.update(guard.ticket_flags(actor_data, ticket))
246:         tickets.append(ticket)
247:     return json.dumps({"ok": True, "count": len(tickets), "tickets": tickets}, ensure_ascii=False)

250: @ticket_mcp.tool()
251: def update_ticket(actor: dict[str, Any], ticket_no: str, patch: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
252:     """改工单字段（可撤销动作）。patch 支持 status/urgency/title/description/category。"""
253:     actor_data, denied = guard.require_actor(actor)
254:     if denied:
255:         return denied
256:     allowed = {"status", "urgency", "title", "description", "category"}
257:     patch = {key: value for key, value in (patch or {}).items() if key in allowed}
258:     if not patch:
259:         return guard.deny("invalid_argument", "没有可更新的字段")
260:     if "status" in patch and patch["status"] not in VALID_STATUS:
261:         return guard.deny("invalid_argument", f"status 只能是 {sorted(VALID_STATUS)}")
262:     if "urgency" in patch and patch["urgency"] not in {"low", "medium", "high"}:
263:         return guard.deny("invalid_argument", "urgency 只能是 low/medium/high")
264: 
265:     _init_db()
266:     with _connect() as conn:
267:         ticket = _fetch(conn, ticket_no)
268:         if not ticket:
269:             return guard.deny("not_found", f"工单 {ticket_no} 不存在")
270:         if not guard.can_write_ticket(actor_data, ticket):
271:             return _deny_forbidden(actor_data, ticket_no)
272:         patch["updated_at"] = _now()
273:         columns = ", ".join(f"{key} = ?" for key in patch)
274:         values = list(patch.values()) + [ticket_no]
275:         conn.execute(f"UPDATE tickets SET {columns} WHERE ticket_no = ?", values)
276:         row = _fetch(conn, ticket_no)
277: 
278:     return json.dumps(
279:         {
280:             "ok": True,
281:             "ticket": row,
282:             "updated": "、".join(k for k in patch if k != "updated_at"),
283:         },
284:         ensure_ascii=False,
285:     )

290:     actor: dict[str, Any],
291:     ticket_no: str,
292:     resolution: str = "",
293:     notify_reporter: bool = False,
294: ) -> str:
295:     """关单（可撤销动作）。notify_reporter=True 会通知提单人，属对外可见动作，需要主管审批。"""
296:     actor_data, denied = guard.require_actor(actor)

303:             return guard.deny("not_found", f"工单 {ticket_no} 不存在")
304:         if not guard.can_write_ticket(actor_data, ticket):

310:             ("closed", resolution or "已解决", 1 if notify_reporter else 0, _now(), ticket_no),
311:         )
312:         row = _fetch(conn, ticket_no)
313: 
314:     return json.dumps({"ok": True, "ticket": row}, ensure_ascii=False)

317: @ticket_mcp.tool()
318: def save_approval(
319:     actor: dict[str, Any],
320:     op: str,
321:     target: str,
322:     risk: str = "read",
323:     status: str = "pending",
324:     summary: str = "",
325:     note: str = "",
326:     approver: str = "",
327: ) -> str:
328:     """记一条审批留痕（审批中心的数据源）。进闸门写 pending，人点了再补最终状态。
329: 
330:     - 同一 tenant + op + target 已有 pending 时直接复用：interrupt 恢复会重放节点，这里必须幂等
331:     - 这是 Agent 侧唯一允许写的"审计"；业务数据仍然只有上面 5 个工具能改
332:     """
333:     actor_data, denied = guard.require_actor(actor)
334:     if denied:
335:         return denied
336:     op = (op or "").strip()
337:     target = (target or "").strip()
338:     if not op or not target:
339:         return guard.deny("invalid_argument", "op 和 target 不能为空")
340:     if status not in APPROVAL_STATUSES:
341:         return guard.deny("invalid_argument", f"status 只能是 {sorted(APPROVAL_STATUSES)}")
342: 
343:     _init_db()
344:     with _connect() as conn:
345:         row = conn.execute(
346:             "SELECT * FROM approvals WHERE tenant_id = ? AND op = ? AND target = ? AND status = 'pending'"
347:             " ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",
348:             (actor_data["tenant_id"], op, target),
349:         ).fetchone()
350: 
351:         if status == "pending":
352:             if row:  # 节点重放：复用已存在的待办，不重复插
353:                 return json.dumps(
354:                     {"ok": True, "existing": True, "approval": dict(row)}, ensure_ascii=False
355:                 )
356:             cur = conn.execute(
357:                 "INSERT INTO approvals (tenant_id, op, target, risk, summary, requester, status, created_at)"
358:                 " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
359:                 (actor_data["tenant_id"], op, target, risk or "read", summary or "",
360:                  actor_data["user_id"], "pending", _now()),
361:             )
362:             approval_id = cur.lastrowid
363:         elif row:
364:             conn.execute(
365:                 "UPDATE approvals SET status = ?, approver = ?, note = ?, decided_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
366:                 (status, approver or "", note or "", _now(), row["id"]),
367:             )
368:             approval_id = row["id"]
369:         else:
370:             cur = conn.execute(
371:                 "INSERT INTO approvals"
372:                 " (tenant_id, op, target, risk, summary, requester, status, approver, note, created_at, decided_at)"
373:                 " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
374:                 (actor_data["tenant_id"], op, target, risk or "read", summary or "",
375:                  actor_data["user_id"], status, approver or "", note or "", _now(), _now()),
376:             )
377:             approval_id = cur.lastrowid
378: 
379: 
380:     return json.dumps({"ok": True, "approval_id": approval_id, "status": status}, ensure_ascii=False)
381:

1: """MCP 客户端：一个 MultiServerMCPClient 管两个 server（工单 / 知识库）。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义《第3节 MCP》：
4: - `MultiServerMCPClient({"ticket": {...}, "kb": {...}})` 一次装配多个 server；
5: - stdio 连接需要 transport/command/args/env/cwd 五项；
6: - 工具是异步的，必须 `await tool.ainvoke(...)`，所以这里用一个同步门面 `call_tool()` 包住。
7: 
8: 工程补充（讲义没讲、但必须有）：传输降级。沙箱/受限环境禁止起子进程时，
9: 自动切到进程内模式（`fastmcp.Client(server.mcp)`）——协议一样、工具实现是同一份代码。
10: """
11: 
12: from __future__ import annotations
13: 
14: import asyncio
15: import importlib
16: import json
17: import logging
18: import os
19: import sys
20: from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
21: from pathlib import Path
22: from typing import Any
23: 
24: from langchain_mcp_adapters.client import MultiServerMCPClient
25: 
26: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
27: 
28: ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
29: SERVERS: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {
30:     "ticket": {
31:         "script": ROOT_DIR / "backend" / "app" / "mcp" / "servers" / "ticket_server.py",
32:         "module": "backend.app.mcp.servers.ticket_server",
33:         "mcp_attr": "ticket_mcp",
34:     },
35:     "kb": {
36:         "script": ROOT_DIR / "backend" / "app" / "mcp" / "servers" / "kb_server.py",
37:         "module": "backend.app.mcp.servers.kb_server",
38:         "mcp_attr": "kb_mcp",
39:     },
40: }
41: 
42: # 工具中文名（CLI 的 tools 子命令与前端展示共用）。
43: # 注意：风险等级不在这里——唯一真相在 nodes/plan_action.py 的 RISK_BY_OP / compute_risk，
44: # 因为风险会随参数升级（close_ticket(notify_reporter=True) 才是不可逆），静态表记不住。
45: TOOL_CATALOG = {
46:     "query_ticket": {"label": "查工单"},
47:     "list_tickets": {"label": "列工单"},
48:     "create_ticket": {"label": "建工单"},
49:     "update_ticket": {"label": "改工单"},
50:     "close_ticket": {"label": "关工单"},
51:     "search_kb": {"label": "检索知识库"},
52:     "publish_kb_article": {"label": "发布知识文章"},
53:     "save_approval": {"label": "审批留痕"},
54:     "list_approvals": {"label": "审批中心"},
55: }
56: 
57: _KB_TOOLS = {"search_kb", "publish_kb_article"}
58: # 治理类工具（审批留痕/查询）：不进 plan_action 的 op 枚举，风险由服务端判，不参与业务风险分级
59: _GOVERNANCE_TOOLS = {"save_approval", "list_approvals"}
60: 
61: _TRANSPORT_MODE: str | None = os.environ.get("MCP_TRANSPORT", "").strip() or None  # None=自动
62: _CLIENT: MultiServerMCPClient | None = None
63: 
64: 
65: def transport_mode() -> str:
66:     return _TRANSPORT_MODE or "auto"
67: 
68: 
69: def transport_info() -> dict[str, Any]:
70:     return {
71:         "mode": transport_mode(),
72:         "python": sys.executable,
73:         "servers": {name: str(cfg["script"]) for name, cfg in SERVERS.items()},
74:         "hint": "stdio=独立进程；memory=进程内调用同一份 server 代码",
75:     }
76: 
77: 
78: def _switch_to_memory(reason: str) -> None:
79:     global _TRANSPORT_MODE, _CLIENT
80:     if _TRANSPORT_MODE == "memory":
81:         return
82:     logger.warning("stdio 不可用（%s），自动切换进程内模式", reason)
83:     _TRANSPORT_MODE = "memory"
84:     _CLIENT = None
85: 
86: 
87: def _connection_spec(server: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
88:     env = {
89:         "PYTHONUTF8": "1",
90:         "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8",
91:         "TICKET_DB_PATH": str(ROOT_DIR / ".data" / "tickets.sqlite"),
92:     }
93:     return {
94:         "transport": "stdio",
95:         "command": sys.executable,
96:         "args": [str(SERVERS[server]["script"])],
97:         "cwd": str(ROOT_DIR),
98:         "env": env,
99:     }
100: 
101: 
102: def get_client() -> MultiServerMCPClient:
103:     """进程内单例：一个客户端装两个 server（讲义里的多 server 装配）。"""
104:     global _CLIENT
105:     if _CLIENT is None:
106:         _CLIENT = MultiServerMCPClient({name: _connection_spec(name) for name in SERVERS})
107:     return _CLIENT
108: 
109: 
110: def _is_permission_error(exc: BaseException) -> bool:
111:     """递归找 PermissionError / WinError 5（anyio 会把它们包进 ExceptionGroup）。"""
112:     seen: set[int] = set()
113:     stack: list[BaseException] = [exc]
114:     while stack:
115:         current = stack.pop()
116:         if id(current) in seen:
117:             continue
118:         seen.add(id(current))
119:         if isinstance(current, PermissionError):
120:             return True
121:         if getattr(current, "winerror", None) == 5:
122:             return True
123:         for attr in ("exceptions", "exceptions_", "__cause__", "__context__"):
124:             value = getattr(current, attr, None)
125:             if isinstance(value, BaseException):
126:                 stack.append(value)
127:             elif isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
128:                 stack.extend(item for item in value if isinstance(item, BaseException))
129:     return False
130: 
131: 
132: def _text_of(item: Any) -> str:
133:     """从单个内容块里取文本（兼容 str / dict 块 / 带 text 属性的对象）。"""
134:     if isinstance(item, str):
135:         return item
136:     if isinstance(item, dict):
137:         return str(item.get("text") or "")
138:     return str(getattr(item, "text", "") or "")
139: 
140: 
141: def _parse_text(result: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
142:     """把 MCP 返回的文本 JSON 解析成 dict（两个传输都返回 {"ok": ...} 文本）。
143: 
144:     兼容三种形状：ToolMessage、裸的 list[{"type": "text", "text": ...}]、以及直接返回的 dict。
145:     不同版本的 langchain-mcp-adapters 会在这三种之间变化，这里统一兜住。
146:     """
147:     if isinstance(result, dict) and "ok" in result:
148:         return result
149:     content = getattr(result, "content", None)
150:     blocks = content if content is not None else result
151:     for block in blocks if isinstance(blocks, (list, tuple)) else [blocks]:
152:         text = _text_of(block)
153:         if not text:
154:             continue
155:         try:
156:             data = json.loads(text)
157:         except json.JSONDecodeError:
158:             return {"ok": False, "error": text}
159:         if isinstance(data, dict):
160:             return data
161:         return {"ok": False, "error": f"工具返回的不是对象：{data!r}"}
162:     for attr in ("data", "structured_content"):
163:         value = getattr(result, attr, None)
164:         if isinstance(value, dict):
165:             return value
166:     return {"ok": False, "error": f"无法解析的工具返回：{result!r}"}
167: 
168: 
169: def _server_module(server: str):
170:     return importlib.import_module(SERVERS[server]["module"])
171: 
172: 
173: def _call_stdio(server: str, name: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
174:     async def run() -> dict[str, Any]:
175:         tools = await get_client().get_tools(server_name=server)
176:         tool = next((item for item in tools if item.name == name), None)
177:         if tool is None:
178:             raise LookupError(f"{server} server 上没有工具 {name}")
179:         return _parse_text(await tool.ainvoke(args))
180: 
181:     return asyncio.run(run())
182: 
183: 
184: def _call_memory(server: str, name: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
185:     from fastmcp import Client
186: 
187:     async def run() -> dict[str, Any]:
188:         mcp_instance = getattr(_server_module(server), SERVERS[server]["mcp_attr"])
189:         async with Client(mcp_instance) as client:
190:             result = await client.call_tool(name, args)
191:         return _parse_text(result)
192: 
193:     return asyncio.run(run())
194: 
195: 
196: def _run_in_thread(factory):
197:     with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
198:         return pool.submit(factory).result()
199: 
200: 
201: def _invoke(fn, server: str, name: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
202:     """在独立线程里跑一次异步调用（节点是同步函数，不能直接 await）。"""
203:     return _run_in_thread(lambda: fn(server, name, payload))
204: 
205: 
206: def call_tool(server: str, name: str, args: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
207:     """同步调用一个 MCP 工具；stdio 不可用时自动降级为进程内模式。"""
208:     if server not in SERVERS:
209:         return {"ok": False, "error": f"未知 server：{server}"}
210:     payload = dict(args or {})
211: 
212:     if _TRANSPORT_MODE != "memory":
213:         try:
214:             return _invoke(_call_stdio, server, name, payload)
215:         except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
216:             if not _is_permission_error(exc):
217:                 return {"ok": False, "error": f"调用 {server}.{name} 失败：{exc}"}
218:             _switch_to_memory(str(exc))
219: 
220:     try:
221:         return _invoke(_call_memory, server, name, payload)
222:     except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
223:         return {"ok": False, "error": f"调用 {server}.{name} 失败：{exc}"}
224: 
225: 
226: def tool_catalog() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
227:     """列出两个 server 上的工具（名/中文名/读写/风险）。
228: 
229:     风险从 `nodes/plan_action.py` 的 RISK_BY_OP 取，全项目只保留这一份风险表。
230:     """
231:     from ..nodes.plan_action import RISK_BY_OP  # 延迟导入，避免 tools ↔ nodes 循环依赖
232: 
233:     items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
234:     for name, meta in TOOL_CATALOG.items():
235:         server = "kb" if name in _KB_TOOLS else "ticket"
236:         risk = "governance" if name in _GOVERNANCE_TOOLS else RISK_BY_OP.get(name, "write_reversible")
237:         items.append({"server": server, "name": name, "risk": risk, **meta})
238:     return items
239: 
240: 
241: def reset() -> None:
242:     """重置客户端（切换传输 / 测试用）。"""
243:     global _CLIENT, _TRANSPORT_MODE
244:     _CLIENT = None

<path>D:\zuixin\scripts\eval_rag.py</path>

<content>
1: """RAG 评测：拿 golden set 跑检索指标，可选跑真实回答统计引用准确率。
2: 
3: 用法：
4:     python -m scripts.eval_rag                  # 只评检索（快，不调回答模型）
5:     python -m scripts.eval_rag --answers 20     # 额外跑 20 条完整链路，统计引用准确率与延迟
6:     python -m scripts.eval_rag --top-k 5        # 改检索条数
7: 
8: golden set 的每条用例长这样（按「文档 + 小节」标注，换 embedding 模型也不用改）：
9:     {"id": "g01", "query": "...", "tenant_id": "tenant-demo",
10:      "expected_source": "01-员工手册.md", "expected_section": "3.1 年假", "must_include": "年假"}
11: 
12: 产出：
13:     docs/eval/report.md（表格 + 汇总数字，可直接抄进简历）
14:     stdout 一行 JSON（方便 CI / 复现）
15: """
16: 
17: from __future__ import annotations
18: 
19: import argparse
20: import json
21: import statistics
22: import sys
23: import time
24: import uuid
25: from pathlib import Path
26: from typing import Any
27: 
28: ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
29: if str(ROOT_DIR) not in sys.path:
30:     sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT_DIR))
31: 
32: from backend.app.agents import knowledge  # noqa: E402
33: 
34: GOLDEN = ROOT_DIR / "docs" / "eval" / "golden_set.jsonl"
35: REPORT = ROOT_DIR / "docs" / "eval" / "report.md"
36: 
37: 
38: def load_cases() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
39:     cases = []
40:     for line in GOLDEN.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
41:         line = line.strip()
42:         if line:
43:             cases.append(json.loads(line))
44:     return cases
45: 
46: 
47: def _where(chapter: str = "", section: str = "") -> str:
48:     return " · ".join(part for part in (chapter, section) if part)
49: 
50: 
51: def _pick(row: dict[str, Any], *names: str) -> str:
52:     """同一含义在不同层叫法不同：向量库命中是 h2/h3，Agent 的 evidence 是 chapter/section。"""
53:     for name in names:
54:         value = row.get(name)
55:         if value:
56:             return str(value)
57:     return ""
58: 
59: 
60: def _hit_label(hit: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
61:     """命中块的可读标签：01-员工手册.md · 3.1 年假"""
62:     chapter = _pick(hit, "h2", "chapter")
63:     section = _pick(hit, "h3", "section")
64:     return f"{_pick(hit, 'source_file') or '?'} · {_where(chapter, section)}".strip()
65: 
66: 
67: def _hit_matches(hit: dict[str, Any], case: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
68:     """命中判定：同一个源文件 + 命中块的章节里出现期望小节（如 3.1 年假）。"""
69:     if _pick(hit, "source_file") != case["expected_source"]:
70:         return False
71:     heading = _pick(hit, "h2", "chapter") + _pick(hit, "h3", "section")
72:     return case["expected_section"] in heading
73: 
74: 
75: def evaluate_retrieval(cases: list[dict[str, Any]], top_k: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
76:     # 预热一次：首次搜索要等 Milvus 把 collection 载入内存，不能算进延迟统计
77:     knowledge.search(cases[0]["query"], k=top_k, tenant_id=cases[0]["tenant_id"])
78: 
79:     rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
80:     for case in cases:
81:         started = time.perf_counter()
82:         try:
83:             hits = knowledge.search(case["query"], k=top_k, tenant_id=case["tenant_id"])
84:         except knowledge.MilvusUnavailable as exc:
85:             print(f"[FATAL] 知识库不可用：{exc}")
86:             raise SystemExit(2)
87:         latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
88:         rank = next((i + 1 for i, hit in enumerate(hits) if _hit_matches(hit, case)), None)
89:         top1_text = (hits[0].get("content", "") + hits[0].get("title", "")) if hits else ""
90:         rows.append(
91:             {
92:                 "id": case["id"],
93:                 "query": case["query"],
94:                 "expected": f"{case['expected_source']} · {case['expected_section']}",
95:                 "got": [_hit_label(hit) for hit in hits],
96:                 "rank": rank,
97:                 "hit1": rank == 1,
98:                 "hitk": rank is not None,
99:                 "keyword_hit": case["must_include"] in top1_text,
100:                 "latency_ms": latency_ms,
101:             }
102:         )
103: 
104:     total = len(rows)
105:     summary = {
106:         "cases": total,
107:         "top_k": top_k,
108:         "recall@1": round(sum(r["hit1"] for r in rows) / total, 4),
109:         "recall@k": round(sum(r["hitk"] for r in rows) / total, 4),
110:         "mrr@k": round(sum((1.0 / r["rank"]) if r["rank"] else 0.0 for r in rows) / total, 4),
111:         "top1_keyword_rate": round(sum(r["keyword_hit"] for r in rows) / total, 4),
112:         "latency_ms_avg": round(statistics.mean(r["latency_ms"] for r in rows), 1),
113:         "latency_ms_p95": round(sorted(r["latency_ms"] for r in rows)[max(0, int(total * 0.95) - 1)], 1),
114:     }
115:     return {"rows": rows, "summary": summary}
116: 
117: 
118: def evaluate_answers(cases: list[dict[str, Any]], limit: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
119:     """跑真实主图（真模型）：引用里是否包含期望文档的期望小节。"""
120:     from backend.app.agents.graph import get_graph
121:     from backend.app.agents.state import UserContext, initial_state
122: 
123:     graph = get_graph()
124:     context = UserContext(user_id="u-1001", tenant_id="tenant-demo", roles=("employee",))
125:     rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
126:     for case in cases[:limit]:
127:         thread_id = f"eval-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
128:         started = time.perf_counter()
129:         payload = initial_state(tenant_id=case["tenant_id"], user_id="u-1001", query=case["query"])
130:         try:
131:             result = graph.invoke(
132:                 payload,
133:                 {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}},
134:                 context=context,
135:             )
136:         except Exception as exc:  # 单条失败不影响整体
137:             rows.append({"id": case["id"], "query": case["query"], "error": str(exc)})
138:             continue
139:         elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started
140:         evidence = result.get("policy_evidence") or []
141:         cited = [_hit_label(item) for item in evidence]
142:         reply = result.get("reply") or ""
143:         rows.append(
144:             {
145:                 "id": case["id"],
146:                 "query": case["query"],
147:                 "expected": f"{case['expected_source']} · {case['expected_section']}",
148:                 "cited": cited,
149:                 "citation_ok": any(_hit_matches(item, case) for item in evidence),
150:                 "has_citation_text": "[1]" in reply,
151:                 "keyword_in_reply": case["must_include"] in reply,
152:                 "outcome": result.get("outcome"),
153:                 "via": result.get("via"),
154:                 "elapsed_s": round(elapsed, 2),
155:             }
156:         )
157: 
158:     ok = [r for r in rows if "error" not in r]
159:     total = max(1, len(ok))
160:     summary = {
161:         "answer_cases": len(rows),
162:         "answer_errors": len(rows) - len(ok),
163:         "citation_accuracy": round(sum(r["citation_ok"] for r in ok) / total, 4),
164:         "citation_mark_rate": round(sum(r["has_citation_text"] for r in ok) / total, 4),
165:         "keyword_in_reply_rate": round(sum(r["keyword_in_reply"] for r in ok) / total, 4),
166:         "elapsed_s_avg": round(statistics.mean(r["elapsed_s"] for r in ok), 2) if ok else 0,
167:         "elapsed_s_p95": round(sorted(r["elapsed_s"] for r in ok)[max(0, int(total * 0.95) - 1)], 2) if ok else 0,
168:     }
169:     return {"rows": rows, "summary": summary}
170: 
171: 
172: def write_report(retrieval: dict[str, Any], answers: dict[str, Any] | None) -> None:
173:     lines: list[str] = []
174:     rs = retrieval["summary"]
175:     lines.append("# 检索与引用评测报告\n")
176:     lines.append(
177:         f"- 用例：`docs/eval/golden_set.jsonl`，共 **{rs['cases']}** 条"
178:         "（标注到「文档 + 小节」，例如 `01-员工手册.md · 3.1 年假`）"
179:     )
180:     lines.append(f"- 检索：Milvus 服务端混合检索（dense + 内置 BM25 + RRFRanker），top_k={rs['top_k']}")
181:     health = knowledge.health()
182:     lines.append(
183:         f"- 环境：向量库 backend={health.get('backend')} · collection={health.get('collection')} · "
184:         f"chunks={health.get('rows')}\n"
185:     )
186:     lines.append(f"## 1. 检索指标（{rs['cases']} 条）\n")
187:     lines.append("| 指标 | 数值 | 含义 |")
188:     lines.append("|---|---|---|")
189:     lines.append(f"| Recall@1 | **{rs['recall@1']:.2%}** | 期望小节排第一的比例 |")
190:     lines.append(f"| Recall@{rs['top_k']} | **{rs['recall@k']:.2%}** | 期望小节进前 {rs['top_k']} 的比例 |")
191:     lines.append(f"| MRR@{rs['top_k']} | **{rs['mrr@k']:.4f}** | 期望小节排名倒数的平均值（越接近 1 越好） |")
192:     lines.append(f"| 首条关键词命中率 | {rs['top1_keyword_rate']:.2%} | 首条检索结果里出现期望关键词 |")
193:     lines.append(f"| 检索延迟 | 平均 {rs['latency_ms_avg']} ms / P95 {rs['latency_ms_p95']} ms | 单次 hybrid_search |")
194:     lines.append("")
195:     if answers:
196:         an = answers["summary"]
197:         lines.append(f"## 2. 端到端回答（前 {an['answer_cases']} 条，真模型）\n")
198:         lines.append("| 指标 | 数值 | 含义 |")
199:         lines.append("|---|---|---|")
200:         lines.append(f"| 引用准确率 | **{an['citation_accuracy']:.2%}** | 引用的资料里含期望小节 |")
201:         lines.append(f"| 引用标记率 | {an['citation_mark_rate']:.2%} | 回答正文带 `[1]` 这类标注 |")
202:         lines.append(f"| 关键词落进回答 | {an['keyword_in_reply_rate']:.2%} | 回答正文出现期望关键词 |")
203:         lines.append(f"| 端到端延迟 | 平均 {an['elapsed_s_avg']} s / P95 {an['elapsed_s_p95']} s | 分类 + 检索 + 生成 |")
204:         lines.append(f"| 失败条数 | {an['answer_errors']} | 抛异常的用例 |")
205:         lines.append("")
206:         lines.append("## 3. 端到端逐条\n")
207:         lines.append("| id | 问句 | 期望 | 引用到的 | 引用命中 | 耗时(s) |")
208:         lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
209:         for row in answers["rows"]:
210:             if "error" in row:
211:                 lines.append(f"| {row['id']} | {row['query']} | - | 失败：{row['error'][:40]} | ✗ | - |")
212:                 continue
213:             cited = "、".join(row["cited"]) or "（无引用）"
214:             lines.append(
215:                 f"| {row['id']} | {row['query']} | {row['expected']} | {cited} | "
216:                 f"{'✓' if row['citation_ok'] else '✗'} | {row['elapsed_s']} |"
217:             )
218:         lines.append("")
219:     lines.append("## 4. 未命中的用例（用于调参）\n")
220:     misses = [r for r in retrieval["rows"] if not r["hitk"]]
221:     if not misses:
222:         lines.append(f"无：全部 {rs['cases']} 条都把期望小节排进了前 {rs['top_k']}。\n")
223:     else:
224:         lines.append("| id | 问句 | 期望 | 实际返回 |")
225:         lines.append("|---|---|---|---|")
226:         for row in misses:
227:             lines.append(f"| {row['id']} | {row['query']} | {row['expected']} | {'、'.join(row['got']) or '空'} |")
228:         lines.append("")
229:     lines.append("## 5. 怎么复现\n")
230:     lines.append("```bash")
231:     lines.append("cd deploy && docker compose up -d        # 起 PG / Milvus / attu")
232:     lines.append(".venv/Scripts/python.exe -m scripts.ingest_docs --md docs/kb/md --to-milvus")
233:     lines.append(".venv/Scripts/python.exe -m scripts.eval_rag              # 检索指标")
234:     lines.append(".venv/Scripts/python.exe -m scripts.eval_rag --answers 20 # 加端到端引用指标")
235:     lines.append("```")
236:     REPORT.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
237: 
238: 
239: def main() -> int:
240:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="RAG 检索/引用评测")
241:     parser.add_argument("--top-k", type=int, default=3, help="检索条数（默认 3）")
242:     parser.add_argument("--answers", type=int, default=0, help="额外跑多少条完整链路（默认 0）")
243:     args = parser.parse_args()
244: 
245:     cases = load_cases()
246:     retrieval = evaluate_retrieval(cases, max(1, args.top_k))
247: 
248: 
249:     answers = evaluate_answers(cases, args.answers) if args.answers > 0 else None
250:     write_report(retrieval, answers)
251: 
252:     payload: dict[str, Any] = {"retrieval": retrieval["summary"]}
253:     if answers:
254:         payload["answers"] = answers["summary"]
255:     print(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False))
256:     print(f"report -> {REPORT}")
257:     return 0

261:     raise SystemExit(main())

<path>D:\zuixin\backend\app\agents\checkpoint.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """Checkpointer：让中断（interrupt）真正"落盘"，进程重启还能恢复。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义：
4: - 《第1节 LangChain核心组件》—— InMemorySaver / SqliteSaver / PostgresSaver
5: - 《第1节 基本概念和入门》—— compile(checkpointer=...) + thread_id
6: - 《第1节 AI私厨》—— 生产化时把 SqliteSaver 换成 PostgresSaver
7: 
8: 当前实现：PostgresSaver 优先，连不上就回退 SqliteSaver（打印一行警告）。
9: 两者对外接口一样，所以 graph.py 一行都不用改 —— 这就是 checkpointer 抽象的价值。
10: """
11: 
12: from __future__ import annotations
13: 
14: import sqlite3
15: from typing import Any
16: 
17: from langgraph.checkpoint.base import BaseCheckpointSaver
18: 
19: from .settings import ensure_data_dir, settings
20: 
21: _bundle: dict[str, Any] = {"saver": None, "conn": None, "backend": ""}
22: 
23: 
24: def _init_postgres() -> None:
25:     """连接 PG 并建表（checkpoints / checkpoint_blobs / checkpoint_writes / migrations）。
26: 
27:     关键点（踩坑记录）：
28:     - 必须 autocommit=True，否则 PostgresSaver 的建表/写入会在事务里挂住；
29:     - prepare_threshold=0 关闭 psycopg 的 prepared statement 缓存，
30:       避免 checkpoint 频繁 DDL/长连接下出现 "prepared statement already exists"；
31:     - row_factory=dict_row 让返回行是 dict（PostgresSaver 内部按下标取值也能用，
32:       但官方 from_conn_string 就是这么设的，保持一致）。
33:     """
34:     import psycopg
35:     from psycopg.rows import dict_row
36: 
37:     from langgraph.checkpoint.postgres import PostgresSaver
38: 
39:     conn = psycopg.connect(
40:         settings.database_url,
41:         autocommit=True,
42:         prepare_threshold=0,
43:         row_factory=dict_row,
44:         connect_timeout=5,
45:     )
46:     saver = PostgresSaver(conn)
47:     saver.setup()
48:     _bundle.update(saver=saver, conn=conn, backend="postgres")
49: 
50: 
51: def _init_sqlite() -> None:
52:     from langgraph.checkpoint.sqlite import SqliteSaver
53: 
54:     ensure_data_dir()
55:     conn = sqlite3.connect(str(settings.db_path), check_same_thread=False)
56:     saver = SqliteSaver(conn)
57:     saver.setup()
58:     _bundle.update(saver=saver, conn=conn, backend="sqlite")
59: 
60: 
61: def get_checkpointer() -> BaseCheckpointSaver:
62:     """懒加载单例：同一进程共用一个连接。"""
63:     if _bundle["saver"] is not None:
64:         return _bundle["saver"]
65: 
66:     if settings.checkpoint_backend == "postgres":
67:         try:
68:             _init_postgres()
69:         except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - 演示项目：连不上就降级
70:             print(
71:                 f"[checkpoint] Postgres 不可用（{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}），"
72:                 f"回退 SQLite: {settings.db_path}",
73:                 flush=True,
74:             )
75:     if _bundle["saver"] is None:
76:         _init_sqlite()
77:     return _bundle["saver"]
78: 
79: 
80: def checkpoint_backend_in_use() -> str:
81:     """给 CLI / 健康检查显示当前真实生效的后端。"""
82:     get_checkpointer()
83:     return _bundle["backend"]
84: 
85: 
86: def close_checkpointer() -> None:
87:     conn = _bundle.get("conn")
88:     if conn is not None:
89:         try:
90:             conn.close()
91:         except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
92:             pass
93:     _bundle.update(saver=None, conn=None, backend="")
94: 
95: 
96: def thread_config(thread_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
97:     """所有 invoke / 恢复 / 查状态都靠这个 thread_id 串起来。"""
98:     return {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}

(End of file - total 98 lines)
</content>

<content>
1: """所有 HTTP 接口（一个文件放完）。
2: 
3: 这一层很薄，只做三件事：解析请求 → 从请求头组出 `UserContext` → 调图或调工具。
4: 业务逻辑一行都不在这里：权限判定在 MCP server，流程编排在 `agents/graph.py`。
5: """
6: 
7: from __future__ import annotations
8: 
9: from typing import Any
10: 
11: from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
12: from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse
13: from langgraph.types import Command
14: 
15: from ..agents.graph import get_graph
16: from ..agents.nodes.action_spec import fields_of, label_of, summary_of
17: from ..agents.state import UserContext, initial_state
18: from ..agents.tools import ticket_tools
19: from .deps import actor_of_context, get_context
20: from .schemas import ChatRequest, ResumeRequest
21: from .streaming import iter_sse
22: 
23: router = APIRouter()
24: 
25: # SSE 必须关掉代理缓冲，否则 token 会被攒着一次性吐出来
26: SSE_HEADERS = {
27:     "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
28:     "Connection": "keep-alive",
29:     "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
30: }
31: 
32: 
33: def _identity(context: UserContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
34:     """把身份塞进 meta 事件，前端用来显示"当前是谁在问"。"""
35:     return {
36:         "user_id": context.user_id,
37:         "tenant_id": context.tenant_id,
38:         "roles": list(context.roles),
39:     }
40: 
41: 
42: def _sse(events) -> StreamingResponse:
43:     return StreamingResponse(events, media_type="text/event-stream", headers=SSE_HEADERS)
44: 
45: 
46: @router.post("/chat/stream", summary="发一句话，流式返回（token / 节点进度 / 暂停）")
47: def chat_stream(
48:     body: ChatRequest, context: UserContext = Depends(get_context)
49: ) -> StreamingResponse:
50:     """知识型：`token` 逐字输出 → `done`（带引用）；动作型：`node`…→ `interrupt`（卡片）。"""
51:     graph = get_graph()
52:     config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": body.thread_id}}
53:     state = initial_state(tenant_id=context.tenant_id, user_id=context.user_id, query=body.message)
54:     events = iter_sse(
55:         graph,
56:         state,
57:         config,
58:         context=context,
59:         thread_id=body.thread_id,
60:         identity=_identity(context),
61:     )
62:     return _sse(events)
63: 
64: 
65: @router.post("/runs/{thread_id}/resume", summary="从暂停点继续（确认 / 审批 / 补参数）")
66: def resume_run(
67:     thread_id: str,
68:     body: ResumeRequest,
69:     context: UserContext = Depends(get_context),
70: ) -> StreamingResponse:
71:     """跨进程恢复的关键：只靠 `thread_id` 找回 checkpoint，不需要把 State 传回来。
72: 
73:     谁在 resume 决定"能不能点通过"：审批门会按**这次请求的请求头身份**再判一次权限，
74:     所以员工自己点通过只会继续挂起，主管用同一个 thread_id 来 resume 才生效。
75:     """
76:     graph = get_graph()
77:     config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}
78:     snapshot = graph.get_state(config)
79: 
80:     if not snapshot.values:
81:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"没有这个会话：{thread_id}")
82:     stored_tenant = snapshot.values.get("tenant_id")
83:     if stored_tenant and stored_tenant != context.tenant_id:
84:         raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="thread 不属于这个租户")
85:     if not snapshot.interrupts:
86:         raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="这个会话没有停在闸门上，不需要 resume")
87: 
88:     events = iter_sse(
89:         graph,
90:         Command(resume=body.to_resume_payload()),
91:         config,
92:         context=context,
93:         thread_id=thread_id,
94:         identity=_identity(context),
95:     )
96:     return _sse(events)
97: 
98: 
99: @router.get("/runs/{thread_id}", summary="会话当前快照（停在哪、状态、草稿卡片）")
100: def read_run(thread_id: str, context: UserContext = Depends(get_context)) -> dict[str, Any]:
101:     """页面刷新后靠这个接口把"审批卡片"重新画出来。"""
102:     graph = get_graph()
103:     config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}
104:     snapshot = graph.get_state(config)
105:     if not snapshot.values:
106:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"没有这个会话：{thread_id}")
107: 
108:     values = dict(snapshot.values or {})
109:     if values.get("tenant_id") and values["tenant_id"] != context.tenant_id:
110:         raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="thread 不属于这个租户")
111: 
112:     interrupts = [getattr(item, "value", None) for item in (snapshot.interrupts or ())]
113:     action = values.get("action") or {}
114:     return {
115:         "thread_id": thread_id,
116:         "next": list(snapshot.next or ()),
117:         "paused": bool(interrupts),
118:         "interrupt": interrupts[0] if interrupts else None,
119:         "outcome": values.get("outcome"),
120:         "intent": values.get("intent"),
121:         "gate": values.get("gate"),
122:         "risk": values.get("risk"),
123:         "query": values.get("query"),
124:         "reply": values.get("reply"),
125:         "citations": [
126:             hit.get("citation") or hit.get("title") for hit in (values.get("policy_evidence") or [])
127:         ],
128:         "view": {
129:             "op": action.get("op"),
130:             "label": label_of(action),
131:             "summary": summary_of(action),
132:             "fields": fields_of(action),
133:             "risk": values.get("risk"),
134:         }
135:         if action
136:         else None,
137:         "approval": values.get("approval"),
138:         "ticket_no": _ticket_no(values.get("result")),
139:         "via": values.get("via"),
140:     }
141: 
142: 
143: def _ticket_no(result: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str | None:
144:     """单号从工具返回值现取，State 里不再存第二份。"""
145:     data = result or {}
146:     ticket = data.get("ticket") if isinstance(data.get("ticket"), dict) else {}
147:     return ticket.get("ticket_no") or data.get("ticket_no") or None
148: 
149: 
150: @router.get("/graph/mermaid", summary="主图 Mermaid（和 CLI 的 graph 子命令同源）")
151: def graph_mermaid() -> dict[str, str]:
152:     return {"mermaid": get_graph().get_graph().draw_mermaid()}
153: 
154: 
155: @router.get("/tickets", summary="工单列表（员工只看自己报的；主管看全租户）")
156: def list_tickets(
157:     status: str = Query(default="", description="open / in_progress / closed"),
158:     limit: int = Query(default=20, ge=1, le=50),
159:     context: UserContext = Depends(get_context),
160: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
161:     """权限在 MCP server 里判（`list_tickets` 内部按 actor 过滤），这里不重复实现。"""
162:     return ticket_tools.list_tickets(actor_of_context(context), status=status, limit=limit)
163: 
164: 
165: @router.get("/approvals", summary="审批中心（谁申请、什么事、批没批）")
166: def list_approvals(
167:     status: str = Query(default="", description="pending / approved / edited / rejected"),
168:     limit: int = Query(default=20, ge=1, le=100),
169:     context: UserContext = Depends(get_context),
170: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
171:     """权限同样在 server 侧：非特权角色只能看到自己发起的审批。"""
172:     return ticket_tools.list_approvals(actor_of_context(context), status=status, limit=limit)

1: <script setup lang="ts">
2: import MarkdownIt from 'markdown-it'
3: import { computed, nextTick, onMounted, ref } from 'vue'
4: import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
5: 
6: import { api } from '@/api/client'
7: import { chatStream, resumeStream } from '@/api/sse'
8: import InterruptCard from '@/components/InterruptCard.vue'
9: import { useIdentity } from '@/stores/identity'
10: import type { ChatItem, InterruptPayload, RunDetail } from '@/types'
11: 
12: const md = new MarkdownIt({ breaks: true, linkify: true })
13: const identity = useIdentity()
14: 
15: const messages = ref<ChatItem[]>([])
16: const inputText = ref('')
17: const threadId = ref('')
18: const running = ref(false)
19: const nodes = ref<Array<{ node: string; info: string; ms?: number }>>([])
20: const interrupt = ref<InterruptPayload | null>(null)
21: const detail = ref<RunDetail | null>(null)
22: const controller = ref<AbortController | null>(null)
23: const listRef = ref<HTMLElement | null>(null)
24: const lastTokens = ref<{ in: number; out: number } | null>(null)
25: 
26: const NODE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
27:   classify_request: '① 理解',
28:   gather_evidence: '② 取证据',
29:   answer_with_citations: '③ 作答',
30:   plan_action: '④ 计划',
31:   ask_missing_info: '↩ 反问（clarify）',
32:   confirm_action: '✋ 确认（confirm）',
33:   approve_action: '🔐 审批（approve）',
34:   execute_action: '⚙ 执行',
35:   compose_reply: '⑤ 回复',
36: }
37: 
38: function newNodeThread() {
39:   threadId.value = `web-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace(/[-:T]/g, '')}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`
40: }
41: 
42: function scrollToBottom() {
43:   void nextTick(() => {
44:     if (listRef.value) listRef.value.scrollTop = listRef.value.scrollHeight
45:   })
46: }
47: 
48: function render(text: string): string {
49:   return md.render(text || '')
50: }
51: 
52: function nodeInfo(node: string, data: Record<string, any>): string {
53:   const pieces: string[] = []
54:   if (data.intent) pieces.push(`intent=${data.intent}`)
55:   if (data.gate) pieces.push(`gate=${data.gate}`)
56:   if (data.risk) pieces.push(`risk=${data.risk}`)
57:   if (typeof data.evidence_count === 'number') pieces.push(`证据 ${data.evidence_count}`)
58:   if (typeof data.facts_count === 'number' && data.facts_count > 0) pieces.push(`工单事实 ${data.facts_count}`)
59:   if (data.ticket_no) pieces.push(data.ticket_no)
60:   if (data.via) pieces.push(`via=${data.via}`)
61:   if (data.outcome) pieces.push(data.outcome)
62:   return pieces.join(' · ')
63: }
64: 
65: function assistantIndex(): number {
66:   for (let i = messages.value.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
67:     if (messages.value[i].role === 'assistant' && messages.value[i].streaming) return i
68:   }
69:   return -1
70: }
71: 
72: function handleEvent(event: string, data: any) {
73:   if (event === 'node') {
74:     // 服务端发的是扁平对象：{ node: "classify_request", ms: 1234, intent: "..." }
75:     const payload = (data ?? {}) as Record<string, any>
76:     const node = String(payload.node ?? '')
77:     if (!node) return
78:     nodes.value = [...nodes.value, { node, info: nodeInfo(node, payload), ms: payload.ms }]
79:     const index = assistantIndex()
80:     if (index >= 0 && payload.reply && !messages.value[index].text) {
81:       messages.value[index].text = String(payload.reply)
82:     }
83:     return
84:   }
85:   if (event === 'token') {
86:     const index = assistantIndex()
87:     if (index >= 0) messages.value[index].text += String(data?.text ?? '')
88:     scrollToBottom()
89:     return
90:   }
91:   if (event === 'interrupt') {
92:     const payload = (data?.payloads?.[0] ?? null) as InterruptPayload | null
93:     if (data?.tokens) lastTokens.value = data.tokens
94:     if (payload) {
95:       interrupt.value = payload
96:       const index = assistantIndex()
97:       if (index >= 0 && !messages.value[index].text) {
98:         messages.value[index].text = payload.title || '需要你处理一下。'
99:       }
100:     }
101:     return
102:   }
103:   if (event === 'done') {
104:     const index = assistantIndex()
105:     const tokens = data?.tokens as ChatItem['tokens'] | undefined
106:     if (tokens) lastTokens.value = tokens
107:     if (index >= 0) {
108:       messages.value[index].text = String(data?.reply ?? messages.value[index].text)
109:       messages.value[index].citations = (data?.citations ?? []) as ChatItem['citations']
110:       messages.value[index].tokens = tokens
111:       messages.value[index].streaming = false
112:     }
113:     return
114:   }
115:   if (event === 'error') {
116:     ElMessage.error(`出错了：${data?.message ?? JSON.stringify(data)}`)
117:     const index = assistantIndex()
118:     if (index >= 0) messages.value[index].streaming = false
119:   }
120: }
121: 
122: async function refreshDetail() {
123:   if (!threadId.value) return
124:   try {
125:     detail.value = await api.run(threadId.value)
126:     interrupt.value = detail.value.interrupt
127:   } catch {
128:     detail.value = null
129:   }
130: }
131: 
132: async function consume(stream: Promise<void>) {
133:   running.value = true
134:   try {
135:     await stream
136:   } catch (error) {
137:     ElMessage.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error))
138:     const index = assistantIndex()
139:     if (index >= 0) messages.value[index].streaming = false
140:   } finally {
141:     running.value = false
142:     controller.value = null
143:     await refreshDetail()
144:     scrollToBottom()
145:   }
146: }
147: 
148: async function send() {
149:   const text = inputText.value.trim()
150:   if (!text || running.value) return
151:   if (!threadId.value) newNodeThread()
152: 
153:   messages.value = [...messages.value, { role: 'user', text, citations: [] }]
154:   messages.value = [...messages.value, { role: 'assistant', text: '', citations: [], streaming: true }]
155:   inputText.value = ''
156:   nodes.value = []
157:   interrupt.value = null
158:   lastTokens.value = null
159:   scrollToBottom()
160: 
161:   controller.value = new AbortController()
162:   const currentThread = threadId.value
163:   await consume(
164:     chatStream(text, currentThread, identity.headers, {
165:       signal: controller.value.signal,
166:       onEvent: (event) => handleEvent(event.event, event.data),
167:     }),
168:   )
169: }
170: 
171: async function onGateSubmit(body: Record<string, unknown>) {
172:   if (running.value || !threadId.value) return
173:   messages.value = [
174:     ...messages.value,
175:     { role: 'assistant', text: '（继续处理…）', citations: [], streaming: true },
176:   ]
177:   running.value = true
178:   interrupt.value = null
179:   controller.value = new AbortController()
180:   await consume(
181:     resumeStream(threadId.value, body, identity.headers, {
182:       signal: controller.value.signal,
183:       onEvent: (event) => handleEvent(event.event, event.data),
184:     }),
185:   )
186: }
187: 
188: function stop() {
189:   controller.value?.abort()
190:   const index = assistantIndex()
191:   if (index >= 0) messages.value[index].streaming = false
192:   running.value = false
193: }
194: 
195: function newSession() {
196:   newNodeThread()
197:   messages.value = []
198:   nodes.value = []
199:   interrupt.value = null
200:   detail.value = null
201: }
202: 
203: const statusTag = computed(() => {
204:   const run = detail.value
205:   if (!run) return { type: 'info' as const, text: '空闲' }
206:   if (run.paused) return { type: 'warning' as const, text: run.interrupt ? `等待中 · ${run.interrupt.type}` : '等待中' }
207:   if (run.outcome === 'denied') return { type: 'danger' as const, text: '无权执行' }
208:   if (run.outcome === 'rejected') return { type: 'danger' as const, text: '已拒绝' }
209:   if (run.outcome === 'failed') return { type: 'danger' as const, text: '失败' }
210:   return { type: 'success' as const, text: '已完成' }
211: })

1: <script setup lang="ts">
2: import { computed, ref, watch } from 'vue'
3: 
4: import type { Citation, ActionField, InterruptPayload } from '@/types'
5: 
6: const props = defineProps<{ payload: InterruptPayload; disabled?: boolean }>()
7: const emit = defineEmits<{ submit: [body: Record<string, unknown>] }>()
8: 
9: const GATE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
10:   clarify: 'AI 需要补一个参数',
11:   confirm: '请你确认这张卡片',
12:   approve: '需要审批',
13: }
14: 
15: const TYPE_TAG: Record<string, 'warning' | 'primary' | 'danger'> = {
16:   clarify: 'warning',
17:   confirm: 'primary',
18:   approve: 'danger',
19: }
20: 
21: /** 每个字段一个可编辑值（数组用 JSON 字符串编辑，避免做复杂的子表单）。 */
22: const editable = ref<Record<string, string>>({})
23: const answers = ref<Record<string, string>>({})
24: const rejectMessage = ref('')
25: 
26: function toStringValue(value: unknown): string {
27:   if (value === null || value === undefined) return ''
28:   if (typeof value === 'string') return value
29:   if (typeof value === 'object') return JSON.stringify(value, null, 0)
30:   return String(value)
31: }
32: 
33: watch(
34:   () => props.payload,
35:   (payload) => {
36:     const next: Record<string, string> = {}
37:     for (const field of payload.fields ?? []) next[field.name] = toStringValue(field.value)
38:     editable.value = next
39: 
40:     const answerDraft: Record<string, string> = {}
41:     for (const name of payload.missing ?? []) answerDraft[name] = ''
42:     answers.value = answerDraft
43:     rejectMessage.value = ''
44:   },
45:   { immediate: true, deep: true },
46: )
47: 
48: const fields = computed<ActionField[]>(() => props.payload.fields ?? [])
49: const citations = computed<Citation[]>(() => props.payload.citations ?? [])
50: const options = computed<string[]>(() => props.payload.options ?? [])
51: 
52: /** 只把"改过的字段"放进 patch；一个都没改就是单纯的 approve。 */
53: const patch = computed<Record<string, unknown>>(() => {
54:   const result: Record<string, unknown> = {}
55:   for (const field of fields.value) {
56:     const now = editable.value[field.name] ?? ''
57:     const before = toStringValue(field.value)
58:     if (now === before) continue
59:     try {
60:       result[field.name] = JSON.parse(now)
61:     } catch {
62:       result[field.name] = now
63:     }
64:   }
65:   return result
66: })
67: 
68: const changed = computed(() => Object.keys(patch.value).length > 0)
69: 
70: function pickOption(option: string) {
71:   const first = Object.keys(answers.value)[0] ?? 'ticket_no'
72:   answers.value = { ...answers.value, [first]: option }
73: }
74: 
75: function approve() {
76:   emit('submit', {
77:     decision: changed.value ? { type: 'edit', patch: patch.value } : { type: 'approve' },
78:   })
79: }
80: 
81: function reject() {
82:   emit('submit', { decision: { type: 'reject', message: rejectMessage.value } })
83: }
84: 
85: function sendAnswers() {
86:   emit('submit', { answers: { ...answers.value } })
87: }
88: </script>

140:           </template>

147:           {{ citation }}

[assistant]
<acp tokens="4.3K" type="text" time="2026-11-06 18:49:11">m00066</acp>
I have thoroughly inspected the entire **SmartDesk AI** codebase (`backend/`, `frontend/`, `scripts/`, `docs/`, `deploy/`). Below is an objective evaluation of how this project stacks up for an **internship resume**, followed by how to write it on your resume and how to prepare for interviews.

---

### 一、客观评价：作为实习简历项目，它的含金量如何？

**总体打分：8.5 / 10（在本科/硕士找 AI 应用开发、Agent 工程、后端开发实习中属于「中上偏优秀」水平）**

你已经在 [讲解卡.md](<docs/讲解卡.md>) 里整理了非常清晰的答辩框架。脱离背诵视角，站在面试官（一线工程师 / Tech Lead）的角度看，这个项目有几个非常扎实的亮点，也有几处一眼能看出来的「课程拓展痕迹」和代码细节瑕疵。

#### 1. 核心加分项（真正能打动面试官的地方）
* **没有停留在「调包跑通 Demo」，而是有明确的工程边界与安全意识**
  绝大多数学生简历上的 Agent 项目都是 `create_react_agent` 一把梭，工具随便调。你这个项目最大的亮点是**把「模型不确定性」关在笼子里**：
  * **状态与上下文分离**（[state.py](<backend/app/agents/state.py#L29-L90>)）：`UserContext`（`frozen=True`）只读且不进 Checkpoint，通过 `actor_of(runtime)` 生成可信身份票据传给 MCP Server，防止 Prompt 注入篡改 `tenant_id` / `user_id`。
  * **风险由规则表+参数动态升级决定，不靠 LLM 猜**（[action_spec.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/action_spec.py#L12-L35>)）：`RISK_BY_OP` + `compute_risk`（例如 `close_ticket` 默认可撤销，但勾上 `notify_reporter=True` 会对外发通知，动态升级为 `write_irreversible`）。
  * **双层权限与三道人工闸门**（[human_gate.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/human_gate.py#L159-L296>) & [guard.py](<backend/app/mcp/servers/guard.py#L28-L96>)）：`clarify`（缺参反问）、`confirm`（提单人确认并支持改参后**重算风险升级审批**）、`approve`（发起与批准分离、跨进程按 `thread_id` 恢复时二次鉴权、4-eyes 自审标记 `self_approved`），外加 [execute.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/execute.py#L86-L92>) 和 MCP Server 侧的最终兜底。
* **真正在解决 LangGraph 落地时的工程痛点**
  * **Interrupt 重放幂等**（[human_gate.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/human_gate.py#L166>) & [ticket_server.py](<backend/app/mcp/servers/ticket_server.py#L345-L355>)）：意识到 LangGraph `interrupt()` 恢复时会从节点开头重跑，因此在 `save_approval` 里做了 `pending` 状态幂等复用，且从落库记录里取回原始 `requester`，避免恢复时把审批人覆盖成发起人。
  * **MCP 传输层降级与多形态解析兼容**（[mcp_client.py](<backend/app/agents/tools/mcp_client.py#L110-L224>)）：`MultiServerMCPClient`（stdio 独立子进程）遇沙箱权限错误自动降级为 `fastmcp.Client` 进程内调用；兼容不同版本 `langchain-mcp-adapters` 返回形状。
* **RAG 链路完整且有可复现的量化评测指标**
  * **结构感知切分 + 面包屑注入**（[splitter.py](<backend/app/agents/rag/splitter.py#L46-L308>)）：处理了 MinerU 解析 PDF 时的 HTML 表格转 Markdown、退化粗体标题还原层级、去免责声明噪音，并把 `【文档 · 章 · 节】` 拼进 `chunk.text` 让稠密向量和 BM25 同时吃到章节语义。
  * **Milvus 原生双路召回 + 服务端 RRF**（[vector_store.py](<backend/app/agents/rag/vector_store.py#L86-L281>)）：`COSINE` 稠密向量 + Milvus 内置中文 `BM25` Function + `RRFRanker(k=60)` + `tenant_id` 标量过滤 + `_chunk_pk` SHA1 幂等覆盖。
  * **有 Golden Set 和评测脚本**（[eval_rag.py](<scripts/eval_rag.py>) & [report.md](<docs/eval/report.md>)）：51 条测试集，Recall@1 82.35%、Recall@3 96.08%、MRR@3 0.8922，端到端引用准确率 100%。简历上有真实可复现的数字非常加分。
* **全栈闭环交付**
  * 后端 FastAPI 双流式 SSE（`stream_mode=["updates", "messages"]`，只过滤转发 `answer_with_citations` 节点的 token）+ 前端 Vue3 实时节点耗时时间轴、动态可编辑确认/审批卡片（[ChatView.vue](<frontend/src/views/ChatView.vue>)、[InterruptCard.vue](<frontend/src/components/InterruptCard.vue>)）。

---

#### 2. 客观短板与面试官可能挑刺的地方（务必提前准备或顺手修掉）

1. **源码注释里有明显的「黑马讲义」痕迹（建议面试前清理）**
   * 在 [graph.py](<backend/app/agents/graph.py#L15>)、[state.py](<backend/app/agents/state.py#L3-L6>)、[human_gate.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/human_gate.py#L3-L6>)、[execute.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/execute.py#L3-L6>)、[vector_store.py](<backend/app/agents/rag/vector_store.py#L3>) 等十几个文件的 docstring 里都有 `对应黑马讲义：《第X节 ...》`。
   * **建议**：虽然你在黑马课程基础上做了大量深度拓展（MCP 双端鉴权、三级风险闸门、MinerU 清洗、评测集等），但如果把仓库链接放上简历，面试官点开看到「对应黑马讲义」会直接贴上「培训班课程项目」标签，大幅折损你的原创分。**把注释里的「对应黑马讲义…」全部改成纯工程设计说明**。
2. **`plan_action.py` 的参数抽取是规则/正则硬编码，而非 LLM 提取**
   * 在 [plan_action.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/plan_action.py#L6>) 的 docstring 里写着 `with_structured_output：模型负责补参数`，但实际代码（[plan_action.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/plan_action.py#L49-L175>)）里并没有调 LLM，而是复用了 `classify` 阶段的 `summary/urgency/topic`，外加正则（`TICKET_NO_PATTERN`、`_extract_resolution`、`_extract_patch` 关键词匹配 `"紧急" in query`）。
   * **面试应对**：不要吹成「在 `plan_action` 节点用大模型做复杂槽位抽取」；实话实说：**「为了降低单次请求的 LLM 调用延迟与 Token 成本，我在 `classify_request` 阶段一次结构化调用拿到意图、摘要、指代类型，而在 `plan_action` 阶段用确定性规则 + 正则校验单号与参数来源标记（`from_user` / `agent_inferred`），保证工单号绝不被模型幻觉编造，同时把端到端耗时控制在 3 秒左右。」** 这样反而成了工程权衡的亮点。
3. **两处值得顺手修掉的小 Bug（代码细节审查）**
   * **前端不可编辑字段未禁用输入框**：后端 [action_spec.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/action_spec.py#L65-L96>) 专门定义了 `EDITABLE_BLOCK = {"reporter", "tenant_id"}` 并返回了 `editable: bool` 字段；但前端 [InterruptCard.vue](<frontend/src/components/InterruptCard.vue#L134>) 的 `<el-input v-model="editable[row.name]" size="small" />` 忘了绑定 `:disabled="!row.editable"`，导致用户在前端卡片上依然能改 `reporter`（虽然最后到 MCP Server 会被 `actor` 覆盖掉，但前后端契约没对齐）。
   * **确认门通过后升审批门的留痕覆盖问题**：当用户在 `confirm_action` 把 `notify_reporter` 改成 `True` 点通过时，[human_gate.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/human_gate.py#L191-L198>) 先写了一条 `status="edited"` 的审批记录，随后路由跳进 `approve_action`（[human_gate.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/human_gate.py#L233>)）又写了一条 `status="pending"`。因为 `save_approval`（[ticket_server.py](<backend/app/mcp/servers/ticket_server.py#L345-L361>)）只查 `status='pending'`，所以会产生两条记录（一条确认门的 `edited`，一条审批门的 `pending`）。如果面试官细问审计表设计，可以解释为「确认门与审批门各留一条流水」。
4. **同步阻塞与数据库连接池**
   * FastAPI 路由和 LangGraph 节点目前是同步函数，MCP 异步客户端通过 `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)` + `asyncio.run()` 包装（[mcp_client.py](<backend/app/agents/tools/mcp_client.py#L181-L203>)），每次工具调用都会起一次 stdio 子进程会话；`checkpoint.py` 单连接共用。这在实习项目/单机演示中完全够用，但如果面试官问「高并发下怎么优化」，你要能主动答出：**改用 `AsyncPostgresSaver` + `graph.astream` + 持久化的 MCP Session 连接池**。

---

### 二、简历怎么写？（按不同岗位方向提供可直接复制的模板）

在简历上写这个项目时，**切忌写成「用 LangGraph 做了一个客服问答和提工单机器人」**，一定要突出**「状态机编排 + 动态风险分级与跨进程 HITL 审批 + 双路混合检索 RAG 评测 + MCP 零信任工具鉴权」**。

#### 模板 A：投递【AI Agent 应用开发 / 大模型应用工程】实习（最推荐，详略得当）

> **SmartDesk AI —— 基于 LangGraph 与 MCP 的企业级 IT 服务台智能体系统**
> **技术栈**：Python, LangGraph, LangChain, FastMCP, Milvus, PostgreSQL, FastAPI (SSE), Vue 3
> **项目简介**：面向企业内部 IT 服务台场景，构建集「制度规范检索问答」与「工单/知识库受控操作」于一体的多租户 Agent 系统，解决传统 Agent 工具调用不可控、缺乏跨角色审批及制度问答幻觉问题。
> **核心工作**：
> - **状态机编排与跨进程 HITL（Human-in-the-Loop）**：基于 LangGraph 构建 9 节点有向图工作流，严格分离可变图状态（`TicketState`）与只读调用上下文（`UserContext`）；设计「只读直通 / 可撤销确认 / 不可逆审批」三级风险闸门，支持基于参数变更（如关单勾选外部通知）动态重算风险并升级审批流。
> - **跨进程中断恢复与幂等审计**：基于 `PostgresSaver`（支持 SQLite 自动降级）与 `interrupt()` 实现「员工发起、主管异地按 `thread_id` 恢复审批」的跨进程协作；针对节点重放机制设计 `pending` 状态幂等留痕与恢复期二次 RBAC 鉴权，保障 4-Eyes 审计合规。
> - **MCP 工具解耦与服务端零信任鉴权**：基于 FastMCP 将工单与知识库操作封装为独立 MCP Server，客户端支持 `stdio` 子进程与进程内 `memory` 模式自动降级；由 Runtime Context 注入可信身份票据（`actor`）并在 Server 侧完成租户隔离、对象级读写校验与未结工单去重，防御提示词注入越权。
> - **结构感知混合检索 RAG 与量化评测**：基于 MinerU 解析 PDF 制度文档，实现 HTML 表格转置、标题层级还原与「文档·章·节」面包屑注入；采用 Milvus 稠密向量（Cosine）+ 服务端内置中文 BM25 双路召回与 `RRFRanker(k=60)` 融合；构建 51 条章节级 Golden Set 评测基准，**Recall@1 达 82.35%、Recall@3 达 96.08%、MRR@3 达 0.8922，端到端引用准确率 100%**（P95 检索延迟 361ms）。
> - **双流式 SSE 与全栈交互落地**：基于 FastAPI 封装 `updates`（节点耗时与状态增量）与 `messages`（仅过滤转发回答节点 Token）双模式 SSE 流协议，配合 Vue 3 实现实时节点追踪时间轴与可编辑中断确认卡片。

#### 模板 B：精简版（适合一页纸简历空间紧张，控制在 4 行内）

> **SmartDesk AI —— 企业级 IT 服务台 Agent 系统（LangGraph / MCP / Milvus / FastAPI）**
> - **可控工作流与跨进程审批**：基于 LangGraph 编排 9 节点状态机，分离只读 `UserContext` 与 `TicketState`；通过规则表与参数动态计算三级动作风险，结合 `PostgresSaver` 与 `interrupt` 实现「确认改参重算风险」及「员工发起、主管跨进程恢复审批」的 HITL 闭环，并保证节点重放时审计落库幂等。
> - **MCP 零信任工具层**：基于 FastMCP 构建工单与知识库 Server（支持 stdio/进程内自动降级），通过 Runtime 注入可信 `actor` 票据在服务端强制执行多租户隔离、RBAC 校验与建单幂等去重，阻断 Prompt 注入越权。
> - **混合检索 RAG 与评测体系**：针对 MinerU 解析产物实现结构感知切分与章节面包屑注入，基于 Milvus 稠密向量 + 内置中文 BM25 + `RRFRanker(k=60)` 实现多租户混合检索；自建 51 条 Golden Set 评测集，**Recall@3 达 96.08%、MRR@3 达 0.8922、端到端引用准确率 100%**。

---

### 三、简历与面试中的「怎么用」实战建议

1. **面试介绍节奏（遵循 STAR 原则，1.5 分钟版）**
   * **S/T（背景与痛点）**：「企业内部 IT 服务台有两类高频需求：查规章制度和办工单业务。如果直接用普通 ReAct Agent，会有两个致命问题：一是制度回答容易张冠李戴或把工单状态当成公司规定；二是写操作（改单、关单发通知、发公告）如果让模型自己调工具，既可能被 Prompt 注入越权，也没法做跨角色的审批流。」
   * **A（你的核心设计，抛出 3 个钩子）**：
     1. *钩子一（证据分袋 + 混合检索）*：在 `gather_evidence` 把「制度依据」和「工单事实」分两个通道装，检索端用面包屑切分 + Milvus Dense/BM25 RRF；
     2. *钩子二（确定性风险表 + 跨进程 Interrupt）*：风险不让模型判，用 `RISK_BY_OP` + `compute_risk` 动态算，配合 `confirm_action` / `approve_action` 和 `PostgresSaver` 实现发起与审批分离；
     3. *钩子三（MCP 服务端鉴权）*：身份从只读 `UserContext` 取，绝不信模型填的 `reporter/tenant_id`。
   * **R（量化结果）**：抛出 Golden Set 的 `Recall@3 96.08%`、`引用准确率 100%`、以及完整的三态闭环演示。

2. **如果面试官追问：「你那 3.92%（2条）没命中的 Bad Case 是什么？怎么优化？」**
   * 你的 [report.md](<docs/eval/report.md#L52-L58>) 第 4 节正好记录了 `g33`（「已经用掉的服务时长能不能退」期望命中 `5.1 已消耗与定制内容`，实际命中了 `2.2 / 2.3 退款条件`）和 `g49`（「我能查到别的子公司的工单吗」期望命中 `2.3 跨租户访问`，实际命中了 `1.2 租户模型`）。
   * **高分回答**：
     1. **原因分析**：这两条都是典型的「口语化表述与制度术语存在词汇鸿沟」（例如用户说「别的子公司」，原文写的是「跨租户访问」；用户说「用掉的服务时长」，原文写的是「已消耗与定制内容」），且同一文档其他章节频繁出现「退款」「租户」等高频词，在 `top_k=3` 时把目标小节挤到了第 4~5 名。
     2. **改进方案**：第一，把召回阶段 `hybrid_search` 的粗排窗口放大到 `top_k=10~15`，后接一层 **Cross-Encoder 重排模型（如 `gte-rerank-v2` 或 `bge-reranker-v2-m3`）** 截断到 Top 3；第二，在入库时为每个小节生成 2~3 条合成问题（Hypothetical Questions / 别名索引）或做 Query Rewrite。

3. **需要我顺手帮你做的事（可选）**
   * 如果你准备把这个项目上传到 GitHub 并放进简历，我可以帮你：
     1. 清理掉代码中所有 `对应黑马讲义...` 的注释，替换为专业的架构设计注释；
     2. 修复 [InterruptCard.vue](<frontend/src/components/InterruptCard.vue#L134>) 中 `:disabled="!row.editable"` 的遗漏；
     3. 在项目根目录生成一份专门面向招聘方/面试官阅读的高质量 `README.md`（含架构图、亮点提炼、评测数据表格、快速启动指南）。
   
   如果需要我直接动手修改或生成 `README.md`，随时告诉我！
