<content>
1: """clarify 闸门：信息不够时，AI 反过来问员工（第一类人类介入）。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义《第1节 基本概念和入门》Interrupts：
4: - 节点里调用 interrupt(payload) 会暂停整张图，payload 通过 response["__interrupt__"] 交给前端
5: - 恢复时用 invoke(Command(resume=...), config)，同一个 thread_id 即可接着跑
6: - 必须有 checkpointer（我们用的是 PostgresSaver / SqliteSaver 兜底）
7: 
8: 恢复后的行为：
9: - 拿到 answers 合并进 action.args
10: - 回到 plan_action 重新组装草稿（可能仍有缺口，会再次暂停，直到参数齐）
11: """
12: 
13: from __future__ import annotations
14: 
15: from typing import Any
16: 
17: from langgraph.types import interrupt
18: 
19: from ..state import TicketState
20: from .action_spec import summary_of
21: from .human_gate import normalize_human_input
22: 
23: QUESTIONS = {
24:     "ticket_no": "请提供工单号（形如 TK-20261031-0001），我按单号去查/改/关。",
25:     "patch": "你想改哪个字段？例如「优先级改成高」「状态改成处理中」。",
26:     "title": "工单标题写什么？一句话说清现象就行（例如：财务系统登录失败）。",
27:     "content": "要发布的内容是什么？可以把处理过程直接贴给我。",
28:     "op": "你要我做什么？建单、改单、关单，还是把经验发到知识库？",
29: }
30: 
31: 
32: def _question_for(missing: list[str], facts: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
33:     """缺参数时问什么。
34: 
35:     名下有多张单时先把单号列出来让员工点一张：批量执行写操作风险太大，
36:     这里选择"不猜、不批量"，而不是替员工决定改哪一张。
37:     """
38:     numbers = [str(item.get("ticket_no")) for item in facts if item.get("ticket_no")]
39:     if missing and missing[0] == "ticket_no" and len(numbers) > 1:
40:         return (
41:             f"你名下有 {len(numbers)} 张单，我不会一次性批量处理；请指定其中一张：" + "、".join(numbers),
42:             numbers,
43:         )
44:     if missing:
45:         return QUESTIONS.get(missing[0], "还差一点信息，能再补一句吗？"), numbers
46:     return "还需要补充信息。", numbers
47: 
48: 
49: def ask_missing_info(state: TicketState) -> dict[str, Any]:
50:     """缺参数时问人一句（AI 问、员工答），答完回 plan_action 重算。"""
51:     action = state.get("action") or {}
52:     missing = list(action.get("missing") or [])
53:     question, options = _question_for(missing, state.get("facts") or [])
54: 
55:     payload = {
56:         "type": "clarify",
57:         "question": question,
58:         "missing": missing,
59:         "options": options,
60:         "title": summary_of(action),
61:         "hint": "回答后会从断点继续，不需要重新描述一遍。",
62:     }
63:     answer = interrupt(payload)
64:     parsed = normalize_human_input(answer)
65:     answers = parsed.get("answers") or {}
66: 
67:     action = state.get("action") or {}
68:     args = {**(action.get("args") or {}), **answers}
69:     update = {
70:         "action": {**action, "args": args, "missing": []},
71:         "reply": None,
72:     }
73:     return update

(End of file - total 73 lines)

1: """respond 节点：把结构化结果组织成"员工看得懂的一段话"，交给 FastAPI/前端。
2: 
3: 对应黑马讲义：
4: - 《第2节 AI私厨》：流式输出前最后一步就是拼好 AIMessage，再走 SSE
5: - 《第3节 Think In LangGraph》：回复里要带"下一步"，而不是只回一个 ok
6: 
7: 按链路拼话术：
8: - 闲聊：直接打招呼
9: - 知识：answer 已经写好了带引用的回答（这里只补一句下一步）
10: - 动作：按 result 说清楚"办成了没有、单号是多少、走了哪条通道、被谁确认/审批"
11: """
12: 
13: from __future__ import annotations
14: 
15: from typing import Any
16: 
17: from ..state import TicketState
18: 
19: 
20: def _chat_reply(state: TicketState) -> str:
21:     return (
22:         "你好，我是企业服务台助手。你可以直接问我制度流程（例如「发票怎么开」「年假多少天」），"
23:         "也可以让我办事（例如「帮我建个工单：财务系统登录不上」「把 TK-20261031-0001 关掉」）。"
24:     )
25: 
26: 
27: def _action_reply(state: TicketState) -> str:
28:     action = state.get("action") or {}
29:     result = state.get("result") or {}
30:     label = action.get("label") or "操作"
31:     op = action.get("op") or ""
32:     args = action.get("args") or {}
33: 
34:     existing = (state.get("reply") or "").strip()
35:     if state.get("outcome"):
36:         return existing or f"{label}没有执行。需要的话换个说法再来一次。"
37: 
38:     if not result:
39:         return f"{label}已完成。"
40: 
41:     if not result.get("ok"):
42:         error = result.get("error") or "未知错误"
43:         if isinstance(error, dict):
44:             # server 侧鉴权/校验失败时回的是 {"code":..,"reason":..}，只把 reason 说给员工听
45:             error = error.get("reason") or error.get("code") or "未知错误"
46:         return (
47:             f"{label}没成功：{error}\n"
48:             "已经把失败原因记录下来，你可以稍后重试，或找受理人看一眼日志。"
49:         )
50: 
51:     if op == "create_ticket":
52:         ticket = result.get("ticket") or {}
53:         ticket_no = ticket.get("ticket_no") or result.get("ticket_no") or "（未返回单号）"
54:         if result.get("deduplicated"):
55:             return (
56:                 f"这条和你之前提的是同一件事，我直接复用了原来的工单 {ticket_no}"
57:                 f"（状态：{ticket.get('status', 'open')}），没有重复建单。"
58:             )
59:         lines = [
60:             f"已建单：{ticket_no}",
61:             f"标题：{ticket.get('title') or args.get('title')}",
62:             f"优先级：{ticket.get('urgency') or args.get('urgency')}",
63:             f"状态：{ticket.get('status', 'open')}（提单人：{ticket.get('reporter', '-')}）",
64:         ]
65:         lines += [
66:             "",
67:             f"通道：MCP · {result.get('server', 'ticket')}-server",
68:             "处理人会在工单里回复进展；你也可以随时问我「查一下 " + str(ticket_no) + "」。",
69:         ]
70:         return "\n".join(lines)
71: 
72:     if op == "close_ticket":
73:         ticket = result.get("ticket") or {}
74:         ticket_no = ticket.get("ticket_no") or args.get("ticket_no") or ""
75:         notify = "，并已通知提单人" if args.get("notify_reporter") else ""
76:         return f"已关闭工单 {ticket_no}{notify}。如果问题复现，随时告诉我再开一张。"
77: 
78:     if op == "update_ticket":
79:         ticket = result.get("ticket") or {}
80:         return f"已更新工单 {ticket.get('ticket_no') or args.get('ticket_no')}：{result.get('updated') or '字段已改'}。"
81: 
82:     if op == "publish_kb_article":
83:         doc_id = result.get("doc_id") or "-"
84:         approval = state.get("approval") or {}
85:         who = approval.get("user_id") or "知识库 owner"
86:         return (
87:             f"已发布知识文章（{doc_id}），审批人：{who}。\n"
88:             "现在全员都能在知识库里搜到这篇，后续同类问题会直接引用它。"
89:         )
90: 
91:     if op == "query_ticket":
92:         ticket = result.get("ticket") or {}
93:         return (
94:             f"工单 {ticket.get('ticket_no')}：{ticket.get('title')}\n"
95:             f"状态：{ticket.get('status')}｜优先级：{ticket.get('urgency')}｜提单人：{ticket.get('reporter')}"
96:         )
97: 
98:     if op == "list_tickets":
99:         tickets = result.get("tickets") or []
100:         if not tickets:
101:             return "这个租户下暂时没有工单。"
102:         lines = [f"最近 {len(tickets)} 张工单："]
103:         for ticket in tickets:
104:             lines.append(
105:                 f"- {ticket.get('ticket_no')}｜{ticket.get('status')}｜{ticket.get('urgency')}｜{ticket.get('title')}"
106:             )
107:         return "\n".join(lines)
108: 
109:     return f"{label}已完成。"
110: 
111: 
112: def compose_reply(state: TicketState) -> dict[str, Any]:
113:     intent = state.get("intent") or "knowledge"
114:     reply = state.get("reply")
115: 
116:     if intent == "chat":
117:         reply = _chat_reply(state)
118:     elif intent == "action":
119:         reply = _action_reply(state)
120:     elif not reply:
121:         reply = "我没能生成回答，请换个说法再问一次。"
122: 
123:     if state.get("outcome") == "failed" and not reply:
124:         reply = "这次没有执行成功，请稍后再试或换一种说法。"
125: 
126:     return {"reply": reply}

<content>
1: """知识库入口：只走 Milvus，没有本地兜底。
2: 
3: 分层（面试可讲）：节点只认这里的函数名；换 pgvector / ES 只改这一层与 rag/vector_store.py。
4: 检索能力全部来自 Milvus 官方封装：dense（embedding + COSINE）+ sparse（内置 BM25），
5: 融合由服务端 `hybrid_search + RRFRanker(k=60)` 完成，客户端不重算分数。
6: 
7: 知识从哪来：`docs/kb/pdf` 的公开制度文档 → MinerU 解析成 Markdown（`rag/pdf_parser.py`）
8: → 结构感知切分（`rag/splitter.py`）→ 本模块 `ingest()` 写进 Milvus。
9: Milvus 或 embedding 不可用就直接抛错（`MilvusUnavailable`）——
10: "检索不到"和"检索服务挂了"是两种事故，不能混成一个空列表。
11: """
12: 
13: from __future__ import annotations
14: 
15: import logging
16: from typing import Any
17: 
18: from .rag import vector_store
19: from .rag.splitter import Chunk
20: from .settings import settings
21: 
22: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
23: 
24: DEFAULT_TENANT = settings.default_tenant
25: 
26: _ready = False
27: 
28: 
29: class MilvusUnavailable(RuntimeError):
30:     """Milvus 连不上、collection 不可用，或者知识库还没入库。"""
31: 
32: 
33: def ensure_ready() -> None:
34:     """第一次检索前确认 collection 存在且非空；不自动造数据（知识库只接受真实文档）。"""
35:     global _ready
36:     if _ready:
37:         return
38:     try:
39:         stats = vector_store.collection_stats()
40:     except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
41:         raise MilvusUnavailable(
42:             f"Milvus 不可用（{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}）。"
43:             "请先 `cd deploy && docker compose up -d`，再用 `python -m scripts.env_status` 看配置。"
44:         ) from exc
45:     if not stats["exists"] or stats["rows"] == 0:
46:         raise MilvusUnavailable(
47:             "知识库是空的。先入库一次："
48:             "`python -m scripts.ingest_docs --md docs/kb/md --to-milvus`"
49:         )
50:     _ready = True
51: 
52: 
53: def search(query: str, k: int = 3, tenant_id: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
54:     """知识库检索：dense + BM25 两路召回，服务端 RRF 融合。"""
55:     ensure_ready()
56:     return vector_store.hybrid_search(query, k=k, tenant_id=tenant_id)
57: 
58: 
59: def ingest(
60:     chunks: list[Chunk],
61:     *,
62:     tenant_id: str | None = None,
63:     force: bool = False,
64: ) -> int:
65:     """把切分好的 chunk 写进 Milvus（`--to-milvus` 调这里；force 会重建 collection）。"""
66:     global _ready
67:     count = vector_store.upsert_chunks(chunks, tenant_id=tenant_id, force=force)
68:     _ready = count > 0
69:     logger.info("知识库入库完成：%s 条 chunk，租户=%s", count, tenant_id or DEFAULT_TENANT)
70:     return count
71: 
72: 
73: def publish_article(
74:     *,
75:     title: str,
76:     content: str,
77:     tags: str = "",
78:     change_note: str = "",
79:     tenant_id: str | None = None,
80:     doc_status: str = "published",
81: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
82:     """发布一篇知识文章（kb-server 的 publish_kb_article 落到这里）。"""
83:     ensure_ready()
84:     return vector_store.publish_article(
85:         title=title,
86:         content=content,
87:         tags=[tag.strip() for tag in tags.split(",") if tag.strip()],
88:         change_note=change_note,
89:         tenant_id=tenant_id or DEFAULT_TENANT,
90:         doc_status=doc_status,
91:     )
92: 
93: 
94: def health() -> dict[str, Any]:
95:     """健康检查：连得上、collection 存在、有几行。"""
96:     stats = vector_store.collection_stats()
97:     return {"backend": "milvus", "collection": stats["collection"], "rows": stats["rows"]}
98: 
99: 
100: __all__ = [
101:     "DEFAULT_TENANT",
102:     "MilvusUnavailable",
103:     "ensure_ready",
104:     "health",
105:     "ingest",
106:     "publish_article",
107:     "search",
108: ]

[tool • read]
<path>D:\zuixin\backend\app\agents\rag\pdf_parser.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """PDF → Markdown：用 MinerU 官方 SDK（mineru-open-sdk）。
2: 
3: 为什么不用 PyPDFLoader：普通 PDF 抽文本会把左右分栏、表格、页眉页脚全搅在一起，
4: MinerU 这类版面解析工具输出的是 **Markdown**（标题层级 + 还原后的表格），
5: 后面才能做「结构感知切分」。对应黑马《第1节 RAG Agent》2.1.3 节。
6: 
7: 两种模式（SDK 里就是两个方法）：
8: - flash：轻量解析，免登录，适合 <=20 页的文档（我们要用的制度 PDF 都在这范围内）；
9: - precision：精准解析，需要 MINERU_API_KEY（mineru.net 免费申请），支持更大页数。
10: 
11: 本模块只做「拿 Markdown」，不负责切分（切分见 splitter.py），也不碰向量库。
12: """
13: 
14: from __future__ import annotations
15: 
16: import time
17: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
18: from pathlib import Path
19: from typing import Any
20: 
21: from ..settings import settings
22: 
23: 
24: class MinerUUnavailable(RuntimeError):
25:     """MinerU 调不通（没装 SDK / 没配 token / 网络或额度问题）。"""
26: 
27: 
28: @dataclass(frozen=True)
29: class ParsedDocument:
30:     """一份解析完的文档。markdown 是唯一真正重要的东西。"""
31: 
32:     source: str
33:     markdown: str
34:     mode: str
35:     elapsed_s: float
36:     task_id: str = ""
37:     image_count: int = 0
38:     extra: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
39: 
40:     @property
41:     def stem(self) -> str:
42:         """文件名主干，用来当文档 title / doc_id 的一部分。"""
43:         name = self.source.rsplit("/", 1)[-1].rsplit("\\", 1)[-1]
44:         return Path(name).stem
45: 
46: 
47: def _client(require_token: bool) -> "Any":
48:     """延迟导入 SDK：没装也不影响其它链路。"""
49:     try:
50:         from mineru import MinerU
51:     except ImportError as exc:  # pragma: no cover - 依赖缺失时才走到
52:         raise MinerUUnavailable(
53:             "缺少依赖 mineru-open-sdk，请执行：uv pip install mineru-open-sdk"
54:         ) from exc
55: 
56:     token = settings.mineru_api_key or None
57:     if require_token and not token:
58:         raise MinerUUnavailable(
59:             "precision 模式需要 MINERU_API_KEY（或 MINERU_TOKEN）："
60:             "请到 https://mineru.net/ 创建 Token，写进项目根目录 .env（我不读你的值）"
61:         )
62:     return MinerU(token=token)
63: 
64: 
65: def parse(
66:     source: str | Path,
67:     *,
68:     mode: str | None = None,
69:     ocr: bool | None = None,
70:     language: str | None = None,
71:     timeout: int | None = None,
72: ) -> ParsedDocument:
73:     """把一个 PDF（本地路径或公网 URL）解析成 Markdown。
74: 
75:     mode: "flash"（默认，免 token）| "precision"（需 token）
76:     ocr:  扫描件才需要 True；文本型 PDF 保持 False 更快
77:     """
78:     mode = (mode or settings.mineru_mode or "flash").lower()
79:     if mode not in ("flash", "precision"):
80:         raise ValueError(f"未知的 MinerU 模式：{mode}（只能是 flash 或 precision）")
81: 
82:     ocr_flag = settings.mineru_ocr if ocr is None else ocr
83:     lang = language or settings.mineru_language
84:     wait = timeout or settings.mineru_timeout
85:     src = str(source)
86:     started = time.perf_counter()
87: 
88:     try:
89:         client = _client(require_token=(mode == "precision"))
90:         if mode == "flash":
91:             result = client.flash_extract(
92:                 src,
93:                 language=lang,
94:                 is_ocr=ocr_flag,
95:                 enable_formula=True,
96:                 enable_table=True,
97:                 timeout=wait,
98:             )
99:         else:
100:             result = client.extract(src, ocr=ocr_flag, language=lang, timeout=wait)
101:     except MinerUUnavailable:
102:         raise
103:     except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - SDK 的异常类型很多，统一包装
104:         raise MinerUUnavailable(f"MinerU 解析失败（{mode}）：{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}") from exc
105: 
106:     markdown = getattr(result, "markdown", None)
107:     if not markdown:
108:         raise MinerUUnavailable(f"MinerU 返回里没有 markdown（task_id={getattr(result, 'task_id', '')}）")
109: 
110:     return ParsedDocument(
111:         source=src,
112:         markdown=markdown,
113:         mode=mode,
114:         elapsed_s=round(time.perf_counter() - started, 2),
115:         task_id=getattr(result, "task_id", "") or "",
116:         image_count=len(getattr(result, "images", []) or []),
117:         extra={"ocr": ocr_flag, "language": lang},
118:     )
119: 
120: 
121: def parse_many(sources: list[str | Path], **kwargs: Any) -> list[ParsedDocument]:
122:     """批量解析；单个失败不拖垮整批，失败信息放进返回值之外的日志由调用方处理。"""
123:     docs: list[ParsedDocument] = []
124:     for src in sources:
125:         docs.append(parse(src, **kwargs))
126:     return docs

(End of file - total 126 lines)
</content>

<path>D:\zuixin\scripts\run_graph.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: r"""CLI：不起 FastAPI 也能把整张图走完（对应讲义《第2节 AI私厨》之前的手工联调阶段）。
2: 
3: 用法（在项目根目录、用 .venv 的 python）：
4: 
5:     python -m scripts.run_graph run    --thread t1 -m "发票怎么开"
6:     python -m scripts.run_graph run    --thread t2 -m "帮我建个工单：财务系统登录不上，影响 3 个人"
7:     python -m scripts.run_graph state  --thread t2
8:     python -m scripts.run_graph resume --thread t2 --approve
9:     python -m scripts.run_graph resume --thread t2 --edit urgency=high,title="财务系统登录失败"
10:     python -m scripts.run_graph resume --thread t2 --answer ticket_no=TK-20261031-0001
11:     python -m scripts.run_graph resume --thread t2 --reject -m "标题写错了"
12:     python -m scripts.run_graph chat   --thread t9
13:     python -m scripts.run_graph graph
14:     python -m scripts.run_graph tools
15:     python -m scripts.run_graph approvals --role approver          # 审批中心（谁申请、什么事、批没批）
16:     python -m scripts.run_graph approvals --status pending --role approver
17:     
18: 跨进程恢复的关键：同一个 --thread 只影响 config.configurable.thread_id，
19: State 早就落在 Postgres（或兜底 SQLite）里了，进程退出不影响。
20: """
21: 
22: from __future__ import annotations
23: 
24: import argparse
25: import json
26: import sys
27: from typing import Any
28: 
29: from backend.app.agents.graph import get_graph
30: from backend.app.agents.state import UserContext, initial_state
31: 
32: sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
33: 
34: 
35: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
36: # 基础设施：上下文 / 配置 / 打印
37: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
38: def _context(args) -> UserContext:
39:     roles = tuple(args.role or ["employee"])
40:     return UserContext(
41:         user_id=args.user,
42:         tenant_id=args.tenant,
43:         roles=roles,
44:         display_name=args.user,
45:     )
46: 
47: 
48: def _config(args) -> dict[str, Any]:
49:     return {"configurable": {"thread_id": args.thread}}
50: 
51: 
52: def _print_interrupt(payload: Any) -> None:
53:     data = dict(payload or {})
54:     kind = data.get("type")
55:     print("\n[暂停] 等你一句话" + (f"（类型：{kind}）" if kind else ""))
56:     if data.get("question"):
57:         print(f"  AI 问：{data['question']}")
58:     if data.get("title"):
59:         print(f"  标题：{data['title']}")
60:     if data.get("hint"):
61:         print(f"  提示：{data['hint']}")
62:     for field in data.get("fields") or []:
63:         source = field.get("source")
64:         print(f"  - {field.get('label')}: {field.get('value')}  [{source}]")
65:     if data.get("citations"):
66:         print("  引用：" + "、".join(str(x) for x in data["citations"] if x))
67:     print("  继续：python -m scripts.run_graph resume --thread <id> --approve / --reject / --edit k=v / --answer k=v")
68:     print("\n[原始载荷]")
69:     print(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str))
70: 
71: 
72: def _show_result(result: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
73:     print("\n[完成]")
74:     for key in ("outcome", "intent", "gate", "via"):
75:         if result.get(key):
76:             print(f"  {key:10s}: {result[key]}")
77:     if result.get("reply"):
78:         print("\n[回复]")
79:         print(result["reply"])
80:     evidence = result.get("policy_evidence") or []
81:     if evidence:
82:         print("\n[引用]")
83:         for item in evidence:
84:             print(f"  {item.get('citation')}  score={item.get('score')}")
85:     if result.get("error"):
86:         print(f"\n[错误] {result['error']}")
87: 
88: 
89: def _snapshot_summary(snapshot: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
90:     values = dict(snapshot.values or {})
91:     return {
92:         "next": list(snapshot.next or ()),
93:         "outcome": values.get("outcome"),
94:         "intent": values.get("intent"),
95:         "gate": values.get("gate"),
96:         "risk": values.get("risk"),
97:         "reply": (values.get("reply") or "")[:120],
98:     }
99: 
100: 
101: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
102: # 子命令
103: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
104: def _invoke(args, resume_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
105:     graph = get_graph()
106:     config = _config(args)
107:     context = _context(args)
108:     if resume_payload is not None:
109:         from langgraph.types import Command
110: 
111:         return graph.invoke(Command(resume=resume_payload), config, context=context)
112:     state = initial_state(tenant_id=args.tenant, user_id=args.user, query=args.message)
113:     return graph.invoke(state, config, context=context)
114: 
115: 
116: def _handle_output(result: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
117:     interrupts = result.get("__interrupt__")
118:     if interrupts:
119:         _print_interrupt(interrupts[0].value)
120:         return
121:     _show_result(result)
122: 
123: 
124: def cmd_run(args) -> None:
125:     _handle_output(_invoke(args))
126: 
127: 
128: def _parse_pairs(raw: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
129:     pairs: dict[str, Any] = {}
130:     for chunk in (raw or "").split(","):
131:         chunk = chunk.strip()
132:         if not chunk or "=" not in chunk:
133:             continue
134:         key, value = chunk.split("=", 1)
135:         value = value.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
136:         if value.lower() in {"true", "false"}:
137:             pairs[key.strip()] = value.lower() == "true"
138:         else:
139:             pairs[key.strip()] = value
140:     return pairs
141: 
142: 
143: def cmd_resume(args) -> None:
144:     payload: dict[str, Any]
145:     if args.answer:
146:         payload = {"answers": _parse_pairs(args.answer)}
147:     elif args.approve:
148:         payload = {"type": "decision", "decision": {"type": "approve"}}
149:     elif args.reject:
150:         payload = {"type": "decision", "decision": {"type": "reject", "message": args.message or "不同意"}}
151:     elif args.edit:
152:         payload = {"type": "decision", "decision": {"type": "edit", "patch": _parse_pairs(args.edit)}}
153:     else:
154:         payload = {"type": "decision", "decision": {"type": "approve"}}
155:     _handle_output(_invoke(args, payload))
156: 
157: 
158: def cmd_state(args) -> None:
159:     graph = get_graph()
160:     snapshot = graph.get_state(_config(args))
161:     print(json.dumps(_snapshot_summary(snapshot), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str))
162:     action = (snapshot.values or {}).get("action")
163:     if action:
164:         print("\n[action]")
165:         print(json.dumps(action, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str))
166: 
167: 
168: def cmd_graph(args) -> None:
169:     print(get_graph().get_graph().draw_mermaid())
170: 
171: 
172: def cmd_tools(args) -> None:
173:     from backend.app.agents.tools import mcp_client
174: 
175:     print(json.dumps(mcp_client.transport_info(), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
176:     catalog = mcp_client.tool_catalog()
177:     print(f"\n共 {len(catalog)} 个工具：")
178:     for item in catalog:
179:         print(f"  [{item['server']}] {item['name']:22s} {item['label']:8s} {item['risk']}")
180: 
181: 
182: def _actor(args) -> dict[str, Any]:
183:     """CLI 参数 → 身份票据（和节点里的 actor_of 同一个形状）。"""
184:     return {"user_id": args.user, "tenant_id": args.tenant, "roles": list(args.role or ["employee"])}
185: 
186: 
187: def cmd_approvals(args) -> None:
188:     from backend.app.agents.tools import ticket_tools
189: 
190:     data = ticket_tools.list_approvals(_actor(args), status=args.status or "", limit=args.limit)
191:     print(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
192: 
193: 
194: def cmd_chat(args) -> None:
195:     print("输入问题，回车发送；:q 退出。")
196:     while True:
197:         try:
198:             line = input("\n你> ").strip()
199:         except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
200:             break
201:         if not line:
202:             continue
203:         if line in {":q", "quit", "exit"}:
204:             break
205:         args.message = line
206:         _handle_output(_invoke(args))
207: 
208: 
209: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
210: # 参数
211: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
212: def _add_common(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser) -> None:
213:     parser.add_argument("--thread", default="t1", help="会话 id（= thread_id，跨进程恢复靠它）")
214:     parser.add_argument("--tenant", default="tenant-demo", help="租户 id（多租户隔离）")
215:     parser.add_argument("--user", default="u-1001", help="员工 id")
216:     parser.add_argument(
217:         "--role",
218:         action="append",
219:         choices=["employee", "approver", "kb_owner"],
220:         help="角色，可重复：--role employee --role kb_owner",
221:     )
222: 
223: 
224: def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
225:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="SmartDesk AI 服务台 Agent CLI")
226:     sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
227: 
228:     run = sub.add_parser("run", help="发一句话，跑到暂停或结束")
229:     _add_common(run)
230:     run.add_argument("-m", "--message", required=True, help="员工说的话")
231:     run.set_defaults(func=cmd_run)

[tool • read]
<path>D:\zuixin\scripts\trace_run.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """逐节点跟读工具：跑一条请求，把每个节点「写了哪些字段」一个个打出来。
2: 
3: 用法：
4:     python -m scripts.trace_run -m "发票怎么开"
5:     python -m scripts.trace_run -m "帮我建个工单：VPN 连不上，影响 2 个人"
6:     python -m scripts.trace_run -m "..." --resume '{"type":"decision","decision":"approve"}'
7: 
8: 它比 run 子命令多做的唯一一件事：用 stream_mode="updates" 看「每一步谁写了什么」，
9: 而不是只看最后结果。这是读代码时最需要的视角。
10: """
11: 
12: from __future__ import annotations
13: 
14: import argparse
15: import json
16: import sys
17: from typing import Any
18: 
19: from backend.app.agents.graph import get_graph
20: from backend.app.agents.state import UserContext, initial_state
21: 
22: 
23: MAX = 88
24: 
25: 
26: def _short(value: Any) -> str:
27:     if isinstance(value, str):
28:         text = value.replace("\n", "\\n")
29:         return text if len(text) <= MAX else text[:MAX] + "…"
30:     if isinstance(value, list):
31:         if not value:
32:             return "[]"
33:         head = value[0]
34:         if isinstance(head, dict):
35:             keys = "/".join(list(head.keys())[:5])
36:             return f"[{len(value)} 条, 每条形如 {{{keys}}}]"
37:         return f"[{len(value)} 条] " + _short(str(head))
38:     if isinstance(value, dict):
39:         keys = list(value.keys())
40:         return "{" + ", ".join(keys[:6]) + ("…" if len(keys) > 6 else "") + "}"
41:     return str(value)
42: 
43: 
44: def _print_update(node: str, update: Any, seen: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
45:     print(f"\n{'=' * 78}\n▶ 节点 {node}")
46:     if not isinstance(update, dict):
47:         print(f"   返回：{_short(update)}")
48:         return
49:     changed = 0
50:     for key, value in update.items():
51:         if key == "messages":
52:             print(f"   messages      +{len(value)} 条（由 add_messages reducer 追加）")
53:             for msg in value:
54:                 kind = type(msg).__name__
55:                 print(f"                 · {kind}: {_short(getattr(msg, 'content', ''))}")
56:             changed += 1
57:             continue
58:         same = key in seen and seen[key] == value
59:         seen[key] = value
60:         if same:
61:             continue
62:         changed += 1
63:         print(f"   {key:<16} {_short(value)}")
64:     if not changed:
65:         print("   （这个节点没有改动任何字段）")
66:     else:
67:         print(f"   —— 本节点写了 {changed} 个字段，其余 {len(seen) - changed} 个沿用上一步")
68: 
69: 
70: def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
71:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="逐节点跟读一条请求")
72:     parser.add_argument("-m", "--message", required=True, help="用户这一句话")
73:     parser.add_argument("--thread", default="trace", help="thread_id，同一 id 才能接着跑")
74:     parser.add_argument("--tenant", default="tenant-demo")
75:     parser.add_argument("--user", default="u-1001")
76:     parser.add_argument("--role", action="append", default=None, help="可重复，如 --role approver")
77:     parser.add_argument("--resume", default=None, help="JSON 字符串：从上一个 interrupt 继续")
78:     args = parser.parse_args(argv)
79: 
80:     graph = get_graph()
81:     config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": args.thread}}
82:     context = UserContext(
83:         tenant_id=args.tenant,
84:         user_id=args.user,
85:         roles=tuple(args.role or ["employee"]),
86:     )
87: 
88:     seen: dict[str, Any] = {}
89:     payload: Any = initial_state(tenant_id=args.tenant, user_id=args.user, query=args.message)
90:     if isinstance(payload, dict):
91:         seen.update(payload)   # 起点已有的字段不算"本节点写的"
92:     if args.resume:
93:         from langgraph.types import Command
94: 
95:         payload = Command(resume=json.loads(args.resume))
96:         print(f"[跟读] 线程 {args.thread}｜从暂停点继续（Command(resume=...)）")
97:     else:
98:         print(f"[跟读] 线程 {args.thread}｜起点：{args.message!r}")
99: 
100:     for chunk in graph.stream(payload, config, context=context, stream_mode="updates"):
101:         if "__interrupt__" in chunk:
102:             interrupts = chunk["__interrupt__"]
103:             value = interrupts[0].value if hasattr(interrupts[0], "value") else interrupts[0]
104:             print(f"\n{'=' * 78}\n⏸ 图在 interrupt 处停下（进程可以退出，State 已落 checkpoint）")
105:             print(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)[:1600])
106:             print(f"\n继续：python -m scripts.trace_run --thread {args.thread} -m \"同一条输入\" \\")
107:             print("        --resume '{\"type\":\"decision\",\"decision\":\"approve\"}'")
108:             return 0
109:         for node, update in chunk.items():
110:             _print_update(node, update, seen)
111: 
112:     snapshot = graph.get_state(config)
113:     print(f"\n{'=' * 78}\n■ 跑完｜next={snapshot.next}｜outcome={snapshot.values.get('outcome')}")
114:     return 0
115: 
116: 
117: if __name__ == "__main__":
118:     sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
119:     raise SystemExit(main())

(End of file - total 119 lines)

<content>
1: """知识库入库：PDF → Markdown（MinerU）→ chunk（结构感知切分）→ Milvus。
2: 
3: 用法（Python 用 .venv）：
4: 
5:   # 1) 只试 MinerU 解析，把 Markdown 存下来看质量
6:   python -m scripts.ingest_docs --pdf docs/kb/pdf/员工手册.pdf --save-md
7: 
8:   # 2) 解析 + 切分，打印统计与样例 chunk，并把 chunk 落成 jsonl（不入库）
9:   python -m scripts.ingest_docs --source docs/kb/pdf --out docs/kb/chunks.jsonl
10: 
11:   # 3) 直接切现成的 Markdown（不调用 MinerU）并**写进 Milvus**（日常入库就用这条）
12:   python -m scripts.ingest_docs --md docs/kb/md --to-milvus
13: 
14:   # 4) 改了 schema / 换 embedding 模型，重建 collection 再入库
15:   python -m scripts.ingest_docs --md docs/kb/md --to-milvus --force
16: 
17:   # 5) 精准模式（需要 .env 里的 MINERU_API_KEY）
18:   python -m scripts.ingest_docs --source docs/kb/pdf --mode precision
19: 
20: 注意：`--source` 默认扫 docs/kb/pdf。只想切已解析好的 Markdown 时，
21: 把 `--source` 指到一个空目录（或干脆不传 `--md` 之外的参数），避免又把 PDF 重跑一遍 MinerU。
22: """
23: 
24: from __future__ import annotations
25: 
26: import argparse
27: import json
28: import sys
29: from pathlib import Path
30: 
31: from backend.app.agents import knowledge
32: from backend.app.agents.rag import pdf_parser
33: from backend.app.agents.rag.splitter import (
34:     CHUNK_OVERLAP,
35:     CHUNK_SIZE,
36:     Chunk,
37:     chunk_stats,
38:     split_markdown,
39: )
40: from backend.app.agents.settings import ROOT_DIR, settings
41: 
42: SUPPORTED_SUFFIXES = {".pdf"}
43: 
44: 
45: def _collect(source: Path | None, extra: list[str]) -> list[Path]:
46:     """收集待处理文件：命令行显式给的 + 目录里扫到的。"""
47:     files: list[Path] = [Path(p) for p in extra]
48:     if source and source.exists():
49:         if source.is_file():
50:             files.append(source)
51:         else:
52:             files.extend(sorted(p for p in source.rglob("*") if p.suffix.lower() in SUPPORTED_SUFFIXES))
53:     seen, unique = set(), []
54:     for f in files:
55:         key = str(f.resolve()).lower()
56:         if key not in seen:
57:             seen.add(key)
58:             unique.append(f)
59:     return unique
60: 
61: 
62: def _print_chunk(index: int, chunk: Chunk, show: int = 120) -> None:
63:     print(f"   [{index:02d}] {chunk.citation}  ({len(chunk.content)} 字)")
64:     preview = chunk.content.replace("\n", " ⏎ ")
65:     print(f"        {preview[:show]}{'…' if len(preview) > show else ''}")
66: 
67: 
68: def _write_jsonl(path: Path, rows: list[dict]) -> None:
69:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
70:     with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as handle:
71:         for row in rows:
72:             handle.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
73: 
74: 
75: def _chunks_from_markdown(
76:     markdown: str,
77:     *,
78:     source_file: str,
79:     title: str,
80:     tenant_id: str,
81:     chunk_size: int,
82:     chunk_overlap: int,
83: ) -> list[Chunk]:
84:     return split_markdown(
85:         markdown,
86:         source_file=source_file,
87:         title=title,
88:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
89:         chunk_size=chunk_size,
90:         chunk_overlap=chunk_overlap,
91:     )
92: 
93: 
94: def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
95:     sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
96: 
97:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="PDF/Markdown → chunk → Milvus（知识库入库）")
98:     parser.add_argument("--source", default=None, help="PDF 目录（默认 settings.kb_source_dir；给了 --md 就不扫）")
99:     parser.add_argument("--pdf", nargs="*", default=[], help="显式指定 PDF 文件（可多个）")
100:     parser.add_argument("--md", nargs="*", default=[], help="直接切现成 Markdown（跳过 MinerU）")
101:     parser.add_argument("--mode", default=None, choices=["flash", "precision"], help="MinerU 解析模式")
102:     parser.add_argument("--ocr", action="store_true", help="扫描件才需要（更慢）")
103:     parser.add_argument("--tenant", default="tenant-demo", help="租户 id")
104:     parser.add_argument("--chunk-size", type=int, default=CHUNK_SIZE)
105:     parser.add_argument("--chunk-overlap", type=int, default=CHUNK_OVERLAP)
106:     parser.add_argument("--out", default="", help="把 chunk 写成 jsonl 的路径（可选）")
107:     parser.add_argument("--save-md", action="store_true", help="把 MinerU 解析出的 Markdown 存到 docs/kb/md/")
108:     parser.add_argument("--to-milvus", action="store_true", help="写入 Milvus（入库）")
109:     parser.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="配合 --to-milvus：先 drop 重建 collection")
110:     parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0, help="最多处理几个文件（调试用）")
111:     args = parser.parse_args(argv)
112: 
113:     md_files: list[Path] = []
114:     for raw in args.md:
115:         candidate = Path(raw)
116:         if candidate.is_dir():
117:             md_files.extend(sorted(candidate.rglob("*.md")))
118:         else:
119:             md_files.append(candidate)
120:     # 给了 --md 就只切 Markdown；否则扫 PDF 目录（避免顺手把 7 份 PDF 又跑一遍 MinerU）
121:     source_dir = Path(args.source) if args.source else (None if md_files else settings.kb_source_dir)
122:     pdf_files = _collect(source_dir, args.pdf) if source_dir or args.pdf else []
123:     if args.limit:
124:         pdf_files = pdf_files[: args.limit]
125: 
126:     if not md_files and not pdf_files:
127:         print(f"[x] 没找到文档：{source_dir or '未指定目录'} 下没有 PDF，也没有传 --pdf / --md")
128:         print("    先把公开制度 PDF 放进 docs/kb/pdf/，或用 --md 直接切已解析好的 Markdown")
129:         return 2
130: 
131:     print("=" * 78)
132:     print(f"入库参数：租户={args.tenant}  chunk={args.chunk_size}/{args.chunk_overlap}  "
133:           f"MinerU={args.mode or settings.mineru_mode}  token={'已配' if settings.mineru_api_key else '未配（只能用 flash）'}")
134:     print(f"待处理：PDF {len(pdf_files)} 个，Markdown {len(md_files)} 个")
135:     print("=" * 78)
136: 
137:     all_chunks: list[Chunk] = []
138: 
139:     # ---------- Markdown 直接切 ----------
140:     for path in md_files:
141:         markdown = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
142:         chunks = _chunks_from_markdown(
143:             markdown,
144:             source_file=path.name,
145:             title="",
146:             tenant_id=args.tenant,
147:             chunk_size=args.chunk_size,
148:             chunk_overlap=args.chunk_overlap,
149:         )
150:         print(f"\n[md] {path.name}  →  {chunk_stats(chunks)}")
151:         for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks[:5]):
152:             _print_chunk(i, chunk)
153:         all_chunks.extend(chunks)
154: 
155:     # ---------- PDF 走 MinerU ----------
156:     for path in pdf_files:
157:         print(f"\n[pdf] {path.name}  解析中（MinerU {args.mode or settings.mineru_mode}）…")
158:         try:
159:             parsed = pdf_parser.parse(path, mode=args.mode, ocr=(True if args.ocr else None))
160:         except pdf_parser.MinerUUnavailable as exc:
161:             print(f"   [x] 解析失败：{exc}")
162:             continue
163: 
164:         print(f"   ok：{parsed.elapsed_s}s，markdown {len(parsed.markdown)} 字，图片 {parsed.image_count} 张"
165:               f"{'，task_id=' + parsed.task_id if parsed.task_id else ''}")
166:         print("   markdown 前 6 行：")
167:         for line in parsed.markdown.split("\n")[:6]:
168:             print(f"     | {line[:90]}")
169: 
170:         if args.save_md:
171:             out_md = ROOT_DIR / "docs" / "kb" / "md" / f"{path.stem}.md"
172:             out_md.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
173:             out_md.write_text(parsed.markdown, encoding="utf-8")
174:             print(f"   markdown 已存：{out_md}")
175: 
176:         chunks = _chunks_from_markdown(
177:             parsed.markdown,
178:             source_file=path.name,
179:             title="",
180:             tenant_id=args.tenant,
181:             chunk_size=args.chunk_size,
182:             chunk_overlap=args.chunk_overlap,
183:         )
184:         print(f"   切分：{chunk_stats(chunks)}")
185:         for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks[:3]):
186:             _print_chunk(i, chunk)
187:         all_chunks.extend(chunks)
188: 
189:     print("\n" + "=" * 78)
190:     print(f"合计 chunk：{len(all_chunks)}")
191: 
192:     if args.out and all_chunks:
193:         out = Path(args.out)
194:         if not out.is_absolute():
195:             out = ROOT_DIR / out
196:         _write_jsonl(out, [{"text": c.text, "content": c.content, **c.metadata} for c in all_chunks])
197:         print(f"chunk 已写：{out}（{len(all_chunks)} 行）")
198: 
199:     if args.to_milvus and all_chunks:
200:         print(f"\n写入 Milvus（集合 {settings.milvus_collection}，租户 {args.tenant}）…")
201:         try:
202:             count = knowledge.ingest(all_chunks, tenant_id=args.tenant, force=args.force)
203:         except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
204:             print(f"[x] 入库失败：{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
205:             return 2
206:         stats = knowledge.health()
207:         print(f"入库完成：{count} 条；collection 当前 {stats['rows']} 行")
208:     return 0
209: 
210: 
211: if __name__ == "__main__":
212:     raise SystemExit(main())

<path>D:\zuixin\scripts\gen_kb_pdfs.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """把 docs/kb/src/*.md 渲染成 PDF（供 MinerU 解析入库）。
2: 
3: 只支持本项目用到的那一小撮 Markdown：# / ## / ###、段落、- 列表、
4: | 表格 |、> 提示块、**粗体**、`行内代码`。
5: """
6: from __future__ import annotations
7: 
8: import html
9: import re
10: import sys
11: from pathlib import Path
12: 
13: from reportlab.lib import colors
14: from reportlab.lib.enums import TA_CENTER, TA_JUSTIFY
15: from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
16: from reportlab.lib.styles import ParagraphStyle
17: from reportlab.lib.units import cm
18: from reportlab.pdfbase import pdfmetrics
19: from reportlab.pdfbase.ttfonts import TTFont
20: from reportlab.platypus import (BaseDocTemplate, Frame, KeepTogether, PageTemplate,
21:                                 Paragraph, Spacer, Table, TableStyle)
22: 
23: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
24: SRC_DIR = ROOT / "docs" / "kb" / "src"
25: PDF_DIR = ROOT / "docs" / "kb" / "pdf"
26: 
27: FONT_BODY = "XlSans"
28: FONT_BOLD = "XlSans-Bold"
29: FONT_FILES = [
30:     (FONT_BODY, r"C:\Windows\Fonts\msyh.ttc"),
31:     (FONT_BOLD, r"C:\Windows\Fonts\msyhbd.ttc"),
32: ]
33: 
34: INK = colors.HexColor("#1F2733")
35: INK2 = colors.HexColor("#5A6672")
36: ACCENT = colors.HexColor("#1F4E79")
37: RULE = colors.HexColor("#C9D3DE")
38: HEAD_BG = colors.HexColor("#E8EEF7")
39: MONO = colors.HexColor("#B03060")
40: 
41: CONTENT_W = A4[0] - 4.4 * cm
42: 
43: 
44: class NumberedCanvas:
45:     """页脚写「第 N 页 / 共 M 页」。"""
46: 
47:     def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
48:         from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas as _canvas
49: 
50:         class _Canvas(_canvas.Canvas):
51:             def __init__(self, *a, **kw):
52:                 super().__init__(*a, **kw)
53:                 self._saved = []
54: 
55:             def showPage(self):
56:                 self._saved.append(dict(self.__dict__))
57:                 self._startPage()
58: 
59:             def save(self):
60:                 total = len(self._saved)
61:                 for state in self._saved:
62:                     self.__dict__.update(state)
63:                     self._footer(total)
64:                     super().showPage()
65:                 super().save()
66: 
67:             def _footer(self, total: int):
68:                 self.setFont(FONT_BODY, 8.5)
69:                 self.setFillColor(INK2)
70:                 page = f"第 {self._pageNumber} 页 / 共 {total} 页"
71:                 self.drawCentredString(A4[0] / 2, 1.35 * cm, page)
72: 
73:         return _Canvas(*args, **kwargs)
74: 
75: 
76: def register_fonts() -> None:
77:     for name, path in FONT_FILES:
78:         if name not in pdfmetrics.getRegisteredFontNames():
79:             pdfmetrics.registerFont(TTFont(name, path, subfontIndex=0))
80: 
81: 
82: def inline(text: str) -> str:
83:     out = html.escape(text, quote=False)
84:     out = re.sub(r"\*\*(.+?)\*\*", r"<b>\1</b>", out)
85:     out = re.sub(r"`(.+?)`", rf'<font color="{MONO.hexval()[2:]}">\1</font>', out)
86:     return out
87: 
88: 
89: def styles() -> dict[str, ParagraphStyle]:
90:     base = dict(fontName=FONT_BODY, wordWrap="CJK")
91:     return {
92:         "title": ParagraphStyle("title", fontName=FONT_BOLD, fontSize=22, leading=30,
93:                                 textColor=ACCENT, spaceAfter=10, wordWrap="CJK"),
94:         "h2": ParagraphStyle("h2", fontName=FONT_BOLD, fontSize=14.5, leading=22,
95:                              textColor=ACCENT, spaceBefore=14, spaceAfter=8, wordWrap="CJK"),
96:         "h3": ParagraphStyle("h3", fontName=FONT_BOLD, fontSize=11.5, leading=19,
97:                              textColor=colors.HexColor("#24476F"), spaceBefore=9, spaceAfter=5,
98:                              wordWrap="CJK"),
99:         "body": ParagraphStyle("body", fontSize=10.5, leading=19, alignment=TA_JUSTIFY,
100:                                spaceAfter=7, textColor=INK, **base),
101:         "bullet": ParagraphStyle("bullet", fontSize=10.5, leading=18, leftIndent=14,
102:                                  bulletIndent=3, spaceAfter=4, textColor=INK, **base),
103:         "note": ParagraphStyle("note", fontSize=9, leading=15, textColor=INK2,
104:                                leftIndent=10, spaceAfter=8, **base),
105:         "cell": ParagraphStyle("cell", fontSize=9, leading=15, textColor=INK, **base),
106:         "cellh": ParagraphStyle("cellh", fontName=FONT_BOLD, fontSize=9, leading=15,
107:                                 textColor=ACCENT, alignment=TA_CENTER, wordWrap="CJK"),
108:         "caption": ParagraphStyle("caption", fontSize=8.5, leading=13, textColor=INK2,
109:                                   alignment=TA_CENTER, spaceBefore=3, spaceAfter=10, wordWrap="CJK"),
110:     }
111: 
112: 
113: def display_len(text: str) -> int:
114:     n = 0
115:     for ch in re.sub(r"[*`]", "", text):
116:         n += 2 if ord(ch) > 0x2E80 else 1
117:     return n
118: 
119: 
120: def build_table(rows: list[list[str]], st: dict[str, ParagraphStyle]) -> Table:
121:     header, body = rows[0], rows[1:]
122:     ncol = len(header)
123:     widths = [max([display_len(r[i]) for r in rows if i < len(r)] + [4]) for i in range(ncol)]
124:     weights = [w ** 0.72 for w in widths]
125:     total = sum(weights)
126:     col_w = [CONTENT_W * w / total for w in weights]
127: 
128:     data = [[Paragraph(inline(c), st["cellh"]) for c in header]]
129:     for row in body:
130:         row = row + [""] * (ncol - len(row))
131:         data.append([Paragraph(inline(c), st["cell"]) for c in row])
132: 
133:     table = Table(data, colWidths=col_w, repeatRows=1, hAlign="CENTER")
134:     table.setStyle(TableStyle([
135:         ("BACKGROUND", (0, 0), (-1, 0), HEAD_BG),
136:         ("GRID", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0.5, RULE),
137:         ("VALIGN", (0, 0), (-1, -1), "MIDDLE"),
138:         ("LEFTPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 5),
139:         ("RIGHTPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 5),
140:         ("TOPPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 4),
141:         ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 4),
142:         ("ROWBACKGROUNDS", (0, 1), (-1, -1), [colors.white, colors.HexColor("#F7F9FC")]),
143:     ]))
144:     return table
145: 
146: 
147: def parse(md: str, st: dict[str, ParagraphStyle]) -> tuple[str, list]:
148:     lines = md.splitlines()
149:     title = "未命名文档"
150:     flow: list = []
151:     buf: list[str] = []
152:     table_rows: list[list[str]] = []
153: 
154:     def flush_para() -> None:
155:         nonlocal buf
156:         if buf:
157:             flow.append(Paragraph(inline("".join(buf)), st["body"]))
158:             buf = []
159: 
160:     def flush_table() -> None:
161:         nonlocal table_rows
162:         if len(table_rows) >= 2:
163:             flow.append(build_table(table_rows, st))
164:             flow.append(Spacer(1, 6))
165:         table_rows = []
166: 
167:     for raw in lines:
168:         line = raw.rstrip()
169:         stripped = line.strip()
170:         if stripped.startswith("|"):
171:             flush_para()
172:             cells = [c.strip() for c in stripped.strip("|").split("|")]
173:             if all(re.fullmatch(r":?-{2,}:?", c) for c in cells):
174:                 continue
175:             table_rows.append(cells)
176:             continue
177:         flush_table()
178:         if not stripped:
179:             flush_para()
180:             continue
181:         if stripped.startswith("# "):
182:             title = stripped[2:].strip()
183:             flow.append(Paragraph(inline(title), st["title"]))
184:             flow.append(Spacer(1, 4))
185:         elif stripped.startswith("## "):
186:             flush_para()
187:             flow.append(Paragraph(inline(stripped[3:].strip()), st["h2"]))
188:         elif stripped.startswith("### "):
189:             flush_para()
190:             flow.append(Paragraph(inline(stripped[4:].strip()), st["h3"]))
191:         elif stripped.startswith("> "):
192:             flush_para()
193:             flow.append(Paragraph(inline(stripped[2:].strip()), st["note"]))
194:         elif stripped.startswith("- "):
195:             flush_para()
196:             flow.append(Paragraph(inline(stripped[2:].strip()), st["bullet"],
197:                                   bulletText="·"))
198:         else:
199:             buf.append(stripped)
200:     flush_table()
201:     flush_para()
202:     return title, flow
203: 
204: 
205: def render(md_path: Path) -> Path:
206:     st = styles()
207:     title, flow = parse(md_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), st)
208:     PDF_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
209:     out = PDF_DIR / f"{md_path.stem}.pdf"
210: 
211:     doc = BaseDocTemplate(
212:         str(out), pagesize=A4,
213:         leftMargin=2.2 * cm, rightMargin=2.2 * cm,
214:         topMargin=2.0 * cm, bottomMargin=2.0 * cm,
215:         title=title, author="星澜科技（虚构示例）",
216:         subject="Agent 项目知识库演示文档",
217:     )
218: 
219:     def on_page(canv, _doc):
220:         canv.saveState()
221:         canv.setFont(FONT_BODY, 8.5)
222:         canv.setFillColor(INK2)
223:         canv.drawString(2.2 * cm, A4[1] - 1.35 * cm, "星澜科技（虚构示例）· 内部制度文档")
224:         canv.drawRightString(A4[0] - 2.2 * cm, A4[1] - 1.35 * cm, md_path.stem)
225:         canv.setStrokeColor(RULE)
226:         canv.setLineWidth(0.5)
227:         canv.line(2.2 * cm, A4[1] - 1.55 * cm, A4[0] - 2.2 * cm, A4[1] - 1.55 * cm)
228:         canv.restoreState()
229: 
230:     frame = Frame(doc.leftMargin, doc.bottomMargin, CONTENT_W,
231:                   A4[1] - doc.topMargin - doc.bottomMargin, id="body")
232:     doc.addPageTemplates([PageTemplate(id="main", frames=[frame], onPage=on_page)])
233:     doc.build(flow, canvasmaker=NumberedCanvas)
234:     return out
235: 
236: 
237: def main() -> int:
238:     register_fonts()
239:     sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
240:     files = sorted(SRC_DIR.glob("*.md"))
241:     if not files:
242:         print("没有找到 docs/kb/src/*.md")
243:         return 1
244:     for md_path in files:
245:         out = render(md_path)
246:         print(f"{md_path.name:38} -> {out.relative_to(ROOT)}  ({out.stat().st_size} bytes)")
247:     return 0
248: 
249: 
250: if __name__ == "__main__":
251:     raise SystemExit(main())

1: """极简 Markdown -> Word：只支持本项目文档用到的语法。
2: 
3: 支持：# ## ### 标题、普通段落、- 列表、1. 有序列表、> 引用、--- 分隔线、
4: ``` 代码块、| 表格 |、![说明](图片路径)、行内 **加粗** 与 `代码`。
5: """
6: from __future__ import annotations
7: 
8: import re
9: import sys
10: from pathlib import Path
11: 
12: from docx import Document
13: from docx.enum.section import WD_ORIENT, WD_SECTION
14: from docx.enum.table import WD_TABLE_ALIGNMENT
15: from docx.enum.text import WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH
16: from docx.oxml import OxmlElement
17: from docx.oxml.ns import qn
18: from docx.shared import Cm, Pt, RGBColor
19: from PIL import Image as PILImage
20: 
21: CN_FONT = "微软雅黑"
22: MONO_FONT = "Consolas"
23: BODY_SIZE = Pt(10.5)
24: 
25: INLINE = re.compile(r"(\*\*.+?\*\*|`[^`]+`)")
26: 
27: 
28: def _shade(cell_or_par, fill: str) -> None:
29:     el = cell_or_par._tc if hasattr(cell_or_par, "_tc") else cell_or_par._p
30:     pr = el.get_or_add_tcPr() if hasattr(cell_or_par, "_tc") else el.get_or_add_pPr()
31:     shd = OxmlElement("w:shd")
32:     shd.set(qn("w:val"), "clear")
33:     shd.set(qn("w:color"), "auto")
34:     shd.set(qn("w:fill"), fill)
35:     pr.append(shd)
36: 
37: 
38: def _font(run, name: str, size, bold: bool | None = None, color: str | None = None) -> None:
39:     run.font.name = name
40:     run.font.size = size
41:     if bold is not None:
42:         run.bold = bold
43:     if color:
44:         run.font.color.rgb = RGBColor.from_string(color)
45:     rpr = run._element.get_or_add_rPr()
46:     fonts = rpr.find(qn("w:rFonts"))
47:     if fonts is None:
48:         fonts = OxmlElement("w:rFonts")
49:         rpr.append(fonts)
50:     fonts.set(qn("w:eastAsia"), name)
51:     fonts.set(qn("w:hAnsi"), name)
52: 
53: 
54: def add_inline(par, text: str, size=BODY_SIZE, bold=False, color=None) -> None:
55:     for piece in INLINE.split(text):
56:         if not piece:
57:             continue
58:         if piece.startswith("**") and piece.endswith("**"):
59:             run = par.add_run(piece[2:-2])
60:             _font(run, CN_FONT, size, bold=True, color=color)
61:         elif piece.startswith("`") and piece.endswith("`"):
62:             run = par.add_run(piece[1:-1])
63:             _font(run, MONO_FONT, Pt(size.pt - 1.0), bold=bold, color="B03060")
64:         else:
65:             run = par.add_run(piece)
66:             _font(run, CN_FONT, size, bold=bold, color=color)
67: 
68: 
69: def heading(doc, text: str, level: int) -> None:
70:     par = doc.add_paragraph()
71:     sizes = {1: Pt(20), 2: Pt(15), 3: Pt(12.5)}
72:     if level == 1:
73:         par.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(0)
74:         par.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(10)
75:     else:
76:         par.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(12 if level == 2 else 9)
77:         par.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(6)
78:     par.paragraph_format.keep_with_next = True
79:     add_inline(par, text, size=sizes.get(level, Pt(12)), bold=True,
80:                color="1F3864" if level <= 2 else "2E5496")
81: 
82: 
83: def body(doc, text: str, indent: float = 0.0, size=BODY_SIZE) -> None:
84:     par = doc.add_paragraph()
85:     par.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(4)
86:     par.paragraph_format.line_spacing = 1.25
87:     if indent:
88:         par.paragraph_format.left_indent = Cm(indent)
89:     add_inline(par, text, size=size)
90: 
91: 
92: def bullet(doc, text: str, ordered: bool = False, indent: float = 0.4) -> None:
93:     par = doc.add_paragraph()
94:     par.paragraph_format.left_indent = Cm(indent + 0.4)
95:     par.paragraph_format.first_line_indent = Cm(-0.4)
96:     par.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(2)
97:     par.paragraph_format.line_spacing = 1.2
98:     add_inline(par, text)
99: 
100: 
101: def code_block(doc, lines: list[str]) -> None:
102:     for i, line in enumerate(lines):
103:         par = doc.add_paragraph()
104:         pf = par.paragraph_format
105:         pf.space_before = Pt(3) if i == 0 else Pt(0)
106:         pf.space_after = Pt(3) if i == len(lines) - 1 else Pt(0)
107:         pf.line_spacing = 1.05
108:         pf.left_indent = Cm(0.3)
109:         if i < len(lines) - 1:
110:             pf.keep_with_next = True
111:         _shade(par, "F4F5F7")
112:         run = par.add_run(line if line else " ")
113:         _font(run, MONO_FONT, Pt(8.5))
114: 
115: 
116: def quote(doc, text: str) -> None:
117:     par = doc.add_paragraph()
118:     par.paragraph_format.left_indent = Cm(0.5)
119:     par.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
120:     add_inline(par, text, size=Pt(10), color="4B5563")
121: 
122: 
123: def _display_len(text: str) -> int:
124:     plain = re.sub(r"\*\*|`", "", text)
125:     return sum(2 if ord(ch) > 127 else 1 for ch in plain)
126: 
127: 
128: def table(doc, rows: list[list[str]]) -> None:
129:     if not rows:
130:         return
131:     ncol = max(len(r) for r in rows)
132:     table = doc.add_table(rows=len(rows), cols=ncol)
133:     table.style = "Table Grid"
134:     table.alignment = WD_TABLE_ALIGNMENT.CENTER
135:     table.autofit = False
136: 
137:     total_cm = 15.8
138:     weights = []
139:     for c in range(ncol):
140:         longest = max((_display_len(r[c]) if c < len(r) else 0) for r in rows)
141:         weights.append(max(longest, 6) ** 0.72)
142:     widths = [max(1.6, total_cm * w / sum(weights)) for w in weights]
143:     scale = total_cm / sum(widths)
144:     widths = [w * scale for w in widths]
145: 
146:     for r, row in enumerate(rows):
147:         tr = table.rows[r]._tr
148:         trpr = tr.get_or_add_trPr()
149:         cant = OxmlElement("w:cantSplit")
150:         cant.set(qn("w:val"), "true")
151:         trpr.append(cant)
152:         if r == 0:
153:             hdr = OxmlElement("w:tblHeader")
154:             hdr.set(qn("w:val"), "true")
155:             trpr.append(hdr)
156:         for c in range(ncol):
157:             cell = table.cell(r, c)
158:             cell.width = Cm(widths[c])
159:             text = row[c] if c < len(row) else ""
160:             cell.text = ""
161:             par = cell.paragraphs[0]
162:             par.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(1)
163:             par.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(1)
164:             add_inline(par, text, size=Pt(9), bold=(r == 0))
165:             if r == 0:
166:                 _shade(cell, "E8EEF7")
167:     doc.add_paragraph().paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(2)
168: 
169: 
170: def image(doc, path: Path, caption: str, landscape: bool = False) -> None:
171:     if not path.exists():
172:         body(doc, f"[缺图] {path}")
173:         return
174:     max_w = 24.4 if landscape else 15.6
175:     max_h = 14.0 if landscape else 21.5
176:     with PILImage.open(path) as im:
177:         w_px, h_px = im.size
178:     width = min(max_w, max_h * w_px / h_px)
179:     par = doc.add_paragraph()
180:     par.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
181:     par.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(6)
182:     par.paragraph_format.keep_with_next = True
183:     par.add_run().add_picture(str(path), width=Cm(width))
184:     cap = doc.add_paragraph()
185:     cap.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
186:     cap.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(8)
187:     add_inline(cap, caption, size=Pt(9), color="6B7280")
188: 
189: 
190: IMG = re.compile(r"^!\[(.*?)\]\((.*?)\)$")
191: HR = re.compile(r"^-{3,}$")
192: 
193: 
194: def convert(md_path: Path, docx_path: Path) -> None:
195:     doc = Document()
196:     style = doc.styles["Normal"]
197:     style.font.name = CN_FONT
198:     style.font.size = BODY_SIZE
199:     style.element.rPr.rFonts.set(qn("w:eastAsia"), CN_FONT)
200:     for section in doc.sections:
201:         section.top_margin = Cm(2.2)
202:         section.bottom_margin = Cm(2.2)
203:         section.left_margin = Cm(2.4)
204:         section.right_margin = Cm(2.4)
205: 
206:     def new_section(landscape: bool) -> None:
207:         sec = doc.add_section(WD_SECTION.NEW_PAGE)
208:         if landscape:
209:             sec.orientation = WD_ORIENT.LANDSCAPE
210:             sec.page_width, sec.page_height = Cm(29.7), Cm(21.0)
211:         else:
212:             sec.orientation = WD_ORIENT.PORTRAIT
213:             sec.page_width, sec.page_height = Cm(21.0), Cm(29.7)
214:         sec.top_margin = Cm(1.8) if landscape else Cm(2.2)
215:         sec.bottom_margin = Cm(1.8) if landscape else Cm(2.2)
216:         sec.left_margin = Cm(2.0) if landscape else Cm(2.4)
217:         sec.right_margin = Cm(2.0) if landscape else Cm(2.4)
218: 
219:     lines = md_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
220:     landscape = False
221:     i = 0
222:     while i < len(lines):
223:         raw = lines[i]
224:         line = raw.strip()
225: 
226:         if line.startswith("<!--landscape"):
227:             if not landscape:
228:                 new_section(True)
229:                 landscape = True
230:             i += 1
231:             continue
232:         if line.startswith("<!--portrait"):
233:             if landscape:
234:                 new_section(False)
235:                 landscape = False
236:             i += 1
237:             continue
238: 
239:         if line.startswith("```"):
240:             i += 1
241:             buf: list[str] = []
242:             while i < len(lines) and not lines[i].strip().startswith("```"):
243:                 buf.append(lines[i].rstrip())
244:                 i += 1
245:             i += 1
246:             code_block(doc, buf)
247:             continue
248: 
249:         m = IMG.match(line)
250:         if m:
251:             caption, src = m.group(1), m.group(2)
252:             image(doc, (md_path.parent / src).resolve(), caption, landscape)
253:             i += 1
254:             continue
255: 
256:         if not line:
257:             i += 1
258:             continue
259: 
260:         if line.startswith("|"):
261:             buf = []
262:             while i < len(lines) and lines[i].strip().startswith("|"):
263:                 row = [c.strip() for c in lines[i].strip().strip("|").split("|")]
264:                 if not all(re.fullmatch(r":?-{2,}:?", c) for c in row):

266:                 i += 1
267:             table(doc, buf)
268:             continue
269: 
270:         if line.startswith("### "):
271:             heading(doc, line[4:], 3)
272:         elif line.startswith("## "):

274:         elif line.startswith("# "):

276:         elif line.startswith(">"):
277:             quote(doc, line[1:].strip())
278:         elif HR.match(line):
279:             par = doc.add_paragraph()
280:             par.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(6)

286:             bottom.set(qn("w:color"), "C9CDD4")

288:             ppr.append(pbdr)

291:         elif re.match(r"^\d+\.\s", line):
292:             bullet(doc, re.sub(r"^(\d+)\.\s", r"\1. ", line))
293:         else:
294:             body(doc, line)
295:         i += 1
296: 
297:     doc.save(str(docx_path))
298:     print(f"saved: {docx_path}")

1: """环境变量体检：只报告「已设置 / 未设置」，绝不打印任何值。
2: 
3: 用法：

31: 
32:

### 一、先给你吃一颗定心丸：学了半个月，很多代码读不懂正常吗？

**完全正常，甚至可以说是 100% 必然的。**

你只学了半个月 Agent，掌握的是**核心主干概念**（什么是 State、Node、Conditional Edge、Interrupt、RAG 切分与向量检索、MCP 工具调用）。而现在这个项目里，让你觉得「读不懂、眼花缭乱」的代码，其实**80% 都不是 Agent 逻辑，而是为了应付边缘情况的「工程胶水代码」和「造数据脚本」**。

我帮你把项目里最难懂的部分拆开看，你就会发现它们根本不用死磕：
1. **造假数据/转格式的排版脚本**（如 [gen_kb_pdfs.py](<scripts/gen_kb_pdfs.py>) 251 行、[md2docx_min.py](<scripts/md2docx_min.py>) 304 行）：里面全是 `reportlab` 画 PDF 页眉页脚、`python-docx` 操作 Word XML 节点 `w:shd` / `w:rFonts`。**这跟 AI Agent 半毛钱关系都没有**，面试官绝对不会问你 ReportLab 怎么算表格列宽！
2. **异步转同步与跨进程异常捕获**（如 [mcp_client.py](<backend/app/agents/tools/mcp_client.py#L110-L204>)）：里面的 `ThreadPoolExecutor`、`ExceptionGroup` 递归遍历 `winerror == 5`、兼容三种返回格式。这是 Python 底层异步和 Windows 沙箱兼容写法，初学半个月看不懂太正常了。
3. **复杂的正则清洗文本**（如 [splitter.py](<backend/app/agents/rag/splitter.py#L34-L124>) 和 [plan_action.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/plan_action.py#L28-L57>)）：一堆 `re.compile` 正则表达式，用来把 HTML `<table>` 变成 Markdown 表格、或者从用户话里抠掉「顺便通知一下提单人」。正则本来就是「写的时候费劲、读的时候天书」，你只需要知道**这个函数输入什么、输出什么**就够了。

---

### 二、哪些是冗杂代码？可以直接删减或精简吗？（保留全部核心技术栈与亮点）

我在通读全部代码后，挑出了**4 类明显冗杂、重复或留着反而容易给自己挖坑**的地方：

#### 1. 空壳目录（强烈建议直接删掉）
在 `backend/` 下有 **7 个完全空的目录**（里面只有一个 `.gitkeep`），明显是一开始按传统 Web 项目建了模板，后来根本没用上：
* `backend/app/core/`、`backend/app/db/`、`backend/app/models/`、`backend/app/schemas/`、`backend/app/rag/`（真正的 rag 在 `backend/app/agents/rag/`）、`backend/app/services/`、`backend/migrations/`。
* **为什么该删**：面试官如果看目录结构，发现 `app/rag` 和 `app/agents/rag` 重名，或者看到 `migrations` 问你「你们 Alembic 数据库迁移是怎么做的？」，你一慌就露怯了。删掉空壳后，目录干净利落。

#### 2. 与 Agent 无关的排版/造数据脚本（建议删除或移出主视野）
* [scripts/md2docx_min.py](<scripts/md2docx_min.py>)（304 行）：把 Markdown 转成 Word 文档的工具脚本，里面甚至还硬编码了不存在的 `D:\zuixin\docs\architecture\AGENT-EXPLAIN.md`（[md2docx_min.py:302](<scripts/md2docx_min.py#L302>)）。**完全多余，直接删掉**，省得自己看着心烦。
* [scripts/gen_kb_pdfs.py](<scripts/gen_kb_pdfs.py>)（251 行）：用来把 `docs/kb/src/*.md` 生成 `docs/kb/pdf/*.pdf` 的脚本。因为你的 PDF 和解析好的 Markdown 都已经在 `docs/kb/` 目录下了，平时根本不用跑它。如果保留，你只需要知道「这是用来把虚构的 Markdown 制度渲染成测试 PDF 供 MinerU 解析的辅助脚本」，完全不用去读里面的 `reportlab` 代码。

#### 3. 业务代码里的重复函数与死代码（精简后更好懂）
* **重复的摘要函数**：[plan_action.py:179–186](<backend/app/agents/nodes/plan_action.py#L179-L186>) 里的 `_compose_summary` 函数，和 [action_spec.py:73–82](<backend/app/agents/nodes/action_spec.py#L73-L82>) 里的 `summary_of` 代码一模一样，而且 `_compose_summary` 在 `plan_action.py` 里根本没被调用过（死代码），可以直接删掉。
* **`run_graph.py` 与 `trace_run.py` 功能重叠**：`scripts/run_graph.py` 是 CLI 工具，`scripts/trace_run.py` 也是跑一条请求看节点输出。其实平时调试看前端右侧面板或者用 `run_graph.py` 就够了。

---

### 三、代码里真正需要修改/修复的 4 个地方（含真实 Bug）

为了让你去面试时底气十足、代码经得起推敲，有 **4 处地方建议修改**（前两处是真 Bug，后两处是防露馅）：

1. **【真 Bug 1】发布知识文章成功后，回复里的「审批人」永远取不到名字**
   * 在 [human_gate.py:279–285](<backend/app/agents/nodes/human_gate.py#L279-L285>) 中，审批通过后写进 State 的字典 key 叫 `"approver"`：
     `approval = {"approver": decider.user_id, ...}`
   * 但是在 [respond.py:84–85](<backend/app/agents/nodes/respond.py#L84-L85>) 拼回复话术时，写的却是：
     `who = approval.get("user_id") or "知识库 owner"`
   * **后果**：因为 key 写成了 `user_id` 而不是 `approver`，所以无论谁批的，最后回复永远显示默认值「知识库 owner」。改成 `approval.get("approver")` 即可修复。

2. **【真 Bug 2】前端确认卡没有锁定「提单人 (`reporter`)」输入框**
   * 后端在 [action_spec.py:65–96](<backend/app/agents/nodes/action_spec.py#L65-L96>) 里专门设计了 `EDITABLE_BLOCK = {"reporter", "tenant_id"}`，给前端传了 `editable: false`，防止用户在确认卡片上改提单人。
   * 但前端 [InterruptCard.vue:134](<frontend/src/components/InterruptCard.vue#L134>) 写 `<el-input v-model="editable[row.name]" size="small" />` 时漏写了 `:disabled="!row.editable"`，导致网页上还是能改提单人。加上这个属性就严丝合缝了。

3. **【注释矛盾】`classify.py` 头部注释和实际代码相反**
   * 在 [classify.py:7](<backend/app/agents/nodes/classify.py#L7>) 写着：`不做条件边，决策写在节点里，用 Command(goto=...) 指定下一跳`。
   * 但实际上你的 [graph.py:105–111](<backend/app/agents/graph.py#L105-L111>) **全部用的是条件边 `add_conditional_edges`**，根本没用 `Command(goto=...)`！要是面试官顺着注释问你「你为什么用 Command(goto) 不用条件边？」，直接就被注释坑了。

4. **【培训班痕迹】十几个文件头部的「对应黑马讲义：《第X节…》」**
   * 把这些注释改成干净的业务/架构说明，既不暴露你是照着半个月网课改的，你自己读起来也更像自己的笔记。

---

### 四、半个月速成：你只需要真正读懂哪 6 个文件？（抓大放小策略）

全项目 36 个 Python 文件，你**不需要、也不应该**每个都背。面试官考你项目，95% 的问题都集中在这 **6 个核心文件（共约 800 行代码）**。把这 6 个文件看懂，这个项目就是你的：

1. **[state.py](<backend/app/agents/state.py>)（117 行）**：看懂 `UserContext`（只读身份：谁、哪个租户、什么角色）和 `TicketState`（随流程走的表单）是怎么分开的。
2. **[graph.py](<backend/app/agents/graph.py>)（127 行）**：看懂 9 个节点是怎么连线的，5 个 `route_after_*` 函数各自看哪个字段决定往哪走。
3. **[action_spec.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/action_spec.py>)（98 行）**：看懂 `RISK_BY_OP`（静态风险表）、`compute_risk`（为什么关单+通知提单人会从可撤销升级成不可逆）、`GATE_BY_RISK`（风险对应哪个门）。
4. **[human_gate.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/human_gate.py>)（296 行）**：看懂 `confirm_action`（本人确认，改了参数后重算风险）和 `approve_action`（主管审批，通过 `interrupt(payload)` 暂停，恢复时检查 `runtime.context.roles`）。
5. **[execute.py](<backend/app/agents/nodes/execute.py>)（120 行）**：看懂全图只有这里真正调写工具，且执行前还会检查一次 `if state.get("risk") == "write_irreversible" and not approved`。
6. **[vector_store.py](<backend/app/agents/rag/vector_store.py>) 的 `hybrid_search` 函数（第 245–281 行，仅 36 行）**：看懂怎么发两个 `AnnSearchRequest`（一个稠密 `COSINE`，一个稀疏 `BM25`），然后交给 `RRFRanker(k=60)` 合并。

至于其他文件（前端 Vue、SSE 转换、PDF 清洗正则、SQLite 建表语句），你只需要知道**「它们在哪、干什么用」**（对照 [讲解卡.md](<docs/讲解卡.md>) 的表格即可），完全不需要去抠里面的语法细节。

---
